# Borg Console — Frontend Redesign / Overhaul Master Plan

_Generated by a multi-agent planning workflow grounded in the actual borg repo (verified routes, live frames, client methods, existing screens, and design system). Part I is the architect synthesis; Parts II–III are the per-area build-ready specs; appendices carry the verified catalog and adversarial review._

---

# Part I — Architecture, IA, Roadmap, Risks

I have everything I need from the extensive ground-truth context. Let me synthesize Part I of the master plan.

# Borg Operator Console — Master Plan, Part I (Front-Matter)

## 1. Executive Summary

### Core thesis

Today's console is **nine sibling screens that don't talk to each other**. Each one re-implements its own detail pane, its own correction modal, its own "why?" drawer, its own provenance rendering, and its own way of showing an id. The same commitment appears in Commit, Directives, Review, and Stream as four unrelated strings. The operator carries the cross-referencing in their head.

This redesign collapses that into **one mental model on three pillars**:

1. **Cognitive Orrery** — the hero visualization. Generalize the existing single-turn `FlowChart`/`Xray` cluster (15-phase pipeline, already props-driven and pure) into a layout-abstracted `<Orrery>` that renders the live turn *and* the surrounding substrate (8 memory-band rings, dream satellites, governance arcs, review fault-nodes) in four modes (Live / Replay / System-health / Provenance-flow). It is the same component at three sizes: full hero (Mission Control), compact (Workbench center, drop-in for `Xray`), and a `<MiniOrrery>` glyph in headers. Crucially, it consumes the **already-built-but-unwired** `flowSnapshotByTurn` (64-turn LRU) and `ledgerByTurn` caches — historical replay is a wiring job, not an engine change.

2. **Universal Inspector** — one right-side drawer that *any* `{type,id}` opens into, with six tabs (Summary / Evidence / Relationships / Timeline / Actions / Raw JSON), tab visibility driven by a client-side **object-model registry** (`inspector-registry.ts`). Every screen stops owning a bespoke detail pane and instead calls `openObject(ref)`. All correction/why/forget/invalidate reuse the **existing verified endpoints** — no new backend is required to ship the Inspector.

3. **Cross-linked provenance** — every id anywhere (ledger citation, `source_stream_entry_ids`, `superseded_by_id`, `from/to_node_id`) becomes a clickable `<IdRef>` chip (generalizing today's `SupersededByChip`) that resolves type by the repo's canonical id-prefix taxonomy and pivots into the Inspector. Provenance stops being plain `[id]` text on five screens and becomes one navigable graph.

### The one mental model

> **Everything is an object. Every object opens in the same drawer. Every id is a link to that drawer. The Orrery shows objects in motion; the Inspector shows one object in depth; the Command Palette (⌘K) jumps to any object or screen by keyboard.**

The operator learns *one* interaction (click an id → Inspector → pivot to related objects → optionally jump to its source screen) and it works for episodes, semantic nodes, commitments, directives, reviews, dream audit rows, attachments, sessions, and identity sub-objects alike.

### What fundamentally changes vs today

| Today | After |
|---|---|
| 9 flat conditional-render routes, screen not in URL | 10 routes via thin `AppShell` + `?view=` URL param (deep-linkable) |
| Each screen hand-rolls a detail pane + correction modal + `WhyDrawer` | One Universal Inspector + `objectRegistry`; screens emit `openObject` |
| `flowSnapshotByTurn`/`ledgerByTurn`/`eventTail` computed but rendered nowhere | Powered by the Orrery (Replay) and the Workbench turn-history strip |
| Provenance ids are non-clickable `[id]` text | Every id is an `<IdRef>` pivot |
| Correction propose-in-Commit / resolve-in-Review split | Review & Repair owns the full loop |
| `Topbar` + `SessionsSidebar` | `InstrumentStrip` + collapsible `SessionFleet` fed by one shell `useLiveCache` |
| Commit + Directives separate | Merged into Governance Studio |
| No keyboard entry point | ⌘K Command Palette over screens, sessions, search, open-by-id |

This is overwhelmingly a **presentation, labeling, and cross-linking** overhaul. It adds **zero recall-gating** and **zero output-policing** (see §8). The backend is touched only by a small, prioritized gap list (§7); the plan ships meaningfully with **none** of those gaps closed, degrading gracefully where they're absent.

## 3. Design-System Summary

Verdict from the audit: **extend, don't reinvent.** The proposal's "section 9" palette (graphite / phosphor-green / amber / red / blue / purple, monospace, motion-as-state) is a near-exact description of the *already-shipped* `:root` token set in `styles.css` (4076 lines, all color in `oklch()`). See the design-system spec for the full token contract.

**At a glance:**

- **Color (adopt verbatim):** warm-graphite surfaces `--bg-0..3` + `--line/-soft/-ghost` (hue 80, chroma ~0.006). Six-step text ramp `--text → --text-ghost`. Signals each with a `-dim` companion: `--acc` (phosphor green = LIVE/ACTIVE only — **rationed**), `--warn` (amber = live-write/danger/dream-running), `--bad` (red = failure/suppression), `--info` (blue = user-vs-borg/causal, sparingly), `--purple` (interior/thought).
- **New tokens (close the documented gaps):** `--purple-dim`; per-signal **tint tokens** (`--acc-tint`/`-strong`, `--warn-tint`, …) to kill ~15 repeated `oklch(<sig> / .06–.18)` literals; **motion** tokens `--dur-fast(80ms)/--dur(120ms)/--dur-slow(240ms)` + `--ease`; a **spacing** scale `--sp-1..6`; a `--fs-3xl` hero step; a **4-rank severity scale** mapped onto existing signals.
- **Type:** mono-default (`--mono`) / `--sans` **only for human prose** — load-bearing split, preserve it. Reconcile stray px literals onto the `--fs-*` ladder; codify the uppercase micro-eyebrow as `.eyebrow`.
- **Motion:** state-indicating only (WS pulse, active phase, token stream, new-entry insertion, dream-running, orrery/severity pulse, graph settle) reusing `@keyframes pulse`/`fc-pulse-*`. No animated backgrounds, no constant blink. Tokenize the de-facto 80/120/240ms ladder.
- **Layout:** fixed full-viewport IDE shell (CSS grid `rail / topbar / main / statusbar`), sharp corners (`--radius` 2px), borders-over-shadows, panes scroll internally.
- **New components** under `components/`: `SeverityChip` (thin `Tag` variant `kind="sev-1..4"` — **ranks, never gates**), `CountBadge`, `DisclosureLabel` (render-time origin/private/operator/trust/taint label — see §7/§8), `MiniOrrery`, plus generalized `Spark` (add `tone`/`mode=bars|line`) and new Timeline/Matrix SVG primitives reusing the semantic edge/dash grammar.

**One must-fix data caveat for `DisclosureLabel`** (see §7): `disclosure`/`origin_audience` are *not* typed top-level fields on `EvidenceLedgerEntry` today — they arrive inside the untyped `state_metadata.disclosure_label` blob, and the real `MemoryDisclosureClass` has **six** values, not the spec's four. The component needs a defined 6→4 collapse that preserves fail-closed-to-`unknown` and must surface the label out of `state_metadata` into a typed field. This is a type-surfacing task, not "field already present."

---

## 4. Phased Roadmap (P0 → P5)

**P0 — Foundation (everything depends on this; no screen migration starts until P0 merges).**
This is the seam the whole-plan completeness review flagged as the single biggest risk: the Inspector is a hard prerequisite for 9 of 12 surfaces but was only "designed," never sequenced. P0 fixes that.

- **P0.1 AppShell + routing.** Replace `App.tsx` conditional render with `AppShell` + `?view=` URL param (extend the proven `useSession` `history.replaceState`/`popstate` pattern; **no react-router**). Promote `RouteId` to 10 destinations. Refactor `Rail` for count badges. New `InstrumentStrip` + collapsible `SessionFleet`.
- **P0.2 `useLiveCache`.** ONE shell-level subscription to global frames (`maintenance:tick`, `dream:process:*`, `borg:reset`, session-activity index off `stream:append`) feeding all screens via context; per-turn flow detail stays in `useTurnStream`. **Decide the per-session-pulse transport question here** (see §5 risk): emit `subscribe_global` (cheapest; server already handles it) **or** derive fleet recency from `last_activity_at` refetch deltas. Do not let three screens encode the false "per-row pulse just works" assumption.
- **P0.3 Object model + Universal Inspector.** `inspector-registry.ts` (type → resolver fn + capabilities + sourceRoute), `InspectorProvider` (target stack for nested pivots + `setRoute` escape hatch), `<Inspector>` (6 tabs), `<IdRef>` chip, id-prefix sniffer keyed on `src/util/ids.ts` taxonomy. **Pick ONE contract now** — the four areas currently spec it four ways (`openInspector`/`onInspect({kind,id})`/`openObject({type,id})`); the completeness review demands a single decision before build. Recommended: a context-provider `openObject({type,id})` with `{type,id}` naming, prop-callback-free.
- **P0.4 Design-system tokens.** Add the new tokens (§3), tokenize tints, add `--purple-dim`/motion/spacing scales, build `SeverityChip`/`CountBadge`/`DisclosureLabel`/`MiniOrrery`.
- **P0.5 Shared infra the review demands.** Shared `Loading`/`ErrorState` + top-level `AppErrorBoundary` (consumed everywhere). **Extract-to-shared migration ticket** (owned once): `SemanticEdgeDetail` (local in `Review/index.tsx:515` + inline in Memory — does **not** exist as a file), `OpenQuestionEventsSection` (local in `Identity/index.tsx:256`), `WhyDrawer.JsonValueView` (local). These are claimed "reuse verbatim" by 3–4 areas but are actually extraction work.

**P1 — Hero + entry points (depends on P0; can parallelize internally).**
- **Cognitive Orrery** core component (layout abstraction over `FlowChart` renderers + the unwired caches). Parallel track A.
- **Command Palette** (⌘K). Parallel track B — depends only on P0.1 routing + P0.3 registry.
- **Mission Control** screen — depends on the Orrery (P1 track A) + SessionFleet (P0.1) + Inspector (P0.3). Lands after the Orrery core.

**P2 — High-traffic screens (depend on P0; parallelize freely — each is an independent screen).**
- **Conversation Workbench** (consumes Orrery-compact + replay caches).
- **Memory Atlas** (per-band views; keep `SemanticTopology`).
- **Stream & Provenance** (turn-grouped + provenance inspector).
These three share only P0 infra and the Inspector, so they run in parallel on separate engineers.

**P3 — Governance & repair cluster (depend on P0; the merge work).**
- **Governance Studio** (Commit + Directives merge — the heaviest consolidation: shared filter/lineage components, `loadDirectiveSupportData` fan-out, scope matrix).
- **Review & Repair** (absorbs correction initiation, healing the propose/resolve split).
These two share the most cross-references (reconciliation reviews span both record types), so co-sequence them on one track even though they're separate screens.

**P4 — Identity & ops (depend on P0; parallelizable).**
- **Identity Studio** (5-region; `OpenQuestionEventsSection` already extracted in P0.5).
- **Dream Ops** (in-place evolution; plan/apply process-subset + budget UI).
- **Prompt Lab** (3-pane diff editor).

**P5 — Admin + polish.**
- **Admin / Runtime** (reset relocation, runtime introspection, client error log, `turnStream.resetCaches()`).
- Cross-link matrix audit (reciprocal deep-links), a11y/keyboard pass across Orrery/Inspector/palette, large-dataset windowing affordances, test backfill for the registry + prefix router.

**Parallelization summary:** P0 is strictly serial-first (one team, fast). After P0, **P2's three screens, P1's two tracks, and P4's three screens are all mutually independent** — gated only on P0 + Inspector. P3's two screens should co-sequence due to shared reconciliation logic. The critical path is **P0 → Orrery → Mission Control**; everything else hangs off P0 in parallel.

**Reuse grounding:** the highest-leverage reuse is real and verified — `use-turn-stream`'s unwired `flowSnapshotByTurn`/`ledgerByTurn`/`eventTail` (the Orrery's substrate), the pure props-driven `FlowChart` geometry/renderers, `SemanticTopology` (a self-contained force-graph reusable for social/relational), the `useApi`/`LiveEventsProvider` patterns, and the entire `Tag`/`Panel`/`Modal`/`Spark`/`SupersededByChip` vocabulary + oklch tokens. The bespoke per-screen detail/correction wiring is the main *deletion* target.

## 5. Risk Register

| # | Risk | Severity | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | **Custom-routing migration breaks deep-linking / session sync.** Promoting `RouteId` to the URL alongside the existing `?session=` param could collide or regress `popstate` handling. | Med | Extend the *exact* `useSession` idiom (`history.replaceState`, delete-param-on-default, `popstate` re-read, strict pattern validation) for `?view=`. Do **not** add react-router (new dep, new mock surface, fights prop-drilled+context architecture). Keep `?session=` and `?view=` orthogonal params. |
| R2 | **Universal Inspector is an unbuilt, unsequenced dependency for 9 screens.** Three areas (Dream/Workbench/Prompt Lab) already failed verdict for binding to it as if it exists. | **High** | **Make the Inspector + registry + provider P0**, merged before any screen migration. Pick ONE `openObject` contract (R-contract below). Every screen pivot section is non-functional until P0.3 lands. |
| R2b | **Inspector contract specified four incompatible ways** (prop-callback vs context-provider vs URL-param; `{kind,id}` vs `{type,id}`). | High | Decide in P0.3: single context-provider `openObject({type,id})`. Codify in the plan before fan-out; divergent contracts guarantee rework. |
| R3 | **Live-cache correctness: per-session pulse transport gap.** `shouldDeliver` gates `stream:append` to `subscribedSessions`; client subscribes to one session and never sends `subscribe_global`. SessionFleet/Mission-Control "live recency per row" silently works for the active session only. Recurs in 3 areas, flagged in 1. | **High** | Fix once in P0.2: client emits `subscribe_global` (server already handles it, `subscribedGlobal` defaults true) **or** derive fleet recency from `last_activity_at` refetch deltas. Block the SessionFleet design until decided. |
| R3b | **Reconnect/post-reset cache invalidation under-specified** for shell `useLiveCache`, 64-LRU snapshots, Inspector target stack, palette index. Stale Inspector target after reset shows deleted objects as live. | Med | Define survive-reconnect vs flush-on-reset matrix in P0.2/P0.3. `borg:reset` keeps `window.location.reload()`; reconnect resync via `getStream` replace (existing pattern). Wire the proposed `turnStream.resetCaches()`. |
| R4 | **Large-dataset perf: silent lower-bound counts.** Mission Control's 7-card AttentionQueue, Governance Scope Matrix, and Stream filters do unbounded full-list client scans over a `limit=50`/cursor window — counts/matrices mislead past 50 rows. | Med | State a windowing contract: surface "showing first N" / reuse `count_is_lower_bound` affordance on every client-aggregated count. Defer true accuracy to the optional counts-by-kind/status backend gaps (§7) — non-blocking at demo scale (76 active episodes) but must not present a window as a total. |
| R5 | **Universal-inspector object-resolution without a unified lookup endpoint.** Raw id strings (cross-links, palette) can't resolve to a typed object; numeric ids (reviews/audit) stay ambiguous. | Med | Resolution is *mostly explicit* (caller knows the type at click site). For raw ids, the id-prefix sniffer is the only discovery path; numeric/ambiguous ids degrade to "Raw JSON + why? only." Quarantine `GET /api/object/:id` + related-object endpoint as named gaps (§7); ship without them. |
| R6 | **Scope creep / over-engineering.** A 10-screen overhaul + hero viz + drawer + palette invites gold-plating (e.g. SVG kanban/scatter/matrix everywhere, animated everything). | Med | The Orrery is *one* component at three sizes, not bespoke per screen. Motion is state-indicating only. Per-band Atlas views are matched to data shape, not invented. Hold the Opus-5.0 line: any added machinery that legislates judgment or gates recall is out. Defer all "v2" features (budget metering, revision history) to the gap list. |
| R7 | **Fabricated reuse: 3 "verbatim" components are local non-exported fns.** `SemanticEdgeDetail`, `OpenQuestionEventsSection`, `JsonValueView` — ownership ambiguity (who extracts?). | Low/Med | Single P0.5 extract-to-shared ticket, owned once, before the 3–4 screens that double-claim them migrate. |
| R8 | **A11y/keyboard regression.** New click-only Orrery + 6-tab drawer + SVG bodies; existing a11y is shallow. | Low/Med | P5 keyboard pass: drawer focus-trap/return + Escape, SVG-node keyboard reachability, palette is the keyboard pivot for everything the Orrery exposes by click. Preserve existing `aria-label`/`role`/`data-testid`/class-name test contracts. |

## 6. Testing Strategy

Align strictly to the existing conventions (Vitest 4 + RTL 16, `jsdom`, `globals: true` but imports still explicit; colocated `<Thing>.test.tsx`; glob `src/**/*.test.ts(x)`; `tsc --noEmit` under strict + `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`).

- **API mocking — keep the relative-URL `fetch` stub pattern.** No module-level client mock. `vi.stubGlobal("fetch", fetchMock)` routing by `requestPath()` (parse against `http://test.invalid`), `jsonResponse`/`jsonErrorResponse` helpers, method-aware branches asserting on `JSON.parse(init.body)`. The client must stay relative-base (`DEFAULT_API_BASE=""`) or these break — **do not** hardcode absolute origins in new code. The helpers are copy-pasted per file today; if the redesign introduces a shared `test-utils`, do it deliberately and apply consistently (the new registry/Inspector tests are a good first consumer).
- **Live events — keep the context-injection pattern.** Never mock `WebSocket`. Use `makeLiveSource()` (`{ live(connectionCount?, wsState?), emit(frame) }`) rendered under `<LiveEventsProvider value={live.live()}>`; push frames via `act(() => live.emit(...))`; simulate reconnect by bumping `connectionCount` across `rerender`. **The new `useLiveCache` and Inspector must consume `LiveEventsProvider`/context — not instantiate their own socket** — so this injection keeps working.
- **Queries — preserve the accessible-query + class-hook contract.** Keep `getByRole`/`getByLabelText`/`getByRole("dialog")`, stable `data-testid`s, and the asserted class hooks (`.opt`, `.stream-row`, `.band-card`, etc.) since `.closest(...)` assertions depend on them. New screens must keep `aria-label`s/`role`s.
- **New single-points-of-failure get dedicated coverage** (the completeness review's gap): the `inspector-registry` resolver table; the **id-prefix → type router** (the load-bearing language-agnostic discovery path — test every `src/util/ids.ts` prefix + the ambiguous-numeric degrade-to-why?-only path); Inspector tab-capability visibility per object type; the global-vs-per-session WS subscription change in `useLiveCache`; the Orrery's mode prop fed a cached `flowSnapshotByTurn`.
- **Verify with full `pnpm typecheck` + `pnpm test`** (root multi-project typecheck + the web filter), per standing instruction — not a single-file/focused run.

---

## 7. Validated Backend-Gap Table

Deduped and prioritized from the aggregated list. **blocker** = a named feature cannot function without it; **polish-multiplier** = improves accuracy/scale/UX but the screen degrades gracefully. The plan ships with **none** of these closed.

| Gap | Tag | Screens that degrade & how |
|---|---|---|
| **Universal Inspector frontend surface** (not an API — the `Inspector`/`registry`/`provider`) | **blocker** | 9 screens' pivots non-functional. **Resolved by sequencing as P0**, not by backend. |
| Per-session-pulse WS transport (client `subscribe_global` or `last_activity_at` deltas) | **blocker** (for the live-pulse *feature*) | SessionFleet / Mission Control: per-row recency pulse fires for active session only; otherwise rows show no live pulse. Fix in P0.2. |
| `DisclosureLabel` type-surfacing: lift `state_metadata.disclosure_label` to a typed field + define 6→4 `MemoryDisclosureClass` collapse | **blocker** (for the label component as specced) | Design system / Memory Atlas / Ledger: without it, disclosure renders only inside opaque Raw JSON, not as a typed chip. |
| List/count quarantined+inactive attachments (`GET /api/attachments` is batch-by-`ids=` only) | **blocker** (for one card) | Mission Control: the quarantined-attachments AttentionQueue card cannot enumerate — omit the card or show "needs id set." |
| Unified `GET /api/object/:id` (resolve any id → typed object) | polish-multiplier | Inspector / Command Palette: raw-id pivots degrade to why?-only; explicit-type clicks unaffected. |
| Related-object endpoint `GET /api/object/:id/related` | polish-multiplier | Inspector Relationships tab: reconstructed client-side from per-row fields; transitive closure not guaranteed. |
| Unified cross-corpus search endpoint | polish-multiplier | Command Palette: "search" scoped to the 3 searchable bands (`episodic/semantic/procedural`) + stream kind-filter. |
| Stream fetch by entry-ids / by turn-id; stream date-range + full-text + turn-status/structural filters; forward (after) cursor | polish-multiplier | Stream & Provenance: filters/search are client-side over the loaded window only; `source_stream_*_ids` pivots resolve only if in-window. |
| Per-id GETs (episode, identity sub-objects, skill/mood/social/relational, single stream entry, entity-by-id) | polish-multiplier | Inspector: relies on caller-supplied hint rows / in-list resolution; opening from a bare id without the list loaded fails. |
| Correction id-prefix routing for `grw_` / `abp_` (growth-marker / autobiographical-period) — **the only genuinely-missing identity prefixes** | polish-multiplier | Identity Studio: why/correct/forget 4xx on growth-markers & periods → Actions tab disables those two. (Value/goal/trait/open-question/commitment **already route** — earlier "4 more missing" claim was wrong.) |
| Per-band correction for procedural/affective/social/relational/commitments ids | polish-multiplier | Memory Atlas: Inspector Actions disabled for those band ids (why/forget/correct unavailable). |
| Pagination + search for social/relational/affective/commitments bands | polish-multiplier | Memory Atlas: those bands load one page; no search box. |
| Persisted historical turn-phase traces / ledgers (survive refresh/restart) | polish-multiplier | Workbench / Inspector: Replay & Timeline empty after refresh past the 64-LRU; `getLedger` 404s post-restart ("ledger not retained pre-restart"). |
| `duration_ms` on `TurnHistoryRow` | polish-multiplier | Workbench: history strip shows duration only for turns still in flow cache. |
| Dedicated `POST /api/reviews/:id/commitment-reconciliation` | polish-multiplier | Review/Governance: commitment-reconciliation resolves via generic `PATCH /api/reviews/:id` (survivor in note) — works, less ergonomic than directive parity. |
| `GET /api/entities` (list) | polish-multiplier | Governance: non-creator entity rows inferred from sessions + creator join. |
| Counts-by-kind/status aggregates (reviews/commitments/directives/outcomes); suppressed/observed outcome count-window | polish-multiplier | Mission Control: full-list client scans (R4 windowing caveat applies). |
| Scheduler writes: per-process budget, enabled toggle, interval edit; live in-run budget meter | polish-multiplier | Dream Ops: budget/interval/enabled read-only; budget shown from plan/apply responses, not a live meter. |
| Prompt revision-history (`GET /api/prompts/:key/revisions` + restore); exact token-count tokenizer | polish-multiplier | Prompt Lab: only single default-vs-current diff; token count is a labeled rough chars/4 estimate; Inspector Relationships/Timeline tabs render empty-state for prompt blocks. |
| Per-review `severity`/`source_process`; correction before/after preview endpoint | polish-multiplier | Review & Repair: severity badge & origin filters derived client-side from kind + refs shape; before/after reconstructed via `getSemanticNode`/band detail. |

**Fabricated-API corrections folded in** (must be relabeled as gaps, not bindings): Command Palette's open-by-id prefix table — `rev_` does **not** exist (reviews are numeric, list-only); `cd_` is wrong (real prefix `cdir_`, and creator directives are **not** a `getWhy` target); `turn_`→`getLedger` dead-ends for historical ids (live-cache only). The real open-by-id set is `ep_/semn_/seme_/cmt_` (getWhy) + `att_` (metadata) + `sess_` (session list). Also: Workbench's `GET /api/turn` is a typo for `POST /api/turn`.

---

## 8. Cardinal-Rule Compliance Statement

This redesign is a **presentation, labeling, navigation, and cross-linking** overhaul of the operator console. It touches nothing in `src/cognition/`, `src/retrieval/`, `src/memory/`, or `src/offline/`. The whole-plan completeness review found **no cardinal-rule drift in any area**, and that posture is affirmed and binding here:

1. **Recall stays global.** No screen, card, ring, filter, or fleet row makes the entity unaware of any memory because of the current audience, session, role, or speaker. Memory Atlas renders **every** band item the entity holds. The Orrery's band rings show **all** bands regardless of who is in the active session. The audience/session controls are **ranking-boost and view-scope** axes only, never `WHERE` predicates on recall. This is structurally enforced by the API surface the console binds to: `getMemoryBand` has **no** audience parameter, so an audience recall gate is literally unconstructible from these screens. The audience-parameterized reads that exist (`getSharedState?audience=`, optional `getCommitments({audience})`) are correctly scoped to **disclosure / common-ground / action-authorization browse**, not cognition recall.

2. **Disclosure is render-time labels.** `origin_audience`, `private`/`operator`, `trust_rank`, `taint`, `citation_type`, `session_scope`, participation policy, and commitment audience all render as `Tag`/`DisclosureLabel` chips at view time. **Labels annotate and rank; they never filter or hide a row.** Combined/unknown disclosure fails closed to `unknown` (shown as a neutral chip) and never demotes a private/unknown source to public. Common-ground (`origin_audience` vs current audience) remains a render-time provenance label, not a new "known-to" mechanism.

3. **No output policing.** No console surface inspects, scores, rewrites, or suppresses the entity's user-facing output. Every mutation routes through the **sanctioned** correction/review/governance/maintenance endpoints (why/forget/correct/invalidate/revoke/supersede/resolve/plan/apply/revert) or host-boundary action controls (participation policy, admin reset). Correction is a **proposal into the review queue** (or a clearly-warned, ack-gated identity direct-write), never an in-flight rewrite. `stream-outcomes` classification is a read-only observer of already-emitted entries, not a gate.

4. **Language-agnostic / structural keying.** Every encoding, filter, badge, and severity rank keys on **ids, enums, slots, and schema fields** (`state`, `enforcement_class`, `record_type`, `last_status`, `ReviewKind`, id-prefix taxonomy, numeric mood valence/arousal) — never on matching words in `label`/`description`/`question`/`narrative`/user content. The id-prefix router and command palette match `src/util/ids.ts` prefixes, never free-text tokens.

5. **Opus 5.0 verdict (whole plan): in-scope.** Every change is "the harness genuinely doesn't surface what the operator needs" (unwired caches, non-navigable provenance, no cross-link, lossy generic detail panes) — not "the model exercised poor judgment." This adds zero harness machinery that legislates the entity's judgment.

### Flags that MUST be honored during implementation

- **F1 — No recall gate may sneak in via "view scope."** `getMemoryBand`/`getMemoryBands` accept a `session` param; keep it strictly a **view-scope convenience** for operator inspection (admin/disclosure-side read). Never let `session` or audience drift into a "what the entity may know" predicate. SessionFleet's status/role/policy filters scope the **navigation roster only** — never any memory view.
- **F2 — `DisclosureLabel` 6→4 collapse must preserve fail-closed.** When mapping the six `MemoryDisclosureClass` values to the component's display axis, `sensitive` and `self_private` must not silently land as `public`; unknown/ambiguous must render `unknown`. Get the mapping reviewed against `src/memory/common/disclosure-label.ts` before shipping the component.
- **F3 — Severity chips rank, never gate.** `SeverityChip` orders review rows; it must never hide a row.
- **F4 — Audience as boost, never filter.** The Orrery's audience axis and any "current-audience" highlight may only re-rank/annotate; never remove a body or row.
- **F5 — Inspector Actions tab is the sanctioned-endpoint boundary.** It exposes only the listed correction/review/governance mutations; do not add any output-inspecting or semantic-judgment affordance to it.

These flags are not optional polish — they are the line that keeps an operator console from regressing into recall-gating or production-policing.

---

# Part II — Cross-cutting primitives (P0 backbone)

## Global Shell — App router replacement, left rail with live badges, top instrument strip, collapsible session fleet, and a shell-level global live cache
# Global Shell — design spec

## Purpose

Replace the ad-hoc routing + chrome currently hardwired into `App.tsx` with a real **operator shell** that (a) routes to all ten destinations, (b) preserves operator context (session/audience/role/policy/creator/WS/mood/counts/version) in a persistent instrument strip, (c) gives a dense, live, collapsible **session fleet** in place of the sessions sidebar, and (d) maintains a single **global live-event cache** so every screen sees the same WS-derived counts/recency without each screen opening its own socket or re-deriving badges. This is the P0 carrier: the rail badges, the strip, the fleet, and the cache are the substrate the redesigned screens and the Universal Inspector hang off of.

Constraint posture (CLAUDE.md): the shell renders **labels**, never gates. Audience / role / participation policy / creator status are disclosure + action-policy metadata shown on the strip and fleet rows; they are NOT passed to any memory/recall view as a filter and the shell never inspects or rewrites model output. The shell exposes provenance/labels and a uniform pivot seam; semantic judgment stays with the model and the sanctioned correction/review endpoints.

## Router strategy — extend custom routing, do NOT add react-router

Per the tooling finding, this app has ~10 flat top-level views, no nested routes, no params, and a proven URL-state pattern (`useSession`). Adding react-router buys nothing and breaks the prop-drilled, fetch-stubbed, context-injected test harness. **Decision: keep zero-dependency routing and add deep-linkable views by mirroring `useSession`.**

- **New file `demo/web/src/hooks/use-view.ts`** — clone of `hooks/use-session.ts`: reads `?view=` on mount, validates against the `RouteId` union (a `VIEW_IDS` `Set`), defaults to `"mission"`, writes via `window.history.replaceState` (deletes the param when it equals the default, exactly like `useSession`), and re-reads on `popstate`. Returns `{ view, setView }`. Session selection stays a separate `?session=` param via the untouched `useSession`. This gives shareable per-screen URLs (e.g. `?view=memory&session=sess_…`) without a router dep and keeps the render-screen-with-props test model intact.
- **`components/Rail.tsx` (refactored, not replaced):** extend `RouteId` to the 11 ids and reorder `RAIL_ITEMS` to the proposal's MC/COG/STR/MEM/ID/GOV/REV/DRM/PMT/ADM. `commit` + `directives` route ids collapse into one `"governance"` id (Governance Studio); add `"mission"` (MC, top) and `"admin"` (ADM, bottom, above the spacer). Glyph/short vocabulary reuses the existing single-glyph idiom: MC `⊕`, COG `ψ`, STR `≣`, MEM `◇`, ID `◐`, GOV `§`, REV `?`, DRM `☾`, PMT `›`, ADM `⚙`. `num` chords renumber 1–9 for the first nine (ADM gets no chord or `0`). `aria-label`/`title`/`aria-current` and the `.rail-btn .num/.glyph/.label` markup contract are preserved (tests assert on `aria-label`).

## Component breakdown

### NEW: `demo/web/src/components/AppShell.tsx` (`export function AppShell`)
The new top-level layout owner; `App.tsx` shrinks to wiring (see migration). Keeps the existing `.app` CSS grid (`var(--rail) 1fr` × `topbar/main/statusbar`) verbatim. Responsibilities:
- Owns `view` (from `useView`), `sessionId` (from `useSession`), `now` clock (reused 1s interval), and the three existing `useApi` resources (`getState`, `getSessions`, `getCreatorEntity`) — unchanged from today.
- Adds two more shell-level `useApi` resources used only for rail badges + admin: `getDreamState` and `getPrompts` (see Badge sourcing). These are shell-owned so the badge counts are stable across screen switches and refetch on the same live triggers.
- Wraps children in `LiveEventsProvider` (unchanged) **and** a new `ShellProvider` (below).
- Renders `<Rail>`, `<InstrumentStrip>`, `<SessionFleet>`, the `.screen-shell` view switch, and `<RuntimeStrip>` (status bar). The view switch is the same `{view === "x" ? <Screen/> : null}` chain, now including `<MissionControlScreen/>`, the merged `<GovernanceScreen/>`, and `<AdminScreen/>` (those three screens are designed by sibling specs; this spec only routes to them).

### NEW: `demo/web/src/hooks/use-shell-context.tsx` (`ShellProvider`, `useShellContext`)
React Context mirroring the existing `live-context.tsx` pattern (Provider + throwing consumer hook). Carries the shell's derived operator context so the strip, fleet, rail, and any screen read one object instead of re-deriving:
```
type ShellContextValue = {
  view: RouteId; setView(v: RouteId): void;
  sessionId: string; setSessionId(id: string): void;
  activeSession: SessionRecord | null;   // sessions.find(id)
  activeAudience: string;                 // activeSession?.audience_label ?? DEFAULT_AUDIENCE
  audienceRole: SessionAudienceRole | null;
  participationPolicy: SessionParticipationPolicy | null;
  creator: EntityRecord | null;
  state: StateSnapshot | null;
  wsState: WsState;
  badges: RailBadges;                     // see below
  openInspector(ref: InspectorRef): void; // SEAM ONLY — see Cross-link
  refetchAll(): Promise<void>;            // Promise.all of the shell useApi refetches
};
```
This is the single source of operator context. Screens that need session/audience/role keep receiving them as **props** from the shell (preserving the test contract), but the strip/fleet/rail read context to avoid prop spaghetti.

### NEW: `demo/web/src/hooks/use-live-cache.ts` (`useLiveCache`)
The global live-event cache. Today `App.tsx` has an inline `live.subscribe` that fires `refetchState/refetchSessions` on `stream:append`, stores `lastMaintenanceTick`, and reloads on `borg:reset`; `useTurnStream` separately caches per-turn flow. This hook **consolidates the global-frame consumption** (everything that is broadcast GLOBALLY or is not per-turn-flow) into one place so badges and the fleet's live pulse are driven once:
- ONE `live.subscribe(handler)` at shell scope. Maintains:
  - `lastMaintenanceTick: MaintenanceTickFrame | null` (from `maintenance:tick`) — feeds RuntimeStrip + DRM badge freshness.
  - `dreamActivity: { process, phase, run_id, startedAt } | null` (from `dream:process:started`/`dream:process:completed`, both GLOBAL-only frames) — feeds the strip's "active dream process" indicator and the DRM badge pulse.
  - `sessionActivity: Map<SessionId, { lastAppendTs, isNew }>` (from `stream:append`, keyed by `frame.session_id ?? entries[0].session_id`) — drives each fleet row's recency pulse without a per-session socket. Cap nothing (one entry per session); `isNew` is a short-lived flash flag cleared on a timer, mirroring `eventTail`'s `isNew` pattern in `use-turn-stream.ts`.
  - debounced refetch triggers: on `stream:append` → `refetchState`+`refetchSessions` (debounced ~250ms to coalesce burst appends, since the engine appends multiple entries/turn — today's code refetches per frame, which is wasteful); on `maintenance:tick` → `refetchState`+`refetchDreamState`; on `borg:reset` → `window.location.reload()` (unchanged — reset wipes everything and a reload is the correct, simplest response, matching current behavior). 
- Returns `{ lastMaintenanceTick, dreamActivity, sessionActivity }`. It does **not** duplicate `useTurnStream`'s per-turn frames (tokens/phases/ledger) — those stay session-scoped in Cognition. The split is: **`useLiveCache` = global broadcast + session-recency index; `useTurnStream` = the live turn's flow.** Both subscribe to the same `LiveEvents` fan-out hub, which already supports many handlers (a `Set` of handlers), so there is no extra socket.
- Language-agnostic by construction: it keys on frame `type`, `session_id`, `process` name (an enum), and timestamps — never on message content.

### NEW: `demo/web/src/components/InstrumentStrip.tsx` (`export function InstrumentStrip`) — replaces `Topbar.tsx`
The persistent top context bar / "instrument strip." Reuses the existing `.topbar`, `.topbar-brand`, `.topbar-crumb`, `.topbar-pills`, `.topbar-pill (.k/.v + tone)`, `.topbar-live` class vocabulary verbatim (the design-system map confirms these are the tokenized chrome classes). Reads `useShellContext`. Left→right:
- **Brand** `[borg]` (green brackets) — unchanged from `Topbar`.
- **Breadcrumb** `console › {session label or short id} › {view}` — reuse `.topbar-crumb .seg/.sep/.here`; the active `view` segment uses `.here`.
- Spacer.
- **Pills** (each `.topbar-pill` with `.k`/`.v` + tone class). Presentation is a horizontal pill row (not a table/graph): these are ≤9 scalar key/values, the canonical "instrument" shape the existing `.topbar-pills` already encodes, so a pill row is the correct data shape per the proposal's "scalars → compact pills" rule.
  - `audience {activeAudience}`
  - `role {audienceRole}` — tone `.acc` when `operator`, neutral when `participant`. **Label only**; the design must not let role gate anything.
  - `policy {participationPolicy}` — tone: `active`→acc, `paused`/`observing`→warn, `muted`→bad. Compact glyph optional; text is primary.
  - `creator {creator?.canonical_name ?? "none"}` — tone `.acc` when set, `.warn` (dim) when null (operator chat is unavailable until a creator is marked — this is the existing 409 condition surfaced proactively).
  - `mood {glyph}` — compact valence/arousal glyph from `state.current_mood` (see Mood glyph below).
  - `turns {counts.turns}` · `commit {counts.commitments}` · `open-q {counts.open_qs}` · `review {counts.open_reviews}` — tabular-nums (`.tab-num`), zero-padded as today.
  - `ws {live|reconnect|down}` — reuse existing `wsLabel`/`wsToneClass` (acc/warn/bad) from `Topbar` (move them into this file).
  - `ver {state.version}` — the runtime version string from `/api/state`.
  - `utc {now}` clock — unchanged.
  - `.topbar-live` dot — `live-dot` (pulsing) when `ws==="live"`, else `dot warn`.
- **End:** `<ResetButton/>` reused unchanged (it already reuses `Modal`/`.btn`). On ADM view this is the primary destructive control; it stays in the strip everywhere for consistency.

Mood glyph: a small fixed quadrant glyph derived structurally from `current_mood.valence`/`arousal` sign+magnitude (e.g. arrows/quadrant marks), with the numeric `v/a` as a `title` tooltip. It is **derived from numbers, not from any word**, so it is language-agnostic. No mood text labels.

### NEW: `demo/web/src/components/SessionFleet.tsx` (`export function SessionFleet`) — replaces `SessionsSidebar.tsx`
The collapsible global session fleet. Reuses the selectable-row-with-glowing-left-bar idiom (`.session-row.active`, `.session-foot .dot.alive`) and the `today/yesterday/earlier` grouping, `relativeTime`, `shortId`, `previewLine` helpers from `SessionsSidebar` (move them in, don't duplicate). Data presentation is a **dense list/table of rows**, not cards or a graph: sessions are a homogeneous, sortable, filterable collection where each row is a compact record — the proposal's "fleet" and the existing sidebar both confirm list rows. Per row (reusing `.session-row-top/.session-label/.session-time/.session-preview/.session-foot`):
- label + `relativeTime(last_activity_at)` (top line)
- preview line: `audience · source/conversation_kind` (reuse `previewLine`/`sourceLabel`)
- foot chips (reuse `.dot`, add `Tag` chips): **role** (operator/participant), **participation policy** (active/paused/observing/muted) as a toned `Tag`, **status** (active/idle/archived), `message_count` msg, and a **last-outcome** chip derived from the session's `last_turn_id` when that turn is in the live cache / turn history (terminal outcome tone: reflected→acc, suppressed_*→warn, aborted/error→bad). When no outcome is known, omit the chip (no fabrication).
- **live pulse:** `.dot.alive` (pulsing green) when `sessionActivity.get(session_id)?.isNew` from `useLiveCache`, else a static dot; the active session always shows the glowing-left-bar `.active` treatment.

Controls (reuse existing, add filters):
- **operator chat** button (`.operator-chat-button`) → `onOpenOperatorChat` (unchanged; 409 → existing `operatorChatError` "mark a creator first").
- **mark creator** form (`.sessions-creator-admin`) → `onSetCreatorByName` (unchanged).
- **change participation policy** — per-row kebab/right-click opens a small action menu posting `setSessionPolicy` (the endpoint already exists and Cognition uses it); locked when `audience_role === "operator"` (mirror the existing `ParticipationPolicyControl` lock rule). This is an **action-policy** write, not a recall gate — explicitly allowed.
- **filters** (`.filter-pills` / `.pill`): status `active|idle|archived`, and role `participant|operator`. Filters affect **only which session rows are listed in this navigation widget** — they do NOT touch what the entity recalls and do not filter any memory screen. (CLAUDE.md compliance: this is UI list filtering of a session roster, an audience/session *navigation* convenience, not a cognition gate.)
- **collapse:** a header toggle collapses the fleet to an icon-width rail (persist the collapsed bool in `localStorage`); the `.main` grid's first track switches from `var(--sidebar)` to a collapsed width. Collapsed rows show only the live-pulse dot + active highlight + a tooltip.

### NEW: `demo/web/src/components/RuntimeStrip.tsx` (`export function RuntimeStrip`) — replaces `StatusBar.tsx`
The bottom global runtime strip. Reuse `.statusbar .seg .k/.v (.ok/.warn/.bad) .sep .grow` verbatim. Reads `useShellContext` + `useLiveCache`. Segments: branch indicator (static, as today), `dream {dream_audit_rows} audit` + the maintenance-tick summary segment (reuse `maintenanceTickLabel`/`maintenanceTickTone` from `StatusBar`, driven by `useLiveCache.lastMaintenanceTick`), an **active-dream-process** segment when `dreamActivity` is non-null (`{process} {phase}` with a `.warn` pulsing tone, matching the dream-running amber convention), `mood`, `review`, `questions`, `commit`, `last {lastPhase}` (still threaded from the Cognition turn stream via context), spacer, `model`, `emb`. The version moves up to the strip; the bottom strip keeps the ops/maintenance telemetry.

### REUSED unchanged
- `components/ResetButton.tsx`, `components/Modal.tsx`, `components/Tag.tsx`, `components/Empty.tsx`, `components/Spark.tsx`, `components/Panel.tsx` — the shell composes these; none change.
- `hooks/use-session.ts`, `hooks/use-api.ts`, `hooks/use-live-events.ts`, `hooks/live-context.tsx` — unchanged transport/fetch primitives.
- `hooks/use-turn-stream.ts` — unchanged; still owns the live turn for Cognition. The shell passes it `lastPhase` into the RuntimeStrip exactly as today.

## Rail badge sourcing (exact, verified)

Badges are computed in `AppShell` into a `RailBadges` object and threaded to `Rail` via context. Each badge is a count + a tone; a zero count renders no badge (no `0` clutter). Display reuses the existing `.rail-btn .num` slot styling extended with a small corner `.rail-badge` pill (new class, tinted like `Tag`).
- **REV (open reviews):** `state.counts.open_reviews` from `GET /api/state`. Tone warn when >0.
- **DRM (dream errors):** count of `getDreamState().processes` with `last_status === "error"` (`GET /api/dream/state`). Tone bad. Pulses while `useLiveCache.dreamActivity` is non-null. Falls back to `lastMaintenanceTick.errors > 0` for liveness between dream-state refetches.
- **MC/active sessions:** count of `getSessions().sessions` with `status === "active"`. Shown on **MC** (Mission Control), neutral tone — it is informational, not an alert.
- **GOV (critical governance):** count of critical, unresolved governance items = commitments with `enforcement_class === "critical" && state === "active"` (`GET /api/commitments`) **plus** open `creator_directive_reconciliation` + `commitment_reconciliation` review rows. To avoid a 5th shell fetch on every render, sources this from: `state.counts` does not break it out, so the badge uses (a) open-review subset already loaded for REV is insufficient (it is a total), therefore GOV needs `getCommitments` + a reconciliation-kind review count. **This is the one badge needing data the shell doesn't already load** — see Backend gap note; the buildable v1 sources it from `getCommitments` (critical+active) only, and adds reconciliation rows once the Review screen's data is lifted to the shell or a counts field is added. Tone bad when >0.
- **PMT (prompt overrides):** count of `getPrompts().blocks` with `overridden === true` (`GET /api/prompts`). Tone neutral/info (overrides are a state, not an error).

All five count derivations key on **structural fields** (enum `state`, `enforcement_class`, `last_status`, `overridden`, `status`) — no content matching, language-agnostic.

## Key interactions
- Click a rail item → `setView(id)` → URL `?view=` updates (replaceState) → conditional render swaps. ⌘-number chords still work (the `title` advertises them; a global keydown listener in `AppShell` maps `1–9` to the first nine view ids, reusing the `RAIL_ITEMS` order — this is new but trivial and matches the advertised `title`).
- Click a fleet row → `setSessionId` (URL `?session=` via `useSession`) → all session-scoped `useApi` deps re-run; the strip breadcrumb + pills update from context.
- Right-click / kebab a fleet row → action menu: change participation policy (`setSessionPolicy`), copy session id, "open in Stream" (`setView("stream")` keeping the session). 
- Collapse fleet → grid reflow + persisted preference.
- `borg:reset` frame → full reload (unchanged).

## Cross-link pivots (into/out of the Universal Inspector)
The Inspector is a sibling piece; this shell provides the **seam and the host**, not the inspector internals:
- `useShellContext.openInspector(ref: InspectorRef)` is the single entry point every screen calls to pivot an object (stream entry / turn / ledger entry / episode / semantic node|edge / commitment / directive / shared-state / review row / dream audit / attachment / prompt block / session / entity). `InspectorRef = { kind, id, sessionId? }` — keyed on structural id+kind, never on words.
- The shell renders the Inspector drawer as a right-side overlay sibling to `.screen-shell` (so it floats over any view). The fleet, strip badges, and rail are all valid pivot sources: clicking the strip's `creator` pill → `openInspector({kind:"entity", id:creator.id})`; clicking a fleet row's last-outcome chip → `openInspector({kind:"turn", id:last_turn_id})`; clicking a rail badge → `setView` to the owning screen (REV→review, DRM→dream, PMT→prompts, GOV→governance) rather than the inspector.
- The Inspector's "Open in source screen" action calls `setView(...)` (and optionally `setSessionId`) — i.e. the inspector drives routing **through the shell's view/session state**, which is why both must live at shell scope. This spec guarantees that contract; the inspector's tabs/content are out of scope here.

## States: loading / empty / error / live
- **Loading:** `AppShell` renders chrome immediately; pills/badges show `—` (existing `countValue` idiom) until `useApi` resolves. Fleet shows a `Empty`/`.notice` skeleton row while `sessionsApi.loading`.
- **Empty:** zero sessions → fleet shows the operator-chat CTA + mark-creator form only (today's behavior). Zero of a badge count → no badge.
- **Error:** any shell `useApi.error` surfaces as a toned strip pill (e.g. `state err`) rather than blanking the chrome; the rail/fleet stay usable. `getDreamState`/`getPrompts` errors degrade gracefully (their badges just don't render).
- **Live:** WS `live` → green `live-dot` + fleet rows flash on `stream:append`; `reconnecting` → strip `ws` pill warn + `dot warn`; `down` after 5 failed attempts → `ws` bad. `onReconnected` (already wired into `useLiveEvents`) triggers `refetchAll`. `maintenance:tick`/`dream:*` update the RuntimeStrip telemetry and DRM/MC badges without a screen change.

## Migration notes
- **`App.tsx`** shrinks to: instantiate `useView`, `useSession`, `useLiveEvents`, the shell `useApi` resources, `useTurnStream`, `useLiveCache`, derive `ShellContextValue`, and render `<LiveEventsProvider><ShellProvider><AppShell/></ShellProvider></LiveEventsProvider>`. The inline `live.subscribe` effect and `lastMaintenanceTick` state **move into `useLiveCache`**. `openOperatorChat`/`markCreatorByName`/`refetchSessionState` move onto the context value (logic unchanged, including the 409 handling).
- **`components/Topbar.tsx` → deleted**, replaced by `InstrumentStrip.tsx` (its `wsLabel`/`wsToneClass`/`formatTurns` helpers move over).
- **`components/StatusBar.tsx` → deleted**, replaced by `RuntimeStrip.tsx` (its `moodLabel`/`countValue`/`maintenanceTickLabel`/`maintenanceTickTone` helpers move over).
- **`components/SessionsSidebar.tsx` → deleted**, replaced by `SessionFleet.tsx` (its grouping + time/preview/id helpers move over). `.main` grid first track becomes collapsible.
- **`components/Rail.tsx` → refactored in place:** `RouteId` union changes (adds `mission`/`admin`, replaces `commit`+`directives` with `governance`), `RAIL_ITEMS` reordered, badge slot added. Every `App.tsx` callsite that passed `onOpenReview={() => setRoute("review")}` etc. now uses `setView` via context.
- **Screens:** `CommitScreen` + `DirectivesScreen` are routed by the new `governance` id (their merge into Governance Studio is a sibling spec; until then `governance` can render a temporary two-tab host of the existing two screens to avoid a dead route). `MissionControlScreen` and `AdminScreen` are new sibling specs; this shell just routes to them.
- **CSS:** add `.rail-badge` (tinted corner pill), a collapsed-fleet width var, and a strip `policy/role/creator/ver/mood` pill — all reusing existing tint/tone tokens; no new color direction. Reconcile the few hardcoded literals only where touched.

## Opus 5.0 verdict
In-scope: this is pure operator-UI presentation/navigation + a shared live-cache plumbing layer. It surfaces existing labels (audience/role/policy/creator/provenance) and existing counts; it adds no recall gate, no output policing, no language-keyed logic. A 10× model would still need a console that shows context and routes — the failure being fixed is "chrome is hardwired into App and each screen re-derives live state," a harness/presentation gap, not a model-judgment gap.
**New files:** demo/web/src/hooks/use-view.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/use-shell-context.tsx, demo/web/src/hooks/use-live-cache.ts, demo/web/src/components/AppShell.tsx, demo/web/src/components/InstrumentStrip.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SessionFleet.tsx, demo/web/src/components/RuntimeStrip.tsx
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/App.tsx (shrunk to wiring), demo/web/src/components/Rail.tsx (refactored: RouteId union + badge slot), demo/web/src/components/ResetButton.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Modal.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Empty.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Spark.tsx, demo/web/src/hooks/use-session.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/use-live-events.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx, demo/web/src/hooks/use-turn-stream.ts, demo/web/src/styles.css (extend tokens/classes: .rail-badge, collapsed-fleet width, strip pills)
**API bindings:** GET /api/state, GET /api/sessions, GET /api/entities/creator, POST /api/entities/creator, POST /api/sessions/operator, POST /api/sessions/:id/participation, GET /api/dream/state, GET /api/prompts, GET /api/commitments, GET /api/reviews, POST /api/admin/reset
**Live frames:** stream:append, maintenance:tick, dream:process:started, dream:process:completed, borg:reset
**Cross-link pivots:** session -> universal inspector (kind:session, from fleet row), entity/creator -> universal inspector (kind:entity, from strip creator pill), turn -> universal inspector (kind:turn, from fleet last-outcome chip via last_turn_id), rail badge REV/DRM/PMT/GOV -> setView(owning screen), inspector 'open in source screen' -> shell setView/setSessionId (inspector drives routing through shell state)
**Backend gaps needed:** A breakout count for 'critical governance' (critical+active commitments AND open reconciliation reviews) is not a single field in GET /api/state.counts. v1 derives it client-side from GET /api/commitments (enforcement_class==='critical' && state==='active') + GET /api/reviews?openOnly filtered to kind in {creator_directive_reconciliation, commitment_reconciliation}. A future GET /api/state.counts.critical_governance field would remove two shell-level fetches but is not required to ship., Per-session 'last turn outcome' for the fleet row depends on resolving session.last_turn_id to a terminal outcome; outcomes are only known for turns observed in the live cache or fetchable via GET /api/turns. No endpoint returns last-outcome-by-session directly, so the chip renders only when the outcome is already cached (omitted otherwise) -- a per-session last_outcome field on SessionRecord would make it always-available but is not required.
---

## Universal Inspector Drawer — the cross-linking primitive
## Universal Inspector Drawer — the cross-linking primitive

### Purpose
A single, persistent right-side drawer that ANY selected object across the console opens into, with a fixed six-tab anatomy (Summary / Evidence / Relationships / Timeline / Actions / Raw JSON). It replaces the ~10 bespoke per-screen detail panels (Memory's `MemoryDrill` properties sidebar, Identity's card actions, Stream's detail pane, Commit/Directives/Review detail panels, Dream audit payload renderer) with one component whose behavior is driven by a per-type **object-model registry**. This is what turns the app from "a set of debug panels" into "one cross-linked organism": every id is clickable and lands in the same place, and from there pivots into every related object.

It is an OPERATOR-CONSOLE inspector that honors the architecture: it surfaces what the entity KNOWS with disclosure/provenance/taint/audience rendered as **labels**, never as recall filters. It exposes only the SANCTIONED correction/review/governance mutations (the verified endpoints); it does not rewrite or suppress the entity's output.

Opus 5.0 verdict: **in-scope** — this is a presentation/cross-linking/labeling primitive (surfacing provenance and disclosure labels the harness already produces), not recall gating and not output policing. The one structural gap it exposes (no unified `{type,id}` lookup endpoint) is flagged, not worked around with heuristics on user content.

---

### Component breakdown

**New files (under `demo/web/src/`):**

- `components/Inspector/InspectorDrawer.tsx` — the drawer shell. Right-side panel docked into `.app` grid (or absolutely-positioned overlay over `.main` so it works from every screen without restructuring each screen's grid). Header: object glyph + type label + short id + Copy-ID button + close. Tab strip (reuses the `semanticViewMode`/`Xray` two-tab visual idiom, generalized to 6). Body renders the active tab. Footer reserved for primary Action. Built on the existing `Modal` motion/escape semantics but as a **docked drawer**, not a centered modal — new CSS `.inspector-*` namespace mirroring `.stream-*`/`.band-detail` panel idioms (hairline borders, `bg-1` body on `bg-0` header, uppercase eyebrows).
- `components/Inspector/inspector-registry.ts` — the heart. A typed `Record<InspectorObjectType, ObjectModel>` declaring, per type: `glyph`, `label`, `resolve(id, ctx) => Promise<Raw>` (which verified client fn(s) to call), `summary(raw)`, `evidence(raw)`, `relationships(raw)`, `timeline(raw)`, `capabilities` (which Action buttons + which tabs are non-empty), and `sourceRoute` (the `RouteId` "Open in source screen" pivots to). Pure data + adapters; no fetching of its own beyond the declared client fns.
- `components/Inspector/inspector-context.tsx` — `InspectorProvider` + `useInspector()`. Holds a **target stack** `InspectorTarget[]` where `InspectorTarget = { type: InspectorObjectType | "unknown", id: string, presetTab?: InspectorTab, hint?: unknown }`. `open(target)` pushes (replacing if same id), `pivot(target)` pushes a child (back-arrow returns), `close()` clears. Exposes `setRoute` escape (threaded from `App`) for "Open in source screen". This is the ONLY new React context besides `LiveEventsContext`; it carries selection, not app data.
- `components/Inspector/inspector-id.ts` — `sniffType(id): InspectorObjectType | "unknown"` — STRUCTURAL prefix routing only (`ep_`→episode, `semn_`→semantic_node, `seme_`→semantic_edge, `sess_`→session, plus any other stable prefixes), generalizing the existing `correctionActionKind` so it lives in one place. Numeric ids (reviews, commitments, audit rows, dream-review) are inherently ambiguous from the id alone → returns `"unknown"`; such cross-links MUST carry an explicit `type` (callers always have it). No content/word matching — pure id-shape.
- `components/Inspector/tabs/SummaryTab.tsx`, `EvidenceTab.tsx`, `RelationshipsTab.tsx`, `TimelineTab.tsx`, `ActionsTab.tsx`, `RawJsonTab.tsx` — thin renderers that consume the registry adapter output.
- `components/Inspector/CorrectionActions.tsx` — the consolidated correct/forget/invalidate/revoke/supersede/resolve action block (replaces the near-identical duplicated flows in Memory and Identity), reusing the existing JSON-patch + reason `Modal` form.
- `components/DisclosureLabels.tsx` — a small row of `Tag`s rendering provenance/disclosure metadata present on a row (`origin_audience`, `session_scope`, `actor`, `trust_rank`, `taint`, `persistence_class`, `citation_type`). Pure labels.

**Existing components reused verbatim or near-verbatim:**

- `components/Modal.tsx`, `components/Panel.tsx`, `components/Tag.tsx` (+ `TagKind`), `components/Empty.tsx`, `components/Spark.tsx`, `components/SupersededByChip.tsx` (lineage chips in Relationships), `components/AttachmentChip.tsx` + `components/ImagePlaceholder.tsx` (attachment evidence).
- `components/SemanticNodeDetail.tsx` and `components/SemanticEdgeDetail.tsx` — dropped directly into the Summary tab for `semantic_node`/`semantic_edge`. They are already clean standalone detail cards.
- `screens/Cognition/LedgerView.tsx` — reused as the Evidence tab body for a `turn` (it already does graceful 404 + per-section trust/taint tagging).
- `components/WhyDrawer.tsx` — its `JsonValueView` + `getWhy(id)` plumbing is lifted into `EvidenceTab` (the "Show provenance / why?" content becomes a tab section rather than a separate modal). The standalone modal export can be retired once all call sites move to the Inspector.
- `screens/Memory/SemanticTopology.tsx` — embeddable as a compact Relationships graph for graph-shaped objects (semantic node/edge neighborhoods) via its existing `graph/selectedId/onSelectNode` props.
- `hooks/use-api.ts` (`useApi`) for all tab fetches; `hooks/live-context.tsx` (`useLiveEventsContext`) for live tab refresh; `hooks/use-turn-stream.ts` caches (`flowSnapshotByTurn`, `ledgerByTurn`, `eventTail`) consumed read-only for turn objects.
- `lib/stream-utils.ts` (`formatTime`, `streamContentText`) and `lib/shared-state-lifecycle.ts` (`lifecycleLabel`/`tagKind`) for shared-state rows.

---

### Client-side object model — how `{type,id}` resolves

The registry maps each `InspectorObjectType` to a resolver and capability set. Resolution path:

1. **Explicit type (the common case).** Every click site already knows the type (a ledger entry's `source_type`, a band row's band, a review row, a commitment). It calls `inspector.open({ type, id })`. The registry's `resolve(id)` fetches via the verified client fn for that type. There is no guessing.
2. **Raw id string (cross-links / command palette "open object by id").** `sniffType(id)` does structural prefix routing. On a known prefix it resolves normally; on `"unknown"` (numeric / unrecognized id) the drawer opens in a **degraded mode**: Raw JSON tab is empty (no fetch), and only the `why?` action is offered (`getWhy(id)` works for any id server-side and degrades on 404). This is the visible cost of the missing unified lookup endpoint.

Per-type resolver bindings (all from the verified client/catalog):

| type | resolve via | source screen |
|---|---|---|
| stream entry | from live `stream:append` cache / `getStream` row (no per-id GET) | stream |
| turn | `getTurns` row + `getLedger(id)` (lazy) + `flowSnapshotByTurn`/`ledgerByTurn` cache | cognition |
| ledger entry | the in-memory `EvidenceLedgerEntry` (no GET; passed as `hint`) → pivots to its `source_type` object | cognition |
| episode | from `getMemoryBand("episodic")` row (no per-id GET) | memory |
| semantic node | `getSemanticNode(id)` | memory |
| semantic edge | `getSemanticEdge(id)` | memory |
| skill / mood / social / relational | from `getMemoryBand(band)` row (passed as `hint`; no per-id GET) | memory |
| value/goal/trait/open question/growth marker | from `getIdentity()` bundle (passed as `hint`; no per-id GET) | identity |
| commitment | `getCommitments` row (filter by id) | commit |
| creator directive | `getCreatorDirectives` row (filter by id) | directives |
| shared-state entry | `getSharedState({audience})` row | directives |
| review row | `getReviews`/`getCorrectionReviews` row (filter by id) | review |
| dream audit row | `getDreamAudit` row (filter by id) | dream |
| attachment | `getAttachmentMetadata(id)` (+ `attachmentBytesUrl` for image) | stream |
| prompt block | `getPrompts` row (by key) + `getAssembledPrompt` | prompts |
| session | `getSessions` row (filter by id) | (sets global session) |
| entity | `getCreatorEntity` / from row `sender_entity_id` (no generic GET-by-id) | — |

Where there is **no per-id GET** (episode, identity sub-objects, skill/mood/social/relational, ledger entry), the registry expects the caller to pass the already-fetched row as `hint` (the click site has it). For ids arriving raw without a hint and with no GET endpoint, the type's Summary degrades to "open in source screen to view" + Raw JSON of the hint if present. This too is the missing-lookup gap surfaced honestly rather than papered over.

---

### Tabs — data presentation per tab

Tab presence is registry-driven (`capabilities.tabs`); absent tabs are hidden, never shown empty-with-error.

- **Summary** — a card. For typed detail components (`SemanticNodeDetail`/`SemanticEdgeDetail`) it embeds them. Otherwise a `.props` key/value block (the existing detail idiom) + a `DisclosureLabels` row of `Tag` chips. Justification: object identity is a flat attribute set → key/value card is the right shape, matching every existing detail pane.
- **Evidence** — provenance + supporting sources. For a `turn`, the full `LedgerView` (sections of `EvidenceLedgerEntry`, each with trust/taint/citation tags) — a **table-per-section** because the ledger is inherently sectioned rows with shared columns. For memory objects, the `getWhy(id)` provenance tree (lifted from `WhyDrawer`) + source-id chips (`source_stream_ids`/`source_episode_ids`/`source_stream_entry_ids`) rendered as `SupersededByChip`-style pivots. Each evidence row carries its disclosure label; nothing is filtered by audience.
- **Relationships** — pivots to connected objects. **Card list of chips** by relation class (sources, derived-from, supersession chain via `SupersededByChip`, contradicting/supporting edges, related reviews, subject entity, directive family). For semantic objects, optionally the embedded `SemanticTopology` neighborhood graph (graph shape justified: semantic memory is natively a graph). Each chip is a `pivot()` into a child inspector target. Relationships are derived from fields already on the resolved row (`from_node_id`/`to_node_id`/`evidence_episode_ids`/`source_stream_entry_ids`/`superseded_by`/`refs`) — NO new related-object endpoint; "show everything connected" completeness is the named gap.
- **Timeline** — event history. **Vertical timeline list** (reuses the Identity `OpenQuestionEventsSection` pattern — the richest existing provenance timeline). For a turn: phase timeline from `flowSnapshotByTurn` / `eventTail`. For commitments/directives: created→superseded/revoked lifecycle. For dream audit: run/apply/revert events. Timeline justified: these are ordered events with timestamps.
- **Actions** — the sanctioned mutations only, registry-gated by capability and by structural state (e.g. revoke only on `active`). Buttons reuse `.btn`/`.btn.primary`/`.btn.live-write`. Maps:
  - episode/semantic-node → `why?` (`getWhy`), `correct` (`postCorrectionCorrect`, JSON-patch+reason, queues review), `forget` (`postCorrectionForget`).
  - semantic edge → above + `invalidate` (`postSemanticEdgeInvalidate`).
  - commitment → `revoke` (`postCommitmentRevoke`), `correct` (queues review).
  - creator directive → `revoke` (`revokeCreatorDirective`), `supersede` (`supersedeCreatorDirective`), open reconciliation review.
  - goal → `complete/block/progress` (`patchGoal`); open question → `resolve/abandon/bump` (`patchOpenQuestion`); value/growth → `correct/forget` via correction endpoints.
  - review row → resolve (`patchReview` / `patchCorrectionReview` / `resolveCreatorDirectiveReconciliation` per kind; `patchReviewItem` dismiss for belief-revision).
  - dream audit row → `revert` (`revertDreamAudit`, gated on reversal payload present + not reverted).
  - prompt block → `save` (`putPrompt`), `reset` (`deletePrompt`).
  - session → `setSessionPolicy`. Live-write actions keep the existing amber `.live-write` + acknowledge warnings.
- **Raw JSON** — the resolved row pretty-printed (the existing raw-JSON `<details>`/`why-pre` idiom). Always present when a row is in hand; empty in degraded/unknown mode.

---

### Cross-link pivots (into and out of the Inspector)

The seven universal controls from the proposal, all wired:
- **Copy ID** — clipboard write (no endpoint).
- **Open in source screen** — `inspector.setRoute(model.sourceRoute)` (threaded from `App`'s `setRoute`), optionally preselecting; for `session` it also sets global `sessionId`.
- **Show provenance / why?** — focuses the Evidence tab (`getWhy(id)`); degrades gracefully on 404 ("no provenance retained").
- **Show related reviews** — Relationships chips filtered to review refs that name this id (from `getReviews` `refs`); pivots into the review object.
- **Show related stream entries** — Relationships chips for `source_stream_*ids`; pivot opens the stream-entry inspector (and "open in source screen" deep-links the Stream tail).
- **Correct / forget / invalidate / revoke / supersede / resolve** — the Actions tab, capability-gated.

Inbound pivots replace every screen's bespoke detail panel: Memory `MemoryDrill` rows, Identity cards, Stream detail rows, Commit/Directives/Review master-detail selections, Dream audit rows, ledger entries in Cognition, and the command palette's "open object by id" all call `inspector.open(...)`. Nested pivots push onto the target stack with a back-arrow (open episode → its semantic node → that node's contradicting edge → the review that invalidated it), which is the "feels powerful" cross-linking.

---

### States
- **Loading** — per-tab `.notice` "loading" (matches `WhyDrawer`/`LedgerView`); the shell opens immediately with header from `{type,id}`.
- **Empty** — registry hides tabs with no data; a tab with a legitimately empty collection shows `Empty`/`.notice` ("no related reviews").
- **Error** — per-tab `.notice.bad` with message; a 404 on a per-id resolver shows the degraded "open in source screen" affordance, not a hard failure (mirrors `LedgerView`'s "ledger not retained" handling).
- **Live** — for turn/stream/dream targets, subscribe via `useLiveEventsContext` and refresh on relevant frames (`stream:append`, `turn:phase:*`, `evidence_ledger:built`, `maintenance:tick`, `dream:process:*`); `borg:reset` closes the drawer. The header live-dot pulses (existing `.dot.alive`) when the inspected object is the active live turn.
- **Disclosure** — every recalled row renders its `DisclosureLabels`; nothing is hidden by current audience. Private/operator/taint shown as `Tag` chips; combined-label `unknown` shown as a neutral chip, never demoted to "public".

---

### Migration note
- **Becomes the Inspector:** the duplicated correction flow (`correctionActionKind` + JSON-patch `Modal` in `Memory/index.tsx`; `IdentityCorrectionButtons` + direct-write modals in `Identity/index.tsx`; Commit's "correct" modal) collapses into `CorrectionActions.tsx` driven by the registry. The read-only Stream detail pane, Dream audit payload renderer, and the Commit/Directives/Review detail panels are reframed as registry adapters feeding the shared tabs.
- **Reused, not rewritten:** `SemanticNodeDetail`/`SemanticEdgeDetail`, `LedgerView`, `SupersededByChip`, `SemanticTopology`, `WhyDrawer`'s JSON renderer, `AttachmentChip`, and the `OpenQuestionEventsSection` timeline pattern are imported rather than reimplemented.
- **Deleted/retired:** `WhyDrawer` as a standalone modal call site (its content lives in the Evidence tab); per-screen ad-hoc "properties sidebar" action wiring; the duplicated correction-modal state machines across Memory/Identity/Commit.
- **Not changed:** `correctionActionKind` logic is generalized into `inspector-id.ts:sniffType` (same structural prefixes), not duplicated. Routing stays the custom `setRoute` pattern (no react-router); the Inspector adds one context, consistent with `LiveEventsContext`.
- **Tests:** colocated `InspectorDrawer.test.tsx` / `inspector-registry.test.ts` using the existing fetch-stub + `LiveEventsProvider` injection conventions; keep `aria-label`/`role="dialog"`/`data-testid` hooks and `.tag`/`.props`/`.btn` class contracts the suite asserts on.

### Backend gaps (flagged, not worked around)
- **Unified `{type,id}` lookup endpoint** — without it, raw numeric ids (reviews/commitments/audit/dream-review) cannot be resolved from the id alone; the Inspector requires an explicit `type` or a passed-in row `hint`, and degrades to why?-only otherwise.
- **Related-object endpoint** — "show everything connected" is reconstructed client-side from fields already on each row; completeness/transitive closure is not guaranteed without it.
- **Per-id GET for episode / identity sub-objects / skill·mood·social·relational / stream entry / generic entity** — these have no fetch-by-id; the Inspector relies on caller-supplied `hint` rows or "open in source screen".
- **Persisted historical turn phase traces** — turn Timeline/Evidence after refresh/restart depends on live caches (`flowSnapshotByTurn`/`ledgerByTurn`) and `getLedger` 404s post-restart.
**New files:** demo/web/src/components/Inspector/InspectorDrawer.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Inspector/inspector-registry.ts, demo/web/src/components/Inspector/inspector-context.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Inspector/inspector-id.ts, demo/web/src/components/Inspector/tabs/SummaryTab.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Inspector/tabs/EvidenceTab.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Inspector/tabs/RelationshipsTab.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Inspector/tabs/TimelineTab.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Inspector/tabs/ActionsTab.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Inspector/tabs/RawJsonTab.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Inspector/CorrectionActions.tsx, demo/web/src/components/DisclosureLabels.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Inspector/InspectorDrawer.test.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Inspector/inspector-registry.test.ts
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/components/Modal.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Panel.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Empty.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Spark.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SupersededByChip.tsx, demo/web/src/components/AttachmentChip.tsx, demo/web/src/components/ImagePlaceholder.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SemanticNodeDetail.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SemanticEdgeDetail.tsx, demo/web/src/components/WhyDrawer.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/LedgerView.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Memory/SemanticTopology.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Memory/index.tsx (correctionActionKind/defaultMemoryPatch -> inspector-id.ts/CorrectionActions), demo/web/src/screens/Identity/index.tsx (IdentityCorrectionButtons + OpenQuestionEventsSection patterns), demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx, demo/web/src/hooks/use-turn-stream.ts, demo/web/src/lib/stream-utils.ts, demo/web/src/lib/shared-state-lifecycle.ts, demo/web/src/api/client.ts, demo/web/src/api/types.ts, demo/web/src/App.tsx (thread setRoute + wrap in InspectorProvider), demo/web/src/styles.css (extend .inspector-* on existing tokens)
**API bindings:** GET /api/state, GET /api/sessions, GET /api/stream, GET /api/turns, GET /api/turns/:id/ledger, GET /api/semantic/nodes/:id, GET /api/semantic/edges/:id, GET /api/semantic/graph, GET /api/memory/bands, GET /api/memory/bands/:id, GET /api/identity, GET /api/commitments, GET /api/creator-directives, GET /api/shared-state, GET /api/reviews, GET /api/correction/reviews, GET /api/correction/:id/why, GET /api/dream/audit, GET /api/attachments/:id, GET /api/attachments, GET /api/attachments/:id/bytes, GET /api/prompts, GET /api/prompts/assembled, GET /api/entities/creator, POST /api/correction/:id/forget, POST /api/correction/:id/correct, POST /api/correction/semantic-edges/:id/invalidate, PATCH /api/reviews/:id, PATCH /api/correction/reviews/:id, POST /api/reviews/:id/creator-directive-reconciliation, POST /api/commitments/:id/revoke, POST /api/creator-directives/:id/revoke, POST /api/creator-directives/:id/supersede, PATCH /api/identity/goals/:id, PATCH /api/identity/open-questions/:id, POST /api/dream/audit/:id/revert, PATCH /api/dream/review/:id, PUT /api/prompts/:key, DELETE /api/prompts/:key, POST /api/sessions/:id/participation
**Live frames:** stream:append, turn:phase:started, turn:phase:completed, turn:phase:failed, turn:terminal, turn:phase:detail, evidence_ledger:built, dream:process:started, dream:process:completed, maintenance:tick, borg:reset
**Cross-link pivots:** ledger entry -> source object (by EvidenceLedgerEntry.source_type/id), turn -> evidence ledger / stream entries / derived episodes / related reviews, episode -> source stream entries / derived semantic nodes+edges / autobiographical period / why?, semantic node -> evidence episodes / supporting+contradicting edges / related reviews / why?, semantic edge -> from_node / to_node / invalidating edge+review / why?, commitment -> source stream entries / directive family / supersession chain / related reviews, creator directive -> authorization+content stream entries / subject entity / supersession chain / reconciliation review, dream audit row -> process / target objects / reversal payload / related dream-report stream entry, review row -> referenced node/edge pair / target object / originating turn, shared-state entry -> canonical target (goal/commitment/action/open-question) / source stream entries, attachment -> parent turn / perception stream entry, any object -> Open in source screen (setRoute) and -> back-stack to parent inspector target, command palette open-object-by-id -> inspector via sniffType structural prefix
**Backend gaps needed:** Unified object lookup endpoint GET /api/object/:id (resolve {type,id} from a raw id alone) — without it, numeric ids (reviews/commitments/audit/dream-review) need an explicit type or a passed-in row hint; raw-id pivots degrade to why?-only, Related-object endpoint (GET /api/object/:id/related) — 'show everything connected' is reconstructed client-side from per-row fields; transitive/complete closure not guaranteed, Per-id GET for episode, identity sub-objects (value/goal/trait/open-question/growth), skill/mood/social/relational items, individual stream entry, and generic entity-by-id — currently only reachable via band/identity bundle rows or live caches, so Inspector relies on caller-supplied hint rows, Persisted historical turn phase traces — turn Timeline/Evidence after refresh/restart depend on in-memory flowSnapshotByTurn/ledgerByTurn and getLedger 404s post-restart, Dedicated POST /api/reviews/:id/commitment-reconciliation resolution endpoint — commitment_reconciliation rows must be resolved via generic PATCH /api/reviews/:id (no survivor-specific endpoint exists, unlike creator-directive reconciliation)
**Scope verdict:** In-scope: this is cross-linking, provenance surfacing, and disclosure-label presentation over what the harness already produces — it never gates recall by audience and never rewrites/suppresses the entity's output; the only structural gap (no unified {type,id} lookup) is flagged as a backend gap rather than solved with heuristics.
---

## Command Palette (⌘K) — keyboard-first operator command surface
## Purpose

A single keyboard-first command surface (⌘K / Ctrl+K) that indexes every navigation and action the operator console already supports, so the operator stops hunting across nine rail screens and hand-editing the `?session=` URL param. It is the P0 "command palette" primitive from proposal §10 and the keyboard entry point into the Universal Inspector object model (§4 "open object by ID").

It is a **navigation + invocation surface only**. It opens screens, switches session, scopes searches, opens objects in the inspector, and triggers the sanctioned operator endpoints (create commitment, dream-plan preview, reset, assembled-prompt). It performs **no** semantic judgment of the entity's output and **no** recall gating — search results are global to the being with disclosure/trust/taint shown as render-time `Tag` labels.

---

## Component breakdown

### New files (under `demo/web/src/`)

- **`components/CommandPalette/index.tsx`** — exports `CommandPalette`. A presentational overlay built on the existing `.modal-backdrop`/`.modal-card` shell (mirror `components/Modal.tsx`, do not reuse it verbatim because the palette needs a custom body: search input + grouped result list + footer hint, not `title/children/footer`). Props are fully threaded from `App.tsx`:
  ```ts
  type CommandPaletteProps = {
    open: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    route: RouteId;
    setRoute: (r: RouteId) => void;
    sessionId: string;
    setSessionId: (id: string) => void;
    sessions: SessionRecord[];          // from sessionsApi.data.sessions
    audience: string;                   // activeAudience
    onRunTurn?: TurnStream["runTurn"];  // optional; not required for v1
  ```
