  ```ts
    onOpenInspector: (ref: ObjectRef) => void; // pivot into Universal Inspector
  };
  ```
- **`components/CommandPalette/commands.ts`** — pure, testable command registry. Exports `buildStaticCommands(ctx)` returning `Command[]` for go-to-screen (one per `RAIL_ITEMS` entry, reusing that array — no parallel route table), switch-session (one per `SessionRecord`), and the action commands. Each `Command` is structural:
  ```ts
  type CommandKind = "goto" | "session" | "action" | "search" | "open-id";
  type Command = {
    id: string; kind: CommandKind; label: string; hint?: string;
    glyph?: string;            // reuse RAIL_ITEMS glyph where applicable
    keywords?: string[];       // STRUCTURAL tokens only (route ids, kinds, slugs) — never user content
    run: (ctx: PaletteCtx) => void | Promise<void>;
    tag?: { text: string; kind: TagKind };  // e.g. "destructive"/bad for reset
    enabled?: (ctx: PaletteCtx) => boolean;  // e.g. create-commitment disabled w/o creator
  };
  ```
- **`components/CommandPalette/object-ref.ts`** — `objectRefFromId(id: string): ObjectRef | null`. The id-prefix → type router. This is a **direct generalization of the existing `correctionActionKind`** in `screens/Memory/index.tsx:86` (`ep_`→episode, `semn_`→semantic_node, `seme_`→semantic_edge), extended to the prefixes whose detail/why routes already exist: `turn_`→turn (→ `getLedger`), `rev_`→review, `cmt_`→commitment, `cd_`→creator_directive, `sess_`→session, `att_`→attachment. Returns `null` for unknown prefixes (palette shows the "no lookup for this id" empty state). **No language matching** — keys purely on id token shape, exactly like `SESSION_ID_PATTERN` in `use-session.ts`.
- **`components/CommandPalette/scored-filter.ts`** — `filterCommands(commands, queryTokens)`. A structural prefix/substring matcher over `label` + `keywords` only. Match is on the **command catalog strings** (route ids, session slugs, action verbs the UI itself defines), never on user-authored memory content, so it is inherently language-agnostic.
- **`hooks/use-palette-hotkey.ts`** — `usePaletteHotkey(onToggle: () => void)`. One global `window` keydown listener (mirror the pattern in `components/Modal.tsx:17-24`) firing on `(metaKey||ctrlKey) && key === "k"`, plus `/` to open when focus is not in an input/textarea/contenteditable (guard against the `ChatInput` textarea at `screens/Cognition/ChatInput.tsx:164`). `preventDefault` only on the chord, never swallowing plain typing.
- **`components/CommandPalette/CommandPalette.test.tsx`** — colocated Vitest/RTL test (matches `src/**/*.test.tsx` glob). Asserts: ⌘K opens (`getByRole("dialog")`), typing filters by structural token, `getByRole("option")` rows, Enter on a goto-command calls `setRoute`, Enter on a session-command calls `setSessionId`, an `ep_`/`semn_` id input surfaces an "open in inspector" result, an unknown id shows the empty state, and the destructive reset command carries the `bad` tag. Live events are not needed; fetch is stubbed only for the search-mode tests (per the repo's global-`fetch` mock convention).

### Existing components / files reused (real paths)

- **`components/Rail.tsx`** — `RAIL_ITEMS` + `RouteId` are imported and iterated to build the goto-commands and reuse each route's `glyph`/`short`/`num`. The `⌘N` hint text already lives in `Rail.tsx:48` (`title={...(⌘${item.num})}`); the palette surfaces those same chords as `.kbd` pills, making the rail's existing number shortcuts discoverable rather than hidden.
- **`components/Tag.tsx`** — disclosure/trust/taint/destructive labels on results (`TagKind` = acc/warn/bad/info/purple/solid). Search results render origin/private/trust/taint via this exact primitive; the reset command renders a `bad` "destructive" tag.
- **`hooks/use-session.ts`** — `setSessionId` (already `history.replaceState`-based, `popstate`-aware, `SESSION_ID_PATTERN`-validated) is the switch-session executor. The palette adds **zero** new URL machinery; it calls the existing setter.
- **`hooks/use-api.ts`** — search-mode results use `useApi(loader, deps)` so palette searches inherit the project's race-guarded fetch + `refetch` pattern (no ad-hoc `fetch`).
- **`api/client.ts`** — bind to existing client fns only: `getMemoryBand(id, {query})`, `getStream({kinds})`, `getReviews`, `getCommitments`, `getCreatorDirectives`, `getAssembledPrompt`, `postDreamPlan`, `postCommitment`, `postAdminReset`, `getLedger`, `getWhy`.
- **`styles.css`** — reuse `.modal-backdrop`/`.modal-card`, `.list`/`.list-row`(+`.selected` glowing-left-bar), `.eyebrow`, `.kbd`, `.tag`/`.dot`, `.notice` (empty/error), `.divider` (group headers), the `box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px var(--acc-dim)` focus ring on the search input. Add only a thin `.cmdp-*` namespace (input row, group label, result meta line, footer hint) following the established per-feature prefix convention. No new tokens needed; if any are added they go in the redesign's token sweep (`--purple-dim`, tint tokens), not here.

---

## Data presentation (choice justified per proposal data-shape rules)

The palette is a **single flat, keyboard-navigable result list grouped by section** — the proposal's prescribed shape for "a set of heterogeneous, individually-actionable rows you scan and pick" (cards are for rich single objects; tables for homogeneous comparison; graph/timeline for relationships). Groups (rendered with the existing `.divider` "── label ──" rule):

1. **Go to** — 9 rows from `RAIL_ITEMS`, each `glyph · label` + `.kbd ⌘N`.
2. **Sessions** — one row per `SessionRecord`: `audience_label · conversation_kind`, a `.dot.alive` when participation is active, status as a `Tag`. Current session marked `.selected`.
3. **Search** (only present when the query is non-empty) — sub-grouped by corpus: per-band rows for the 3 searchable bands (episodic/semantic/procedural) carrying `search_score` (reuse the `scoreMeta` idiom from `Memory/index.tsx:136`), plus stream rows filtered by `kind`. Each result row renders **disclosure/trust/taint as `Tag`s at render time** — labels, never filters.
4. **Open by ID** — appears when the query parses as a known id-prefix via `objectRefFromId`; one row: "open `<type>` `<short-id>` in inspector".
5. **Actions** — create commitment, run dream plan (preview), open assembled prompt, reset demo; each row shows its endpoint as a faint `.eyebrow` meta and any side-effect `Tag` (reset → `bad` "destructive", create-commitment → `warn` "writes live").

A single result list (not tabs) is correct here: the operator is in flight-mode picking one thing fast; tabs would add a mode-switch keystroke. Section grouping gives structure without fragmenting the keyboard cursor (↑/↓ walks the flat ordered list across group boundaries).

---

## Key interactions

- **Open:** ⌘K / Ctrl+K anywhere; `/` when not focused in an input/textarea/contenteditable. Search input autofocuses.
- **Navigate:** ↑/↓ move the active row (`.list-row.selected`, glowing-left-bar); ⌘N (1-9) jumps straight to a screen even with the palette open (reusing the rail's existing number semantics); Enter runs the active command; Esc closes (mirrors `Modal` Escape handling); click also runs.
- **Modes are query-driven, not modal:** empty query → Go-to + Sessions + Actions. Non-empty alphanumeric query → adds the Search group (debounced ~180ms, matching the topology transition timing) and filters static commands by structural token. Query matching `objectRefFromId` → adds the Open-by-ID row at top.
- **goto** → `setRoute(id)` + close. **session** → `setSessionId(id)` + close (URL updates via existing setter; `App` refetches state on the resulting effect). **open-id** → `onOpenInspector(ref)` + close. **action**:
  - create commitment → opens the existing Commit create modal flow (route to `commit` and signal intent, or call `postCommitment` via a small inline form reusing `.modal-form`/`.modal-field`); disabled (greyed, `enabled:false`) when no creator entity is marked, matching the operator-session 409 guard in `App.tsx:68`.
  - run dream plan → `postDreamPlan()` (preview/dry-run, **no mutation**), then route to `dream` so the returned plan renders in the existing plan modal. Apply stays on the Dream screen (the palette never one-keystroke-applies a mutation).
  - open assembled prompt → `getAssembledPrompt()` then route to `prompts` with its preview expanded.
  - reset demo → opens the existing `ResetButton`/`Modal` confirm flow (RESET_CONFIRM_TOKEN); the palette never resets without the confirm-token modal.

---

## Cross-link pivots (Universal Inspector)

The palette is the **keyboard front door to the Universal Inspector** (proposal §4). `objectRefFromId` produces the same `ObjectRef` the inspector consumes, so "open object by ID" and every search result pivot into the one drawer with Summary/Evidence/Relationships/Timeline/Actions/Raw-JSON tabs. Pivots **out**: each result also offers "open in source screen" (goto + select), reusing the `onOpen*` callback pattern already threaded in `App.tsx` (`onOpenReview={() => setRoute("review")}`). Where the inspector ships after this piece, `onOpenInspector` is the single integration seam; until then it can fall back to source-screen navigation with the id pre-selected.

---

## Loading / empty / error / live states

- **Loading (search):** `.notice` "searching…" while the `useApi` loader is in flight; results stream in per corpus as each resolves (3 independent `useApi` calls, not one blocking join).
- **Empty:** no static match + empty search → `.notice` "no commands match". Search with zero hits → "no results in episodic/semantic/procedural or stream — unified search not yet available" (names the gap honestly). Unknown id-prefix → "no lookup for this id (`<prefix>`)".
- **Error:** an `ApiError` from any search/action loader renders inline as a `.notice.bad` row scoped to that group; other groups still work. Reuses the `ApiError` shape already parsed by `client.ts`.
- **Live:** the palette does not subscribe to frames itself (it is transient). Session rows reflect `sessionsApi.data`, which `App` already refetches on `stream:append`/`maintenance:tick`; on `borg:reset` `App` reloads the window, which unmounts the palette — no special handling needed.

---

## Migration note

**Additive, deletes nothing.** No current screen becomes the palette and no code is removed. The palette is a new overlay mounted once in `App.tsx` (sibling of `<Rail>`, inside `LiveEventsProvider`), fed the `route/setRoute/sessionId/setSessionId/sessions/audience` props that already exist there. It **reuses, does not replace**, the rail's number shortcuts (surfacing them) and the existing create-commitment / dream-plan / assembled-prompt / reset flows (it routes into them rather than reimplementing). The `correctionActionKind` id-prefix router in `Memory/index.tsx:86` should be **extracted** into the shared `object-ref.ts` so Memory and the palette share one prefix table (the global CLAUDE.md "grep before adding a helper" rule) — Memory imports it instead of keeping its private copy. Custom routing stays; no react-router (route is still `useState`, not URL-backed — consistent with the build-tooling recommendation).

---

## Scope honesty: real today vs. backend gap

**Backed by real endpoints today:** go-to-screen, switch-session, open assembled prompt (`getAssembledPrompt`), run dream plan preview (`postDreamPlan`), create commitment (`postCommitment`), reset demo (`postAdminReset`), open review/commitment/directive lists (`getReviews`/`getCommitments`/`getCreatorDirectives`), open turn ledger (`getLedger`), open-object "why" (`getWhy`), and **per-band scoped search** for episodic/semantic/procedural via `getMemoryBand(id, {query})` (the only `SEARCHABLE_BANDS`), plus stream filtering by `kind` via `getStream`.

**Genuine gaps (→ requiresBackendGap, proposal §11):** a single unified cross-corpus search endpoint (today search fans out to 3 band calls + a kind-filtered stream read; the other 5 bands, reviews, commitments, and directives have no `query` param), and a unified object-lookup-by-ID endpoint (today open-by-ID resolves only the id-prefixes whose detail/why routes already exist; there is no generic "resolve any id to its object" route). The palette degrades gracefully: it does what the catalog supports and names the gap in the empty state, rather than faking results.
**New files:** demo/web/src/components/CommandPalette/index.tsx, demo/web/src/components/CommandPalette/commands.ts, demo/web/src/components/CommandPalette/object-ref.ts, demo/web/src/components/CommandPalette/scored-filter.ts, demo/web/src/components/CommandPalette/CommandPalette.test.tsx, demo/web/src/hooks/use-palette-hotkey.ts
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/components/Rail.tsx (RAIL_ITEMS + RouteId for goto-commands and glyph/⌘N reuse), demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx (disclosure/trust/taint/destructive labels), demo/web/src/components/Modal.tsx (Escape-handling + .modal-* shell pattern), demo/web/src/hooks/use-session.ts (setSessionId executor for switch-session), demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts (search-mode data fetching), demo/web/src/api/client.ts (all endpoint calls; no ad-hoc fetch), demo/web/src/screens/Memory/index.tsx correctionActionKind (extract -> object-ref.ts shared prefix router), demo/web/src/App.tsx (mount point; route/setRoute/sessionId/setSessionId/sessions props already present), demo/web/src/styles.css (.list-row/.kbd/.eyebrow/.divider/.notice/.tag + acc-dim focus ring)
**API bindings:** GET /api/sessions, GET /api/state, GET /api/memory/bands/:id, GET /api/stream, GET /api/reviews, GET /api/commitments, GET /api/creator-directives, GET /api/prompts/assembled, POST /api/dream/plan, POST /api/commitments, POST /api/admin/reset, GET /api/turns/:id/ledger, GET /api/correction/:id/why
**Live frames:** maintenance:tick, stream:append, borg:reset
**Cross-link pivots:** palette result -> Universal Inspector (open object by id-prefix ObjectRef: ep_/semn_/seme_/turn_/rev_/cmt_/cd_/sess_/att_), palette result -> source screen (setRoute + pre-select, reusing App onOpenReview-style callbacks), open assembled prompt -> Prompts screen preview, run dream plan -> Dream screen plan modal, create commitment -> Commit screen create flow, Universal Inspector -> palette (shared object-ref.ts id-prefix table extracted from Memory correctionActionKind)
**Backend gaps needed:** Unified cross-corpus search endpoint (search memory/stream/reviews/commitments/directives in one call; today only episodic/semantic/procedural bands accept a query param and stream supports only kind-filtering), Unified object-lookup-by-ID endpoint (resolve any id to its typed object; today open-by-ID resolves only id-prefixes whose detail/why routes already exist), Per-band search for affective/self/commitments/social/relational (only episodic/semantic/procedural are in SEARCHABLE_BANDS), Stream free-text/date search filters (stream supports kind + before-cursor only, no text query)
**Scope verdict:** In-scope: this is pure operator-UI navigation/invocation presentation (discoverability of existing routes/sessions/endpoints) — it surfaces structure, applies no recall gating and no output policing; search results are recall-global with disclosure/trust/taint rendered as render-time labels, never filters.
---

## Cognitive Orrery — live interactive system map (hero visualization)
## Purpose

The Cognitive Orrery is the app's navigational hero: one live, interactive system map that renders Borg as an organism. It generalizes today's single-turn `FlowChart` (a baked-in 1200x660 phase diagram) into a layout-abstracted `<Orrery>` with a stable core (stream-spine + 15-phase pipeline) surrounded by orbital bodies (8 memory-band rings, dream satellites, governance arcs, review fault nodes, directive beacons, shared-state clouds). It is the same component at three sizes (hero / compact / mini) and four modes (Live / Replay / System-health / Provenance-flow). It is **presentation**, not cognition — Opus 5.0: in-scope, because the harness already computes `flowSnapshotByTurn`/`ledgerByTurn`/`eventTail` and exposes band/governance/dream/review state via verified endpoints, but no UI surfaces them as a unified map; this is a labeling/surfacing gap, never a recall gate.

**CARDINAL honoring.** Memory rings render *every* band's contents/counts with **disclosure labels** (origin_audience, private/operator, trust_rank, taint, citation_type) drawn at view time. Audience is a **ranking/annotation/glow axis** on a ring, never a filter that removes bodies. The orrery never hides a memory because of the current session/audience. Correction/review pivots route only to the sanctioned endpoints. No element rewrites/suppresses model output. All visual encoding keys on structure (band ids, `source_type`, phase ids, review `kind`, commitment `enforcement`, directive `status`, slot states) — never on matching words in user content (language-agnostic).

---

## Component breakdown

### New files (all under `demo/web/src/`)

- **`components/orrery/Orrery.tsx`** — top-level presentational component. Props (all data passed in, zero internal fetching, mirroring `FlowChart`'s prop-driven contract):
  - `layout: "hero" | "compact"` — picks a `LayoutSpec` (viewBox, core box, ring radii, satellite arc, beacon gutter).
  - `mode: OrreryMode = "live" | "replay" | "health" | "provenance"`.
  - Core/turn props passed straight through to the reused FlowChart renderers: `phases`, `tokenTextByPhase`, `detailByPhase`, `terminalOutcome`, `delibPath`, `finalAttempt` (from a `TurnFlowSnapshot`).
  - Orbital data props: `bands: MemoryBandSummary[]`, `dream: DreamStateResponse`, `commitments: CommitmentItem[]`, `directives: CreatorDirectiveItem[]`, `reviews: ReviewRow[]`, `sharedClouds: { audience: string; entries: SharedStateEntry[] }[]`, `semanticSpark?: SemanticGraphResponse`, `state: StateSnapshot`, `wsState: WsState`.
  - Provenance props: `activeLedger?: EvidenceLedger`, `provenanceHighlight?: { sourceType?: EvidenceLedgerSourceType; bandId?: MemoryBandId }`.
  - Interaction callbacks: `onInspect(ref: OrreryObjectRef)`, `onNavigate(target: OrreryNavTarget)`, `onSelectPhase(phaseId)`, `onHoverBody(ref | null)`.
- **`components/orrery/orrery-layout.ts`** — the layout abstraction the current geometry-by-constant code lacks. Exports `LAYOUTS: Record<"hero"|"compact", LayoutSpec>` and pure helpers: `coreBox(spec)`, `ringRadius(spec, bandIndex)` (8 concentric radii), `bandPolar(spec, bandIndex, count) -> {cx,cy,r}`, `satelliteAnchor(spec, processIndex, total)`, `arcPath(spec, fromPhaseId, toPhaseId)` (constraint arc over the generation/finalizer region), `beaconCone(spec, slotIndex)`, `cloudBlob(spec, audienceIndex)`. The hero spec uses a larger viewBox with the FlowChart pipeline scaled/inset as the core; the compact spec is ~the current 1200x660 with thin ring stubs. This is the single new piece of real geometry.
- **`components/orrery/MemoryRing.tsx`** — one of 8 concentric SVG rings. Renders ring arc, a count badge (`Spark`-style growth tick reusing `Spark`), per-band glyph, and ring-segment density proportional to `band.count` (with `+` when `count_is_lower_bound`). Disclosure-label affordance: ring tooltip lists the band's dominant labels (trust mix / private-vs-public / taint presence) when that metadata is available on the band summary; absent on summaries today (see gap), so it renders count + kind only and defers full labels to the Inspector. Click → `onNavigate({ screen:"memory", bandId })`. Hover → `onHoverBody`. The semantic ring optionally overlays a tiny `semanticSpark` constellation (reusing `ParticleField` aesthetic, not its physics).
- **`components/orrery/DreamSatellite.tsx`** — one outer satellite per offline process from `DreamStateResponse`. State machine reuses the Dream screen's two-sweep status idiom (`planning→planned→running→done|fail`) driven by `dream:process:started/completed` + `maintenance:tick` frames. Idle = faint; running = `--warn` `dreamCardPulse`/`dreamSpin` glow (existing keyframes). Click → `onNavigate({ screen:"dream", process })`.
- **`components/orrery/ConstraintArc.tsx`** — translucent governance arcs spanning the `generation_gate`→`final`→`guards` region (where commitments/directives bite). One arc layer per enforcement tier: `critical` commitments (`--bad-dim`), `advisory` commitments (`--warn-dim`), active directives (`--info-dim`). Arc thickness ∝ count. Click → `onNavigate({ screen:"commit"|"directives", audience })`.
- **`components/orrery/ReviewFault.tsx`** — amber/red fault nodes pinned to the body their `kind` affects (correction/contradiction/duplicate→semantic ring; belief_revision/identity_inconsistency→self ring; commitment_reconciliation→governance arc; creator_directive_reconciliation→directive beacon). Severity by `kind` (reconciliation/contradiction = `--bad`, else `--warn`); only NEW criticals pulse (per motion rules). Click → `onNavigate({ screen:"review", reviewId })`.
- **`components/orrery/DirectiveBeacon.tsx`** — authority beacons with scope cones; cone aperture encodes `scope` (global/audience/entity) keyed on the directive's structural scope fields + allowed/excluded entity ids, never on words. Active = lit, superseded/revoked = dim. Click → inspect directive.
- **`components/orrery/SharedStateCloud.tsx`** — per-audience translucent clouds; one blob per audience in `sharedClouds`, sized by entry count, tinted by dominant `lifecycleLabel` (reuse `lib/shared-state-lifecycle.ts` `tagKind`/`lifecycleLabel`). These are **disclosure/common-ground** clouds (what is shared *to* an audience), explicitly distinct from the global recall rings — a deliberate visual encoding of the "memory global / disclosure contextual" split. Click → `onNavigate({ screen:"directives", audience })` (shared-state lifecycle lives there today).
- **`components/orrery/MiniOrrery.tsx`** — the glyph form. A ~22px inline SVG: a core dot whose pulse = `wsState` (live→`--acc` `pulse`, reconnecting→`--warn`, down→`--bad` static), a thin ring whose lit-arc fraction = open-review pressure (`state.counts.open_reviews`), and a satellite dot lit when any dream process is running (last `maintenance:tick`/`dream:process:*`). Pure props: `{ wsState, counts, dreamActive, currentPhase? }`. No fetching. Drop into `Topbar` (`.topbar-live` slot) and screen `.page-head`s.
- **`components/orrery/orrery-objects.ts`** — `OrreryObjectRef` (discriminated union on `kind`: `band|episode|semantic_node|semantic_edge|commitment|directive|review|dream_audit|shared_state|phase|turn|attachment`) and `OrreryNavTarget` types, plus `objectRefToInspector(ref)` / `objectRefToScreen(ref)` adapters. This is the cross-link contract shared with the Universal Inspector.
- **`screens/MissionControl/OrreryHero.tsx`** — Mission-Control wrapper: the data-fetching shell that runs the `useApi` loaders + subscribes to live frames and feeds `<Orrery layout="hero">`, plus the readout strip below it (current turn id, phase, terminal outcome, final attempt, active dream process, current mood vector from `state.current_mood`) and the mode toggle (`.pill`/`.filter-pills`) + turn-replay selector.
- **`components/orrery/Orrery.test.tsx`**, **`components/orrery/MiniOrrery.test.tsx`** — colocated Vitest/RTL tests using the existing fetch-stub + `makeLiveSource()` `LiveEventsProvider` injection patterns; assert on `aria-label`/`role`/`data-testid` (e.g. `data-testid="orrery-band-episodic"`, `<output data-testid="orrery-current-phase">`) and CSS hooks.

### CSS

- **`styles.css`** — add an `.orr-*` namespace alongside the existing `.fc-*` family, reusing tokens verbatim (oklch signals, `pulse`/`dreamCardPulse`/`fc-pulse-travel`/glow vocabulary). New: `.orr-ring`, `.orr-ring-seg`, `.orr-satellite`, `.orr-arc`, `.orr-fault`, `.orr-beacon`, `.orr-cone`, `.orr-cloud`, `.mini-orrery`. Take the standardization opportunity flagged in the design map: add `--purple-dim`, per-signal tint tokens (`--acc-tint` etc.), and `--dur-fast/--dur/--dur-slow` and use them for the new classes. Keep corners sharp, borders-over-shadows, green rationed to LIVE/ACTIVE.

### Existing components/files reused or refactored (real paths)

- **`screens/Cognition/FlowChart.tsx`** (993 lines) — the core renderers are reused **verbatim** as the orrery's center: `Ganglion`, `Endpoint`, `Edge`, `ThornUp`/`ThornSidecar`, `TierHeader`, `buildPhaseRecord`, `edgeState`, `spinePath`/`forkPath`/`regenPath`/anchor helpers, the marker `<defs>`, and the full status→visual mapping (`fc-node-${status}`, S1/S2 fork lighting, regen arc, suppression thorns, `deriveActiveStreamPhase`/`ActiveStream`). **Refactor (small, behavior-preserving):** extract the baked-in constants (`PHASE_IDS`, `PHASES_LAYOUT`, `TIER_Y`, `VIEW_W/H`) into the renderers accepting a `CoreLayoutSpec` argument so `orrery-layout.ts` can position the same pipeline inside the hero viewBox. `PHASE_IDS`↔hook `PHASES` sync comment (line 88) is preserved — the orrery reuses the same 15 ids as the live-data binding keys.
- **`screens/Cognition/ParticleField.tsx`** (290 lines) — reused as a **decorative aesthetic layer** only (gravitational-lens swarm bending toward the active focal node `{x,y}`); for the orrery the focal point is the first running phase (Live) or the lensed source body (Provenance). Optionally reused at low density for the semantic-ring constellation. Carries no new data.
- **`hooks/use-turn-stream.ts`** (964 lines) — consumed, **not rewritten**. The orrery reads its already-built-but-unwired caches: `flowSnapshotByTurn` (64-turn LRU) for Replay, `ledgerByTurn` for the evidence lens, `eventTail` (60-row kind-tagged feed) for an optional orrery activity log, plus `activeTurnId`/`phases`/`terminalOutcome`/`delibPath`/`finalAttempt` for Live. The turn-arbitration machinery (`ActiveTurnDriver`, observed-turn adoption, supersession, reflect-timeout) is left untouched.
- **`screens/Cognition/Xray.tsx`** (88 lines) + **`screens/Cognition/LedgerView.tsx`** (179 lines) — `LedgerView`'s entry rendering + trust/taint `Tag` mapping is reused for the evidence-lens panel and the Inspector's Evidence tab. `Xray`'s unused `tracePlaceholder` historical-trace stub is the seam the Replay mode finally fills. In compact placement the orrery **replaces** the `Xray` flow tab while keeping its ledger tab.
- **`hooks/use-live-events.ts` / `hooks/live-context.tsx`** — the orrery subscribes via `useLiveEventsContext()` (no new WebSocket), per the test-injection contract.
- **`hooks/use-api.ts`** — all orbital data fetched via the standard `useApi(loader, deps)` hook in the wrapper screens.
- **`components/Tag.tsx`, `Panel.tsx`, `Spark.tsx`, `Empty.tsx`, `SessionsSidebar.tsx`** — Tag (signal chips), Spark (ring growth ticks), Panel (readout/lens containers), Empty (notice), and the existing selectable-row idiom for the replay turn list.
- **`screens/Memory/SemanticTopology.tsx`** — its force-graph engine is *not* embedded in the orrery (too heavy), but the semantic ring deep-links into it; `getSemanticGraph(limit)` powers the optional `semanticSpark` constellation.
- **`lib/shared-state-lifecycle.ts`** — `lifecycleLabel`/`tagKind` for cloud tinting.
- **`components/SupersededByChip.tsx`** — reused inside Inspector pivots for commitment/directive supersession chains opened from arcs/beacons.

---

## Data presentation (and why each shape)

Per the proposal's data-shape rules (graphs only where relationships matter; this is the sanctioned artistic surface):

- **Core = pipeline graph** (reuse FlowChart). Justified: the 15 phases are a fixed directed topology with forks/loops — exactly "relationships matter," and it is the one place artistic effect is sanctioned.
- **Memory bands = orbital rings** (radial small-multiples). Eight bands → eight concentric rings; ring = a count/severity small-multiple, not a table. Justified: Mission Control says "avoid giant tables, use small multiples + count badges + sparklines"; rings are the radial small-multiple. Full per-row data stays in Memory Atlas (the orrery is the index, not the table).
- **Dream = satellites** (status small-multiples on an arc). Process health is categorical state per process → status dots, matching the proposal's "small multiples / count badges."
- **Governance = constraint arcs + beacons** (graph overlay + scope cones). Commitments/directives are scope/lifecycle objects ("use matrices for scope") — but as *overlay on the core* they are arcs/cones, with the full audience×scope **matrix** living in Governance Studio (the arc/beacon deep-links there).
- **Reviews = fault nodes** (severity chips pinned to affected bodies). Severity chip + position encode kind+target; the queue table stays in Review & Repair.
- **Shared state = clouds** (per-audience translucent blobs). Disclosure axis, deliberately rendered separately from recall rings.
- **Evidence ledger = lens** (the existing `LedgerView` flat-list-per-section, reused) — but in Provenance mode the lens *also* lights the source ring per `source_type`, the cross-pane link that does not exist today.

---

## Modes

- **Live** (default): core animates the active turn from `turn:phase:*`/`turn:token`/`turn:token:flush`/`turn:terminal`/`turn:delib_path`/`turn:final_attempt`/`turn:phase:detail`; rings/satellites/arcs reflect current snapshots (refetch on `maintenance:tick`, `stream:append`). The pulse travels the spine; the evidence lens lights when `evidence_ledger:built` fires.
- **Replay-selected-turn**: a turn selector (built from `getTurns` / `flowSnapshotByTurn` keys) drives the core from `flowSnapshotByTurn.get(turnId)` and `ledgerByTurn.get(turnId)` (falling back to `getLedger(turnId)` with the existing graceful 404). Orbital bodies show their current state, dimmed, with the replayed turn's ledger sources lit. Fills the `tracePlaceholder` stub.
- **System-health**: core de-emphasized; the map foregrounds `state.counts` (turns/commitments/open_qs/open_reviews/dream_audit_rows) as ring/satellite badges, `dream.process` health, `wsState`, and `current_mood`. Severity-first coloring.
- **Provenance-flow**: select a ledger entry (or a memory body) → the lens highlights it and draws lit links from the `source_type`/`session_scope`/`via_retrieval`/`citations` back to the originating ring/spine segment, with disclosure labels (taint/trust/origin) shown on the path. This is label rendering, never filtering.

Mode toggle = existing `.filter-pills`; current mode persisted in component state (not URL, matching the route-state convention).

---

## Key interactions

- **Hover phase** → tooltip: `durationMs`, latest `detailByPhase` line, token snippet (reuses `deriveActiveStreamPhase` content).
- **Click phase** → `onInspect({kind:"phase", turnId, phaseId})` opening the Universal Inspector turn view filtered to that phase (Evidence tab scoped to that phase's ledger sections).
- **Click memory ring** → `onNavigate({screen:"memory", bandId})` (Memory Atlas for that band).
- **Click review fault** → `onNavigate({screen:"review", reviewId})`.
- **Click dream satellite** → `onNavigate({screen:"dream", process})`.
- **Click governance arc / beacon / cloud** → `onNavigate({screen:"commit"|"directives", audience})` or `onInspect({kind:"commitment"|"directive"|"shared_state", id})`.
- **Click evidence-lens entry** → cross-highlights the source ring/spine (Provenance) and `onInspect` the source object (`episode`/`semantic_node`/`commitment`/...).
- **Hover any body** → `onHoverBody` shows a compact summary card; the body's disclosure labels render here.

---

## Cross-link pivots (into / out of the Universal Inspector)

Out of the orrery, via `OrreryObjectRef`: phase→turn-inspector(phase-filtered); ring→Memory Atlas band; semantic constellation node→semantic_node inspector; fault→review row; satellite→dream process / dream_audit; arc→commitment(s); beacon→creator_directive (+ supersession chain via `SupersededByChip`); cloud→shared_state entries for that audience; lens entry→episode/semantic_node/edge/commitment/shared_state by `source_type`.

Into the orrery: the Inspector's "open in source screen" for a `turn`/`phase` returns to the orrery in Replay mode at that turn; a memory-band or semantic-node inspector can "show in orrery" to light its ring/constellation node; a review row can "show affected body" to flash its fault node. The `objectRefToInspector`/`objectRefToScreen` adapters in `orrery-objects.ts` are the shared contract so the orrery and Inspector agree on object identity (resolved by id-prefix as the screens do today: `ep_`/`semn_`/`seme_`/etc.).

---

## States: loading / empty / error / live

- **Loading**: core renders `initialPhases()` (all `queue`); rings render as faint outlines with skeleton count badges; satellites idle; `Empty` notice "awaiting live frames" only if no WS and no cached snapshot.
- **Empty** (fresh/reset): on `borg:reset` the parent reloads (existing behavior); orrery shows the core at rest, zero-count rings, no faults — a calm "at rest" map, not an error.
- **Error**: a failed `useApi` loader degrades that body to a `--bad`-tinted stub with a retry affordance (per-loader `refetch`); the core still animates from live frames independent of REST. Ledger 404 in Replay reuses LedgerView's graceful "ledger not retained pre-restart."
- **Live**: `wsState` drives the core glow and MiniOrrery: `live`→`--acc` `pulse`; `reconnecting`→`--warn`; `down`→`--bad` static. New criticals pulse once (motion rule). Token streaming, phase advance, new stream insert, dream-running, and orrery pulses are the only animated elements; dense badges do not animate.

---

## Migration notes

- **What becomes this:** the Cognition screen's right `Xray` pane (flow tab + `FlowChart`) is generalized into `<Orrery layout="compact">` for the Conversation Workbench center column; the `Xray` ledger tab is preserved (or folded into the evidence-lens panel). Mission Control gains `<Orrery layout="hero">` as its center hero. `Topbar`'s `.topbar-live` dot and each screen `.page-head` gain `<MiniOrrery>`.
- **What is reused/refactored:** FlowChart renderers reused verbatim with a small constants→`CoreLayoutSpec` extraction; `use-turn-stream` caches finally wired to UI (no hook changes); `LedgerView`, `ParticleField`, `Tag`/`Panel`/`Spark`, `shared-state-lifecycle`, `SupersededByChip` reused.
- **What is deleted:** nothing in the engine. Once Replay lands, `Xray.tsx`'s dead `tracePlaceholder` stub is removed (its purpose is now realized). The single-turn-only assumption in FlowChart's module-scope constants is removed in favor of the layout spec. No endpoints or frames removed.

---

## Constraint compliance recap

Rings/clouds/arcs **label and rank**, never filter recall: every band's bodies remain visible, audience only boosts/annotates. Disclosure labels (origin_audience, private/operator, trust, taint, citation_type) are render-time only and sourced from data the ledger already carries. No element judges or rewrites output. All encoding is structural (ids/kinds/slots/phase-ids), satisfying language-agnosticism. Correction/review actions only deep-link into the sanctioned endpoints — the orrery itself issues no mutations.
**New files:** demo/web/src/components/orrery/Orrery.tsx, demo/web/src/components/orrery/orrery-layout.ts, demo/web/src/components/orrery/orrery-objects.ts, demo/web/src/components/orrery/MemoryRing.tsx, demo/web/src/components/orrery/DreamSatellite.tsx, demo/web/src/components/orrery/ConstraintArc.tsx, demo/web/src/components/orrery/ReviewFault.tsx, demo/web/src/components/orrery/DirectiveBeacon.tsx, demo/web/src/components/orrery/SharedStateCloud.tsx, demo/web/src/components/orrery/MiniOrrery.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MissionControl/OrreryHero.tsx, demo/web/src/components/orrery/Orrery.test.tsx, demo/web/src/components/orrery/MiniOrrery.test.tsx
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/FlowChart.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/ParticleField.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/Xray.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/LedgerView.tsx, demo/web/src/hooks/use-turn-stream.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/use-live-events.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx, demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts, demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Panel.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Spark.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Empty.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SupersededByChip.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Topbar.tsx, demo/web/src/lib/shared-state-lifecycle.ts, demo/web/src/screens/Memory/SemanticTopology.tsx, demo/web/src/styles.css
**API bindings:** GET /api/state, GET /api/sessions, GET /api/stream, GET /api/turns, GET /api/turns/:id/ledger, GET /api/memory/bands, GET /api/semantic/graph, GET /api/commitments, GET /api/creator-directives, GET /api/reviews, GET /api/dream/state, GET /api/shared-state, GET /api/identity, GET /api/semantic/nodes/:id, GET /api/semantic/edges/:id, GET /api/correction/:id/why
**Live frames:** turn:phase:started, turn:phase:completed, turn:phase:failed, turn:token, turn:token:flush, turn:terminal, turn:phase:detail, turn:delib_path, turn:final_attempt, evidence_ledger:built, stream:append, dream:process:started, dream:process:completed, maintenance:tick, borg:reset
**Cross-link pivots:** phase node -> Universal Inspector (turn view, phase-filtered), memory ring -> Memory Atlas band screen, semantic constellation node -> Universal Inspector (semantic_node), review fault node -> Review & Repair row, dream satellite -> Dream Ops process / dream_audit row, governance constraint arc -> Governance Studio (commitments, scoped to audience), directive beacon -> Universal Inspector (creator_directive) + supersession chain, shared-state cloud -> Directives shared-state lifecycle (per audience), evidence-lens entry -> Universal Inspector (episode/semantic_node/edge/commitment/shared_state by source_type), Inspector 'show in orrery' (turn/band/semantic node) -> orrery Replay/light-body, attachment chip on ledger -> Universal Inspector (attachment)
**Backend gaps needed:** Universal object-lookup endpoint (resolve any id -> typed object) — none exists; the orrery/Inspector resolve by id-prefix (ep_/semn_/seme_/...) as screens do today, so cross-links carry a known kind rather than a blind id, origin_audience / private / trust / taint disclosure labels on GET /api/memory/bands summary rows — currently those labels are only on EvidenceLedger entries, so ring tooltips show count+kind and defer full disclosure labels to the Inspector (per-entry) until a band summary carries label aggregates, per-band live count-delta frame — no WS frame announces band-count changes; rings refetch GET /api/memory/bands on maintenance:tick instead of updating incrementally, live commitment/directive/review change frames — no WS frames for governance/review mutations; arcs/beacons/faults refetch on maintenance:tick rather than streaming, semantic-graph live frame — getSemanticGraph has no push frame; the semantic-ring constellation refetches on maintenance:tick
---

## Borg Operator Console — Design System & Visual Language (extends styles.css)
## Purpose

Define the canonical **design-token contract and visual-language primitives** that govern every screen of the redesigned operator console, *extending* the existing `demo/web/src/styles.css` (4077 lines, single global stylesheet, oklch tokens on `:root`) rather than replacing it. The proposal's "Style direction / Typography / Motion" section (response.md lines 1850-1903) is — verified against ground truth — a near-1:1 description of the **already-shipped** system. So this spec's job is not a new palette; it is to (a) formalize the token set as a contract, (b) close the five documented gaps (`--purple-dim`, alpha-tint tokens, motion-duration tokens, spacing scale, font-size reconciliation), (c) add a small set of new shared primitives (severity-chip, count-badge, disclosure-label, generalized sparkline, timeline, matrix, mini-orrery) that downstream screens consume, and (d) bake the CLAUDE.md cardinal rules into the visual grammar so no screen can express a recall-gate or an output-policer.

This file is **non-feature-bearing**: it ships tokens, CSS utility classes, and ~4 thin presentational components. It changes no API call, no recall path, no live-frame handling. It is purely presentation, which is squarely in-scope.

---

## Migration note (what becomes this / what is reused / what is deleted)

- **`demo/web/src/styles.css`** — extended in place. The `:root` block (lines 10-60) gains new tokens (below). No existing token is renamed or removed (renaming would break the 13 components + every screen that reads them). This is additive plus a *reconciliation pass*: stray font-size literals (`8.5/9.5/11/12/13/14/15px`, counts confirmed: 24×`11px`, 32×`12px`, 18×`14px`, etc.) and the leaked color literals (`rgba(224,160,80,…)` in `.topbar-reset`/`.prompt-card.overridden`, `#7b8a7b` select-arrow at line 1284, `oklch(0.55 0.085 305)` inlined in `.tag.purple` line 956) fold onto the token ladder. The ~repeated `oklch(<signal> / .06–.18)` tint literals (verified: `oklch(0.84 0.155 142 / 0.08)` appears 5×, the green family 17× across alphas) collapse to tint tokens.
- **`components/Tag.tsx`** — reused and extended, not replaced. `TagKind` gains `"sev-1" | "sev-2" | "sev-3" | "sev-4"`. `Tag` stays the chip primitive; severity is a styled variant, so the entire `.tag`/`.dot` CSS vocabulary (lines 922-963, 1114-1138) carries forward.
- **`components/Spark.tsx`** — reused and generalized: add optional `tone?: SignalTone` and `mode?: "bars" | "line"` props (default `bars`, current behavior). The existing `.spark`/`.spark .bar` rules (lines 2895-2907) stay; bars currently hardcode `--acc` at 0.55 opacity — this becomes `var(--spark-tone, var(--acc))` so a non-live sparkline isn't forced to read as "alive" (respects green-rationing).
- **No screen file is deleted by this piece.** This is the foundation layer the P0 "consistent status/tag system + object summary components" depend on (response.md line 1917-1918). Screen consolidation (Governance Studio, Review & Repair, Mission Control, etc.) is separate downstream work that *consumes* these tokens/components.

**New files (all under `demo/web/src/components/`, matching the named-export, prop-driven, presentational convention):**
- `SeverityChip.tsx` — 4-rank severity chip (wraps `Tag`).
- `CountBadge.tsx` — the small count badge used on rail items, summaries, headers (response.md lines 195, 458, 508).
- `DisclosureLabel.tsx` — render-time disclosure/provenance/taint/common-ground label cluster for recalled rows.
- `MiniOrrery.tsx` — the compact phase-tier glyph for topbar/screen-headers (response.md line 367).
- `Timeline.tsx` — generic horizontal SVG event/lifecycle timeline primitive.
- `Matrix.tsx` — small 2-axis dot/heat plot primitive (e.g. affective valence×arousal).
- `lib/design-tokens.ts` — TS mirror of the signal/severity enums (`SignalTone`, `SeverityRank`) so components don't restringly class names; the CSS class names remain the test contract.

---

## 1. Token contract (the canonical `:root` set)

### Reused verbatim (already shipped — do NOT reinvent)
```
Surfaces:  --bg-0 0.145 / --bg-1 0.19 / --bg-2 0.235 / --bg-3 0.29   (oklch, hue 80, chroma .006)
Lines:     --line / --line-soft / --line-ghost
Text:      --text --text-dim --text-mid --text-mute --text-faint --text-ghost  (hue 90 ramp)
Signals:   --acc/--acc-dim (phosphor green, LIVE/ACTIVE only)
           --warn/--warn-dim (amber)  --bad/--bad-dim (red)
           --info/--info-dim (blue, sparingly)  --purple
Type:      --mono (default)  --sans (prose only)
Sizes:     --fs-micro 9 / --fs-xs 10.5 / --fs-sm 11.5 / --fs-md 12.5 (body) / --fs-lg 13.5 / --fs-xl 18 / --fs-2xl 22
Layout:    --rail 60 / --topbar 44 / --statusbar 22 / --sidebar 232
Radius:    --radius 2 / --radius-md 4    Eyebrow: --eyebrow-ls 0.12em
```
**Semantic role map (governs every screen):**
- **phosphor green `--acc`** = LIVE / ACTIVE / running phase / borg-self / primary action. *Rationed* — never decorative. Used by: WS live-dot, active turn phase, running flow node, alive session dot, primary `.btn`.
- **amber `--warn`** = warning / review-pending / live-write danger zone / dream-running / aborted / regen / reconnecting.
- **red `--bad`** = critical / failure / suppression / contradiction / invalidated / error / quarantined.
- **blue `--info`** = user (vs borg) / perception / informational / "causes" edge / attachment. Restrained.
- **purple `--purple`** = dream / thought / interior marker / "prevents" edge.

### New tokens to ADD (gap-closing)
```
/* purple completion — every other signal has a -dim companion; .tag.purple already inlines this */
--purple-dim: oklch(0.55 0.085 305);

/* signal alpha-tints — replace the ~15 repeated inline oklch(<sig> / .NN) fill literals.
   two strengths cover all current usages (.06–.10 = tint, .14–.18 = tint-strong) */
--acc-tint:        oklch(0.84 0.155 142 / 0.08);
--acc-tint-strong: oklch(0.84 0.155 142 / 0.16);
--warn-tint:        oklch(0.835 0.135 85 / 0.08);
--warn-tint-strong: oklch(0.835 0.135 85 / 0.16);
--bad-tint:        oklch(0.7 0.195 26 / 0.08);
--bad-tint-strong: oklch(0.7 0.195 26 / 0.16);
--info-tint:        oklch(0.78 0.115 232 / 0.08);
--info-tint-strong: oklch(0.78 0.115 232 / 0.16);
--purple-tint:        oklch(0.74 0.135 305 / 0.08);
--purple-tint-strong: oklch(0.74 0.135 305 / 0.16);

/* severity scale — a 4-rank RANKING/LABEL axis mapped onto existing signals.
   NOT a new color: sev = how loud a review/fault row should read. */
--sev-1: var(--text-mute);   /* info / low — neutral */
--sev-2: var(--info);        /* notice */
--sev-3: var(--warn);        /* elevated / needs-attention */
--sev-4: var(--bad);         /* critical / contradiction / invalidation */

/* motion — tokenize the de-facto ladder (verified usages: 0.08s ×2, 120ms ×5, 160ms ×3, 180ms ×8, 240ms ×17) */
--dur-fast: 80ms;    /* button press, hover color */
--dur:      120ms;   /* default UI transition */
--dur-med:  180ms;   /* topology / panel */
--dur-slow: 240ms;   /* flow graph / orrery */
--ease: cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);

/* spacing scale — padding/gap are all hardcoded today; 4px base */
--sp-1: 4px; --sp-2: 8px; --sp-3: 12px; --sp-4: 16px; --sp-5: 24px; --sp-6: 32px;

/* glow — the live/active drop-shadow color, currently inline */
--glow-acc:  0 0 8px var(--acc);
--glow-warn: 0 0 8px var(--warn);
--glow-bad:  0 0 8px var(--bad);

/* type — one new hero step for screen titles / hero labels (proposal "larger sans for hero labels") */
--fs-3xl: 28px;
--line-strong: oklch(0.46 0.006 80);  /* the once-referenced fallback, made real */
```

---

## 2. Typography hierarchy

Preserve the **mono-default / `--sans`-for-prose** split (load-bearing: `body { font-family: var(--mono) }`; `--sans` only on chat `.body`, ledger `.text`, identity quotes, freeze warnings). The aggressive hierarchy the proposal wants already exists as an informal idiom; codify it as four named utility classes that every screen reuses:

- **`.eyebrow`** (micro uppercase metadata label) — `font-size: var(--fs-micro)`, `text-transform: uppercase`, `letter-spacing: var(--eyebrow-ls)`, `color: var(--text-faint)`. The dominant metadata treatment (panel headers, pills, status keys). Make it a real shared class (today it's inlined everywhere).
- **`.label-hero`** (NEW) — screen titles / hero labels: `var(--sans)`, `font-size: var(--fs-3xl)`, `font-weight: 600`, `letter-spacing: -0.01em`, `color: var(--text)`. The one place sans goes large per the proposal.
- **`.body`** — content prose: `var(--sans)`, `var(--fs-md)`, `line-height 1.55`.
- **`.tab-num`** — `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` for all counts/timestamps/durations/token-estimates. Already exists; mandate it on every numeric display.

Reconcile the stray font-size literals onto `--fs-*` during the pass: `8.5px→--fs-micro` context, `11px→--fs-sm`, `12px→--fs-md`, `14px→--fs-lg`, `15px→--fs-xl` (judgment per site).

---

## 3. Spacing / grid

- **App shell unchanged**: `.app` grid `var(--rail) 1fr` × `var(--topbar) 1fr var(--statusbar)`, `min-width 900px`, fixed viewport, panes scroll internally. `--rail/--topbar/--statusbar/--sidebar` stay.
- **Spacing scale** (`--sp-1..6`) replaces hardcoded padding/gap. Standard rhythm: panel body padding `--sp-4`, card gap `--sp-3`, inline chip gap `--sp-1`, section gap `--sp-5`.
- **Borders over shadows** — preserve. Elevation = `1px solid var(--line)` + a `--bg-N` step. Shadow reserved for `Modal` card and `--glow-*` on live elements only.
- **Sharp corners** — preserve. `--radius` (2px) for inputs/selects/dots/chips only; most surfaces have no radius. The proposal explicitly forbids "rounded SaaS blobs."
- Per-screen bespoke grids (`.cog`, `.band-detail`, `.identity` 12-col, etc.) stay the established pattern: full-height grid, children `overflow:hidden`, scroll inside.

---

## 4. Badge / chip / severity-chip system

Three tiers, all building on the existing `.tag`/`.dot` primitive (lines 922-963) so they inherit bordered + tinted-fill + signal-colored-text and pass the existing class-hook tests:

**(a) `Tag` (reused)** — the general chip. `kind ∈ acc|warn|bad|info|purple|solid`. Used for: trust state (`trustKind` maps quarantined/contested→bad, tentative/pending→warn, locked/live→acc — already in LedgerView), taint, audience, kind labels, lifecycle state. The `.tag` fill literals switch to `var(--<sig>-tint)`.

**(b) `CountBadge` (NEW, `CountBadge.tsx`)** — the small numeric badge on rail items / object summaries / message badges (response.md lines 195, 458, 508). Props `{ value: number; lowerBound?: boolean; tone?: SignalTone }`. Renders `.count-badge` with `.tab-num`; `lowerBound` appends `+` (mirrors the existing `count_is_lower_bound` "+`" idiom in BandCard). Tone defaults neutral (`--text-mute`) — green only when it represents live/active count.

**(c) `SeverityChip` (NEW, `SeverityChip.tsx`)** — `kind="sev-1..4"` variant of `Tag`. Props `{ rank: SeverityRank; label: string }`. CSS `.tag.sev-N { color: var(--sev-N); border-color: …; background: var(--<mapped>-tint) }`. **Severity is a RANKING/annotation axis derived structurally from review-row fields (`ReviewKind`, contradiction/invalidation flags, enforcement_class critical|advisory) — never from matching words in user content, and never a filter that hides rows.** It governs sort/loudness only. The redesign's review queue ranks by severity; it shows every open row.

---

## 5. Disclosure / provenance label primitive (cardinal-rule critical)

**`DisclosureLabel` (NEW, `DisclosureLabel.tsx`)** — the single shared component for rendering the disclosure/provenance/taint/common-ground axis at view time. This is the *visual enforcement* of "Memory is global to the being; disclosure is contextual to the audience."

Props (structural, language-agnostic — keys on ids/kinds/labels the backend already surfaces): `{ originAudience?: string; currentAudience?: string; disclosure?: "private" | "operator" | "public" | "unknown"; trustRank?: string | number; taint?: string; citationType?: string; viaRetrieval?: boolean; sessionScope?: "current" | "prior" | "global" }` — every field already present on `EvidenceLedgerEntry` / recalled rows (types.ts ledger block 921-982).

Renders a horizontal cluster of `Tag`s:
- **disclosure** → `private`/`operator`→`warn`, `public`→neutral, `unknown`→`bad` (fail-closed, matching the combined-label rule). Label text, not a gate.
- **provenance / origin_audience** → neutral `Tag` reading e.g. `origin: alice`. When `originAudience !== currentAudience`, add a `purple` **`cross-audience`** marker — the *visual cue for the common-ground third axis*: this memory is recallable here but is NOT established common ground for the current audience. (Per CLAUDE.md rule 6: this is a render-time label on already-surfaced `origin_audience` provenance, NOT a new known-to mechanism.)
- **trust** → `trust {rank}` `Tag` (acc/warn/bad via existing trustKind mapping).
- **taint** → `bad` `Tag` when present.
- **citation_type / via_retrieval** → neutral `Tag`.

**Hard constraints baked into this component (and the spec that governs every screen):**
1. **Labels annotate; they never filter.** No screen built on these tokens may use disclosure/audience/taint/origin as a *visibility predicate* on a recall surface. Memory Atlas, Ledger, Stream, semantic graph, identity — all render the full recalled set with `DisclosureLabel`s attached. Current-audience may only *rank/boost* ordering (a sort hint), never remove a row.
2. **No output-policing affordance.** Components here expose inspect / correct / forget / invalidate / supersede / dismiss only through the sanctioned endpoints. There is no "rewrite/suppress the entity's emitted text" control in the vocabulary.
3. **Fail-closed combining.** When a row carries mixed/unknown disclosure, `DisclosureLabel` renders `unknown` (red), never silently demoting to `public`.

---

## 6. Data-viz primitives

Per the proposal's data-shape rule (cards/tables/graph/timeline/matrix — "use the right shape"), four primitives, each reusing the existing visual grammar:

- **Sparkline — `Spark` (generalized).** `mode: "bars" | "line"`, `tone: SignalTone`. Bars = current behavior (growth counts on `BandCard`). Line mode (NEW) = trend over time where a continuous read matters (e.g. open-question urgency, goal progress). Tone-tokenized so non-live sparklines aren't forced green. Justification: sparklines for *at-a-glance trend in a dense row/card* (Mission Control small-multiples, response.md lines 457-461) where a full chart would dominate.
- **Timeline — `Timeline.tsx` (NEW).** Horizontal SVG lane: dots/segments on a time axis, each carrying a kind→signal color and an optional `DisclosureLabel` on hover. Reuses the `formatTime` helper (stream-utils) and the `.list-row` selection idiom. Used for: commitment lifecycle, open-question events (generalizes the existing `OpenQuestionEventsSection` — the richest provenance timeline today), growth-marker trajectory, dream audit runs, affective mood-over-time. Justification: lifecycle/provenance data is inherently chronological; the current flat lists lose the time axis (a named gap in Identity/Commit/affective).
- **Matrix — `Matrix.tsx` (NEW).** Small 2-axis dot/heat plot. Primary use: **affective valence×arousal** (the named Memory-Atlas gap — mood is currently a flat point list; the timestamped valence/arousal/trigger data is plot-ready). Dots colored by recency/trigger, sized by salience; selection bound like the semantic graph. Justification: a 2-D field reveals mood structure a list cannot.
- **Graph — reuse `SemanticTopology.tsx` as-is.** It's a self-contained, prop-driven force-graph (`graph/selectedId/onSelectNode`) with the full edge grammar (is_a/part_of/causes/prevents/supports/contradicts/related_to/instance_of → dash + signal color). Social/relational bands adopt it with a different node/edge adapter. No new graph engine.

**Edge/relationship color grammar (preserve, it's a real visual language):** supports=green dashed, contradicts=red, causes=blue, prevents=purple, is_a=faint dotted, part_of=amber, instance_of=acc-dim. Tokenized via the `-dim` companions.

---

## 7. Mini-orrery glyph

**`MiniOrrery.tsx` (NEW)** — the compact status glyph for the topbar and screen headers (response.md line 367; "mini-orrery glyphs as status indicators"). A small (e.g. 20×20) SVG of the 4 phase-tiers as concentric/linear ganglia, lit by live turn state. Props `{ phases?: PhaseState[]; wsState: WsState; outcome?: TurnTerminalOutcome }` — consumes the *same* `use-turn-stream` data the full FlowChart does, so it's a pure visual reduction, no new data path. States: idle (faint), running (one tier pulsing green), suppressed (amber/red thorn), down (grey). This is the reusable *aesthetic* reduction of the orrery; the full Cognitive Orrery is downstream Mission-Control work that this glyph + tokens + motion vocabulary feed into. Reuses `Ganglion`/pulse styling from `FlowChart`'s `fc-*` classes.

---

## 8. Motion rules (state-indicating only)

Motion is already a codified subsystem; this formalizes it and tokenizes timings. **The rule: animation indicates state, never decorates.** Allowed (each mapped to an existing or token-driven mechanism):

| Signal | Mechanism | Token |
|---|---|---|
| WS live pulse | `@keyframes pulse` (1.4s opacity breathe) on `.live-dot` + `--glow-acc` | reuse |
| active turn phase | `fc-halo-pulse` + `fc-pulse-ring` on running ganglion | reuse |
| token stream | `ActiveStream` footer text append (no extra anim) | reuse |
| new stream/feed entry insertion | a single `--dur-med` fade/slide-in on insert (NEW, one-shot — `eventTail` `isNew` flash already flags it) | `--dur-med` |
| dream process running | `dreamCardPulse` glow breathe on running `DreamCard` | reuse |
| orrery pulse | traveling `fc-pulse-travel` particles on active edges | reuse |
| graph settle | `SemanticTopology` physics settle (~4.2s then idle wobble) | reuse |
| new-critical-review pulse | `--glow-bad` + `pulse` on `SeverityChip[rank=4]` rows, **fires once on arrival, then stops** | `--dur-slow` |

**Forbidden (proposal + CLAUDE.md aesthetic):** constant blink everywhere, animated backgrounds behind dense data, scroll-jacking, transitions that slow inspection. The severity pulse is one-shot on the *new* critical row only — not a perpetual blink. Standard UI transitions use `--dur`/`--ease`; reduce-motion respected via `@media (prefers-reduced-motion)` disabling pulse/glow keyframes (NEW, currently absent).

---

## 9. Cross-link pivots (into/out of the Universal Inspector)

These primitives are the connective tissue for the planned Universal Inspector / cross-linked provenance. Each carries a stable, structural id (never a word-match):
- **`DisclosureLabel` / `Tag` / `CountBadge`** on any object summary expose the object's id+kind → click pivots into the inspector for that id (`getWhy(id)` provenance, plus `getSemanticNode/Edge`, `getLedger`, `getAttachmentMetadata` as appropriate).
- **`SeverityChip`** on a review row pivots to the affected object (the existing `SupersededByChip` jump-to-entity primitive is reused for the lineage hop, not reinvented).
- **`Timeline`** dots pivot to the source stream entry id / event id.
- **`MiniOrrery`** click pivots to the turn inspector for the active turn.
- The inspector→out direction: a `source_stream_id` / `turn_id` / `semn_`/`seme_`/`ep_` id rendered anywhere becomes a navigable chip (closing the "provenance ids are plain `[id]` text" gap), routing via the existing custom-route + callback pattern.

---

## 10. States: loading / empty / error / live

A consistent state vocabulary every screen inherits:
- **Loading** — reuse the `useApi` `{loading}` flag; show a `.notice`-style skeleton or the existing `dreamSpin` loader for in-flight ops. No layout shift (fixed grid).
- **Empty** — reuse `Empty`/`.notice` (30px-padded centered mono mute). Each primitive (`Timeline`, `Matrix`, `Spark`) renders its own neutral empty state, never a green/alive one.
- **Error** — `ApiError` (status+payload) → an inline `bad`-toned `.notice` with the message; the 404 "ledger not retained pre-restart" graceful-degrade pattern (LedgerView) is the model. Never a blocking modal for a read failure.
- **Live** — WS `wsState ∈ live|reconnecting|down` drives the `--glow-acc`/`--warn`/grey treatment on `.live-dot` and `MiniOrrery`; `reconnecting`→amber, `down`→grey-no-pulse. `borg:reset` frame → screens clear and re-fetch (App already does `window.location.reload()`). Maintenance/stream frames keep counts/badges current via the existing refetch-on-tick pattern.

---

## Opus 5.0 / cardinal-rule self-check
- **In-scope:** pure presentation — tokens, utility classes, presentational components. No recall path, no model output touched. The Opus 5.0 test: a 10× smarter model still needs a consistent, legible token system and disclosure *labels* surfaced — this is harness presentation, not model judgment.
- **No recall-gating:** `DisclosureLabel` and `SeverityChip` are label/rank axes only; the spec explicitly forbids any screen using them as visibility predicates on a recall surface. Current-audience is a sort boost at most.
- **No output-policing:** the component vocabulary exposes only sanctioned correction/review affordances; no rewrite/suppress control exists.
- **Language-agnostic:** every primitive keys on ids/kinds/roles/slots/enums (severity from `ReviewKind`/enforcement_class, disclosure from structural fields), never on matching words in user content.
- **Common-ground third axis:** surfaced as the `cross-audience` purple marker driven by existing `origin_audience` provenance — a label, not a new tracking mechanism.
**New files:** demo/web/src/components/SeverityChip.tsx, demo/web/src/components/CountBadge.tsx, demo/web/src/components/DisclosureLabel.tsx, demo/web/src/components/MiniOrrery.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Timeline.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Matrix.tsx, demo/web/src/lib/design-tokens.ts
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/styles.css (:root token block, .tag/.dot/.spark/.btn/.tbl/.eyebrow/.notice, @keyframes pulse + fc-* motion), demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx (TagKind extended with sev-1..4), demo/web/src/components/Spark.tsx (generalized: tone + mode props), demo/web/src/components/Panel.tsx, demo/web/src/components/StatusBar.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Topbar.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Rail.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Modal.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Empty.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SupersededByChip.tsx (lineage cross-link pivot), demo/web/src/screens/Memory/SemanticTopology.tsx (reused as-is for social/relational graphs), demo/web/src/components/SemanticNodeDetail.tsx, demo/web/src/components/WhyDrawer.tsx (universal why/provenance), demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/FlowChart.tsx (Ganglion/fc-* vocabulary feeding MiniOrrery), demo/web/src/lib/stream-utils.ts (formatTime for Timeline)
**API bindings:** GET /api/state, GET /api/memory/bands, GET /api/memory/bands/:id, GET /api/semantic/graph, GET /api/turns/:id/ledger, GET /api/reviews, GET /api/correction/:id/why, GET /api/identity, GET /api/commitments, GET /api/creator-directives, GET /api/dream/state, GET /api/dream/audit, GET /api/shared-state, GET /api/stream, GET /api/attachments/:id
**Live frames:** turn:phase:started, turn:phase:completed, turn:phase:failed, turn:token, turn:token:flush, turn:terminal, stream:append, dream:process:started, dream:process:completed, maintenance:tick, borg:reset
**Cross-link pivots:** object id+kind chip -> Universal Inspector (getWhy + getSemanticNode/Edge + getLedger), SeverityChip on review row -> affected object via SupersededByChip lineage hop, Timeline dot -> source stream entry / event id, MiniOrrery -> active turn inspector, DisclosureLabel cross-audience marker -> origin_audience provenance (common-ground cue, not a gate), stream/turn/semn/seme/ep id rendered anywhere -> navigable inspector chip
**Scope verdict:** In-scope: pure UI presentation (design tokens + presentational primitives); touches no recall path and no model output. Disclosure/severity are render-time labels and ranking axes only — no recall-gating, no output-policing.
---

## Cross-Linking Model & Client Object Registry (Universal Inspector spine)
# Cross-Linking Model & Client Object Registry

## Purpose

Today every screen invents its own detail panel and its own notion of "the selected thing." Stream renders a provenance block, Memory has `MemoryDrill` + `WhyDrawer`, Commit/Directives/Review each re-implement a master/detail pane, and the only cross-object navigation primitive is `SupersededByChip` (used in 3 places for supersession jumps). IDs that appear in payloads (`source_stream_ids`, `evidence_episode_ids`, `from_node_id`, `subject_entity_id`, audit `targets`, review `refs`...) are rendered as **plain non-clickable text**.

This piece builds the connective tissue the proposal (section 8 + "Universal inspector drawer") calls for: a **typed client object registry** that maps `{type,id}` to a fetcher, a label, a pivot set, and an inspector renderer; an **`<IdRef>` chip** that makes any id in any payload clickable by resolving its type structurally; and a **`UniversalInspector`** drawer that any screen opens with `openObject({type,id})`. Every section-8 pivot (chat message <-> stream/turn/ledger/attachments/episodes/reviews; episode <-> stream/semantic/autobiography/correction; semantic node <-> episodes/edges/reviews/correction; commitment <-> stream/family/supersession/reviews; directive <-> authorization/content-source/subject/supersession/reconciliation; dream audit <-> process/targets/reversal/revert) is expressed as registry data, not bespoke per-screen wiring.

### Cardinal-rule posture (Opus 5.0)
This is pure UI presentation + navigation. It does **not** gate recall: audience/disclosure/taint remain render-time **labels and ranking boosts** on rows the entity already recalled; the inspector shows everything, labeled. It does **not** police output: the only mutating pivots are the already-sanctioned `correct`/`forget`/`invalidate`/`revoke`/`supersede`/`revert`/review-resolution endpoints. Type detection keys on **structural id prefixes and schema fields**, never on matching words in user content -- fully language-agnostic. Opus 5.0 verdict: in-scope (presentation/navigation gap; harness wasn't surfacing the provenance graph the data already encodes).

---

## The canonical ID taxonomy (ground truth)

The backend already mints branded ids with stable prefixes (`src/util/ids.ts`, `createIdHelpers`). The registry keys on exactly these:

| prefix | object type | single-fetch endpoint today? |
|---|---|---|
| `strm_` | stream_entry | NO (only via `/api/stream` window) |
| `sess_` (+ literal `default`) | session | via `/api/sessions` list |
| `ep_` | episode | NO (via episodic band/search) |
| `semn_` | semantic_node | **YES** `GET /api/semantic/nodes/:id` |
| `seme_` | semantic_edge | **YES** `GET /api/semantic/edges/:id` |
| `cmt_` | commitment | NO (via `/api/commitments` list) |
| `cdir_` | creator_directive | NO (via `/api/creator-directives` list) |
| `ent_` | entity | NO (creator only via `/api/entities/creator`) |
| `act_` | action_record | NO |
| `rslot_` | relational_slot | NO (via relational band) |
| `dart_` | shared_state_entry | NO (via `/api/shared-state?audience=`) |
| `goal_` `val_` `trt_` `oq_` `grw_` `abp_` | identity rows | NO (bundled in `GET /api/identity`) |
| `att_` | attachment | **YES** `GET /api/attachments/:id` |
| `imgp_` | image_perception | via attachment metadata |
| numeric (`123`) | dream audit row / review row / mood entry | via list endpoints |

> Note: the proposal mentions `cd_` for directives; the **real** prefix is `cdir_`. The 8 *correctable* types are exactly `correctionTargetIdKindDescriptors`: `episode, semantic_node, semantic_edge, value, goal, trait, commitment, open_question` -- this is broader than Memory's current `ep_/semn_/seme_`-only `correctionActionKind` router, and the registry should expose `why/forget/correct` for all 8.

`resolveObjectType(id: string): ObjectType | null` is a pure prefix match (numeric ids resolve only with a hint, since `123` could be audit/review/mood -- see Detection below).

---

## Component breakdown

### NEW files

**`demo/web/src/lib/object-registry.ts`** -- the registry itself. No JSX; pure data + functions.
- `type ObjectType = "stream_entry" | "session" | "episode" | "semantic_node" | "semantic_edge" | "commitment" | "creator_directive" | "entity" | "action_record" | "relational_slot" | "shared_state" | "goal" | "value" | "trait" | "open_question" | "growth_marker" | "autobiographical_period" | "attachment" | "turn" | "review" | "dream_audit" | "mood";`
- `const ID_PREFIXES: ReadonlyArray<{ prefix: string; type: ObjectType }>` -- mirrors `src/util/ids.ts` (single source of truth; a unit test asserts parity against the backend prefixes so it can't silently drift).
- `resolveObjectType(id): ObjectType | null` (prefix scan; longest-prefix-first so `seme_` beats a hypothetical `se`).
- `type ObjectRef = { type: ObjectType; id: string; hint?: { sessionId?: string; turnId?: string; audience?: string } }` -- `hint` carries the context an id needs when no by-id endpoint exists (e.g. which session window to scan for a `strm_`, which audience for a `dart_`).
- `type RegistryEntry = { type; label(obj): string; subtitle?(obj): string; fetch(ref, deps): Promise<ResolvedObject | null>; pivots(obj): Pivot[]; renderSummary(obj): ReactNode; actions(obj): InspectorAction[] }` -- but to keep this file JSX-free, `renderSummary`/`actions` live in the renderer module below and the registry only holds `label/fetch/pivots/capabilities`.
- `type Pivot = { rel: string; label: string; targets: ObjectRef[] | { lazy: true; via: PivotResolver } }` where `rel` is a stable structural key (e.g. `"source_stream"`, `"derived_episodes"`, `"supersession_chain"`, `"directive_family"`). Pivots are computed from fields already on the typed row -- no inference from text.
- `type Reliability = "direct" | "in_list" | "needs_backend"` per pivot, so the UI can render a pivot as a live link, a "scan loaded window" link, or a disabled chip with a "needs related-object endpoint" tooltip.

**`demo/web/src/components/IdRef.tsx`** -- generalizes `SupersededByChip` (same button/`btn sm`/`stopPropagation` shape, same a11y contract). Props `{ id, type?, label?, hint?, onOpen }`. If `type` omitted, calls `resolveObjectType(id)`; renders the short id + a one-glyph type sigil reusing the Rail glyph vocabulary (e.g. `◇` semantic, `≣` episode/stream, `§` commitment/directive, `☾` dream). Unresolvable ids render as a dim `.tag` (non-clickable), never an error. `SupersededByChip` becomes a thin wrapper (`<IdRef active>` styling) so existing call sites in Commit/Directives/Review keep working.

**`demo/web/src/components/UniversalInspector.tsx`** -- the right-side drawer the proposal calls "the most important UX primitive." Six tabs (Summary / Evidence / Relationships / Timeline / Actions / Raw JSON). Reuses `Panel`, `Tag`, `Modal` (for action confirms), `WhyDrawer` content (folded into the Evidence tab), `SemanticNodeDetail`/`SemanticEdgeDetail`, `LedgerView`, `AttachmentChip`. It does NOT fetch directly: it consumes the registry's `fetch` and renders `renderSummary` + pivot chips (`<IdRef>`) + actions.

**`demo/web/src/hooks/inspector-context.tsx`** -- mirrors the existing `live-context.tsx` pattern exactly: `<InspectorProvider>` (mounted in `App.tsx` beside `LiveEventsProvider`) holds a navigation **stack** of `ObjectRef` (so pivoting A->B->C and "back" works), plus `openObject(ref)`, `back()`, `close()`. Read via `useInspector()` (throws if absent, like `useLiveEventsContext`). The stack is the cross-link spine: every pivot is `openObject(targetRef)`, every "open in source screen" calls a passed-in `setRoute` + selection callback.

**`demo/web/src/lib/object-renderers.tsx`** -- per-type `renderSummary`/`actions`/`renderTimeline`, kept out of the registry data file to preserve the "registry is data" separation. One small component per type, modeled on `SemanticNodeDetail`.

**`demo/web/src/lib/object-registry.test.tsx`** + **`UniversalInspector.test.tsx`** -- colocated, fetch-stubbed + `LiveEventsProvider`/`InspectorProvider`-injected per the existing test conventions; assert prefix->type parity, pivot derivation from fixture rows, and `aria-label`/`role`/`data-testid` hooks.

### EXISTING components reused / refactored
- `demo/web/src/components/SupersededByChip.tsx` -> becomes a wrapper over `IdRef` (or is deleted and its 3 call sites import `IdRef`). The button class contract (`btn sm superseded-by-chip`) is preserved for test stability.
- `demo/web/src/components/WhyDrawer.tsx` -> its `getWhy(id)` rendering moves into the inspector's **Evidence** tab; `WhyDrawer` is retained as the renderer the tab embeds.
- `demo/web/src/components/SemanticNodeDetail.tsx` / `SemanticEdgeDetail` -> the `renderSummary` for `semantic_node`/`semantic_edge`.
- `demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/LedgerView.tsx` -> reused for the `turn` type's Evidence tab (it already lazily `getLedger(turnId)` with a graceful 404, and consumes the live `ledgerByTurn` cache).
- `demo/web/src/components/AttachmentChip.tsx` / `ImagePlaceholder.tsx` -> `attachment` type renderer.
- `demo/web/src/lib/shared-state-lifecycle.ts` -> `shared_state` type label/tag (`lifecycleLabel`/`tagKind`).
- `demo/web/src/api/client.ts` typed functions -> the registry's `fetch` implementations call these verbatim (`getSemanticNode`, `getSemanticEdge`, `getAttachmentMetadata`, `getLedger`, `getCommitments`, `getCreatorDirectives`, `getIdentity`, `getSharedState`, `getMemoryBand`, `getStream`, `getReviews`, `getDreamAudit`, `getWhy`).

---

## Data presentation (per the proposal's data-shape rules)

The inspector is a **tabbed card/key-value drawer**, not a graph -- because a single object's identity is key/value-shaped and the existing `.props`/`.why-*` vocabulary already fits. The cross-link surface within it is presented per data-shape:

- **Relationships tab = chip matrix grouped by `rel`.** Each pivot group renders an eyebrow label + a row of `<IdRef>` chips (`.filter-pills`-like flow). Counts use `.tab-num`. This is the right shape because pivots are sets of typed references, not a tree or a force graph -- the matrix makes "what is this connected to, and how" scannable in one view. (Semantic node/edge relationships additionally offer an "open in topology" pivot that hands off to the existing `SemanticTopology` force-graph rather than duplicating it.)
- **Timeline tab = vertical event list** reusing the `OpenQuestionEventsSection` pattern (the richest existing provenance timeline) for objects that carry temporal lineage (episode `lineage`, commitment supersession, directive supersession, dream audit `applied_at`/`reverted_at`). Chronological list is correct here (single-object history is 1-D).
- **Evidence tab = `WhyDrawer` content + ledger-style provenance rows** (reusing `LedgerView`'s trust/taint tagging) showing `source_*_ids` as `<IdRef>` chips with disclosure labels (`origin_audience`, `private`/`operator`, `trust_rank`, `taint`, `citation_type`) rendered beside each -- **labels, not filters**.
- **Summary tab = `.props` key/value** + the type's signal `Tag`s (state/status/enforcement/kind).
- **Raw JSON tab** = collapsible `<details>` (the existing `WhyDrawer` raw idiom).

Within source screens, the change is minimal-surface: every place that today prints an id as text (`source_stream_ids.map(id => <span>[id]</span>)` in Memory line 1433, Commit's `source_stream_entry_ids`, Stream's provenance block, Directives' correlation, Review's `node_ids`/`directive_ids`, Dream's audit `targets`) renders `<IdRef>` instead. That is the "IDs found in payloads become clickable" mechanism: a payload-walking helper `idRefsFromFields(obj, FIELD_SPECS)` reads the **known structural id-bearing fields** (a static per-type list, e.g. episode->`[source_stream_ids, lineage.derived_from, lineage.supersedes]`) and emits typed chips. It does NOT regex arbitrary strings for id-shaped tokens in free text (that would be a language/scan hazard and could misfire on user content) -- it keys on schema field names.

---

## Section-8 pivots, grounded with reliability

For each, the pivot's `targets` come from a **named field on the typed row**, and reliability is one of `direct` (single targeted fetch exists), `in_list` (resolvable only if the relevant list/window is loaded; no by-id endpoint), or `needs_backend`.

**Chat message / stream entry** (`StreamEntry`):
- open turn -> `turn_id` (`turn`) -- ledger is `direct` via `getLedger`; the turn *flow* is `in_list` (only in `flowSnapshotByTurn`/`ledgerByTurn` live cache, not persisted -- proposal gap "historical traces").
- open evidence ledger -> `turn_id` -> `getLedger` **direct**.
- open attachments -> for `user_image_attachment`, `att_` id in content -> `getAttachmentMetadata` **direct**.
- open derived episodes -> **needs_backend** (no stream-entry -> episode reverse index; would scan episodic band's `source_stream_ids` -> `in_list` only if band loaded).
- open related reviews -> **needs_backend** (reviews are keyed by object ids in `refs`, not by stream entry; no reverse lookup).
- sender/reply-target entity -> `sender_entity_id`/`reply_target_entity_id` (`entity`) -- **needs_backend** for full entity fetch (only creator has a GET).

**Episode** (`EpisodeMemoryItem`):
- open source stream entries -> `source_stream_ids` (`stream_entry`) -- **in_list** (no by-id stream fetch; `/api/stream` filters by session/kind/audience/cursor only). Chip opens the entry if it's in the loaded window, else shows "load stream window" affordance.
- open semantic nodes/edges derived from it -> **needs_backend** (no episode->semantic reverse index; only the inverse `semn_.source_episode_ids` exists). Forward pivots from semantic nodes are reliable; this reverse one is a gap.
- open autobiographical period -> **needs_backend** (episode carries no `period_id`).
- correct / forget / why -> **direct** (episode is a correctable type).

**Semantic node** (`SemanticMemoryNode`):
- open evidence episodes -> `source_episode_ids` (`episode`) -- **in_list**.
- open supporting/contradicting edges -> **needs_backend** (node has no edge list; edges reference nodes via `from/to_node_id`, reverse needs scan -- partially `in_list` from the loaded graph snapshot).
- open reviews -> **needs_backend**.
- correct / forget / why / invalidate (edges) -> **direct** (all 3 semantic types correctable; edge `invalidate` via `postSemanticEdgeInvalidate`).
- node itself & single edge -> **direct** (`getSemanticNode`/`getSemanticEdge`).

**Semantic edge** (`SemanticMemoryEdge`): `from_node_id`/`to_node_id` -> nodes **direct**; `evidence_episode_ids` -> **in_list**; `invalidated_by_edge_id` -> **direct**; `invalidated_by_review_id` -> review (numeric) **in_list**.

**Commitment** (`CommitmentItem`):
- open source stream entries -> `source_stream_entry_ids` **in_list**.
- open directive family -> `directive_family` (string, not an id) -> filters the `/api/commitments` list **in_list/direct** (the Commit screen already groups by family).
- open supersession chain -> `superseded_by_id` walked across the loaded commitments list **in_list** (Commit already computes this root/chain).
- open related reviews -> commitment_reconciliation rows reference it in `refs` -> **needs_backend** (no reverse index; can scan loaded reviews `in_list`).
- revoke -> `postCommitmentRevoke` **direct**; correct -> **direct**.
- `canonicalized_by_artifact_entry_id` -> shared_state (`dart_`) **in_list**.

**Creator directive** (`CreatorDirectiveItem`):
- open authorization stream entries -> `authorization_stream_entry_ids` **in_list**.
- open content source entries -> `content_source_stream_entry_ids` **in_list**.
- open subject entity -> `subject_entity_id` (+ `subject_entity_name` label) **needs_backend** for fetch.
- open supersession chain -> `superseded_by_id` over loaded directives **in_list** (Directives screen already does this).
- open reconciliation review -> creator_directive_reconciliation rows reference `directive_ids` in `refs` -> **in_list** (scan loaded reviews) with resolution via `resolveCreatorDirectiveReconciliation` **direct**.
- revoke / supersede -> **direct**.

**Dream audit row** (`MaintenanceAuditRow`):
- open process -> `process` (name string) -> hands off to Dream screen process tile **direct**.
- open target objects -> `targets` (freeform `Record`); `idRefsFromFields` walks it for id-prefixed values -> typed chips, each `in_list`/`direct` per target type.
