- open reversal payload -> `reversal` rendered inline (Dream already has the rich payload renderer) **direct**.
- revert -> `revertDreamAudit` **direct** (gated by reversal presence + not already reverted).
- open related stream dream report -> **needs_backend** (no audit->dream_report stream-entry link; `in_list` scan of `dream_report` kind).

---

## Key interactions

- **Click any `<IdRef>` anywhere** -> `openObject(ref)` pushes onto the inspector stack and opens/raises the drawer.
- **Pivot within the inspector** -> chips in Relationships/Evidence call `openObject`, deepening the stack; a breadcrumb (reusing `.topbar-crumb` styling) shows the path with `back()`.
- **"Open in source screen"** (Actions tab, always present) -> calls the host `setRoute(routeForType(type))` + a selection callback so the full screen (e.g. Memory drill, Commit detail) focuses that object. This is the bridge between the lightweight drawer and the rich per-screen views, not a replacement for them.
- **Copy ID** / **Run why?** (correctable types only) / **Correct / Forget / Invalidate / Revoke / Supersede / Revert** (capability-gated by `actions(obj)`) -> the existing modal+confirm flows, reused verbatim, then `refetch` of the affected list.
- **Live update**: the drawer subscribes via `useLiveEventsContext()` to `maintenance:tick` / `stream:append` / `borg:reset` and refetches the open object (or closes it on `borg:reset`), matching the Review/Dream refetch-on-tick pattern.

## States
- **Loading**: skeleton `.props` rows + a `dot.alive` pulse; tabs render lazily on first open.
- **Empty pivots**: a pivot group with zero targets renders a dim "none" `.notice`, never hidden (so absence is legible).
- **`in_list` not loaded**: chip is enabled but resolving it when the backing list isn't loaded shows a one-line "open <screen> to load" affordance instead of a dead click.
- **`needs_backend`**: chip rendered disabled/dim with a tooltip naming the gap ("requires related-object endpoint"); count still shown if the field carries ids, so the operator sees the relationship exists even if not yet navigable.
- **Error**: failed fetch shows the `ApiError` message inline (reusing the client's `{error:{status,message}}` shape) with a retry; a 404 on a `direct` fetch (e.g. ledger evicted post-restart) reuses LedgerView's graceful "not retained" copy.
- **Unresolvable id**: rendered as plain dim text, never throws.

---

## Migration notes

- **What this replaces**: the per-screen ad-hoc detail panes converge on one inspector. Concretely -- Stream's read-only provenance pane, Memory's `WhyDrawer` invocation + properties sidebar's correction buttons, Commit/Directives detail panels' lineage chips, Review's pair drill-through chrome, and Dream's audit-target text all route through `UniversalInspector` + `objectRegistry`. Screens keep their **list/table/graph/timeline** views (those are not duplicated) and lose only their bespoke "selected object" detail panel, emitting `openObject` instead.
- **`SupersededByChip` is absorbed** by `IdRef` (wrapper kept for class/test stability, or call sites migrated).
- **`correctionActionKind` (Memory) is superseded** by the registry's capability table, which correctly exposes correction for all 8 `correctionTargetIdKindDescriptors` types instead of the current `ep_/semn_/seme_`-only subset -- closing the noted gap where procedural/affective/social rows had no correction path. (Still only the 8 the *backend* supports; no new correction surface invented.)
- **Nothing is deleted from the API client or types**; the registry is additive and reuses existing typed functions.
- **No router change**: cross-screen handoff uses the existing `setRoute` prop-drill; the inspector stack is in-memory (not URL), consistent with the app's "screen isn't in the URL" stance. (Deep-linking an object by id is a possible follow-up via the same `useSession`-style `?object=` pattern, but is not required and is out of scope here.)
- **Tests**: prefix->type parity test guards drift from `src/util/ids.ts`; per-pivot tests assert derivation from fixture rows; a11y/class hooks preserved for the existing suite.

---

## What's reliable today vs. needs the backend gaps

**Reliable now (`direct`)**: semantic node by id, semantic edge by id, attachment by id, turn evidence ledger by turn id; `why`/`correct`/`forget` for episode/semantic_node/semantic_edge/value/goal/trait/commitment/open_question; edge `invalidate`; commitment `revoke`, directive `revoke`/`supersede`, reconciliation resolution, dream audit `revert`; supersession-chain and directive-family pivots (resolved within already-loaded Commit/Directives lists).

**Reliable but list-scoped (`in_list`)**: episodes, commitments, directives, identity rows, shared-state, relational slots, mood, reviews, and `strm_` source-entry pivots -- navigable only when the backing list/window/graph is already loaded, because no by-id endpoint exists for these and `/api/stream` cannot fetch by entry-id or turn-id.

**Genuine backend gaps** (everything else: reverse indices like episode->semantic, object->reviews, stream-entry->episode; entity fetch by id; unified/related-object lookup) routes to `requiresBackendGap` below. The inspector renders these pivots as visible-but-disabled so the relationship is legible even before the endpoints exist.
**API bindings:** GET /api/semantic/nodes/:id, GET /api/semantic/edges/:id, GET /api/attachments/:id, GET /api/attachments?ids=, GET /api/turns/:id/ledger, GET /api/correction/:id/why, POST /api/correction/:id/forget, POST /api/correction/:id/correct, POST /api/correction/semantic-edges/:id/invalidate, GET /api/commitments, POST /api/commitments/:id/revoke, GET /api/creator-directives, POST /api/creator-directives/:id/revoke, POST /api/creator-directives/:id/supersede, POST /api/reviews/:id/creator-directive-reconciliation, GET /api/reviews, PATCH /api/reviews/:id, GET /api/correction/reviews, PATCH /api/correction/reviews/:id, GET /api/identity, GET /api/shared-state, GET /api/memory/bands/:id, GET /api/stream, GET /api/dream/audit, POST /api/dream/audit/:id/revert, GET /api/sessions, GET /api/entities/creator
**Live frames:** maintenance:tick, stream:append, borg:reset, evidence_ledger:built
**Cross-link pivots:** chat_message -> turn (turn_id), chat_message -> evidence_ledger (turn_id, direct via getLedger), chat_message -> attachment (att_ in content, direct), stream_entry -> sender/reply-target entity (ent_, needs entity-by-id), episode -> source stream entries (source_stream_ids, in_list), episode -> derived semantic nodes (needs reverse index), episode -> autobiographical period (needs period_id), episode -> correct/forget/why (direct), semantic_node -> evidence episodes (source_episode_ids, in_list), semantic_node -> edges (needs reverse / loaded-graph in_list), semantic_node -> reviews (needs reverse index), semantic_node -> correct/forget/why (direct), semantic_edge -> from/to nodes (direct), semantic_edge -> evidence episodes (in_list), semantic_edge -> invalidating edge/review (direct/in_list), semantic_edge -> invalidate (direct), commitment -> source stream entries (source_stream_entry_ids, in_list), commitment -> directive family (directive_family, in_list), commitment -> supersession chain (superseded_by_id, in_list), commitment -> related reviews (commitment_reconciliation refs, in_list), commitment -> canonicalizing shared_state (dart_, in_list), commitment -> revoke/correct (direct), creator_directive -> authorization stream entries (authorization_stream_entry_ids, in_list), creator_directive -> content source entries (content_source_stream_entry_ids, in_list), creator_directive -> subject entity (subject_entity_id, needs entity-by-id), creator_directive -> supersession chain (superseded_by_id, in_list), creator_directive -> reconciliation review (directive_ids in refs, in_list), creator_directive -> revoke/supersede (direct), dream_audit -> process (process name, direct handoff), dream_audit -> target objects (targets record walk, per-target), dream_audit -> reversal payload (direct inline), dream_audit -> revert (direct), dream_audit -> related dream_report stream entry (needs link, in_list scan), shared_state -> provenance stream entries + canonicalized goal/commitment/action/open_question ids (in_list), turn -> evidence ledger (direct getLedger) + live flow snapshot (live-cache only), review -> referenced node/edge/episode/directive ids in refs (in_list)
**Backend gaps needed:** Unified object lookup: GET /api/object/:id resolving any prefixed id to its row (today only semantic nodes, semantic edges, attachments have single-object GETs; episode/commitment/directive/identity/shared-state/relational/mood/action are only reachable via list/band endpoints), Related-object endpoint: GET /api/object/:id/related to make 'show everything connected to this object' reliable instead of in_list scans (e.g. episode->derived semantic nodes, node->edges, object->reviews reverse indices), Stream fetch by entry ids and/or by turn id (GET /api/stream?ids=... or ?turn_id=...): /api/stream filters only by session/kind/audience/before-cursor, so source_stream_*_ids pivots cannot be fetched directly and depend on the loaded window, Single-object GET for episode (no GET /api/episodes/:id), commitment, creator directive, and identity rows (goal/value/trait/open_question/growth/period) so the inspector can open them without loading the whole list, Entity-by-id endpoint (GET /api/entities/:id): only the creator entity is fetchable today, so sender/reply-target/subject-entity pivots cannot resolve a full entity, Reverse provenance indices: stream_entry->derived episodes, episode->semantic nodes/edges, and object->reviews-that-reference-it (review refs are forward-only, freeform per kind), Autobiographical-period linkage on episodes (no period_id field) for the episode->period pivot, Dream audit row -> originating dream_report stream entry link (no shared id today), why/correct surface for non-correctable types (action_record, relational_slot, shared_state, growth_marker, autobiographical_period, entity) -- correctionTargetIdKindDescriptors covers only 8 types, Persisted historical turn-phase traces / ledgers so turn-flow pivots survive refresh/restart (currently only in the in-memory flowSnapshotByTurn/ledgerByTurn live cache)

---

# Part III — Screen specifications

## Mission Control — the landing cockpit (3-column: session fleet · Cognitive Orrery · attention queue)
# Mission Control — landing cockpit

## Purpose
The first screen answers, at a glance: is the being alive, what is it doing right now, which session needs attention, is memory healthy, are reviews blocking trust, is governance clean, is the dream cycle maintaining the substrate. It is a **status + pivot** surface, not a working surface: it summarizes and links into the deep screens (Conversation Workbench, Memory Atlas, Governance, Review & Repair, Dream Ops, Prompt Lab) and into the Universal Inspector drawer. It must never become a giant table; per the proposal it uses small multiples, count badges, severity chips, sparklines, mini timelines, last-changed stamps, and live pulse.

It becomes the **default route** (replacing `"cognition"` as the initial `useState<RouteId>` value in `App.tsx:40`).

## Component breakdown

### New files (all under `demo/web/src/screens/MissionControl/`)
- **`index.tsx`** — `export function MissionControlScreen(props)`. Owns the 3-column grid `.mc` (`var(--fleet) minmax(0,1fr) var(--attn)`, e.g. `260px 1fr 340px`, full-height, children `overflow:hidden` + internal scroll — the established per-screen grid idiom, mirrors `.cog`/`.stream-screen`). Receives from `App.tsx`: `sessionId`, `activeAudience`, `state` (StateSnapshot), `sessions`, `creator`, `turnStream` (the existing `useTurnStream` handle), and callbacks `setRoute`, `setSessionId`, plus the session actions already in App (`openOperatorChat`, `markCreatorByName`, `setSessionPolicy` via `onSessionPolicyChanged`). Owns local UI state only: orrery view-mode (`live|replay|health|provenance`), `replayTurnId`, `inspectorTarget` ({kind,id} | null).
- **`SessionFleet.tsx`** — `export function SessionFleet`. Dense session list; **refactor/extract** of the existing `components/SessionsSidebar.tsx` row vocabulary rather than a parallel impl (see migration note). Per row: `label`, `source_type` chip, `audience_label`, `audience_role` (participant/operator), `participation_policy` badge, `status` dot, `message_count`, `last_activity_at` (relative), last-outcome chip (derived from `turnStream` terminal outcome for `last_turn_id` when cached, else neutral), and a live recent-pulse dot (`.dot.alive`) flashed on `stream:append` frames whose `session_id` matches. Header controls: open-operator-session button, mark-creator, and two structural filter pill-groups — status (`active|idle|archived`, keyed on `SessionRecord.status`) and role (`participant|operator`, keyed on `SessionRecord.audience_role`). Clicking a row calls `setSessionId` (global context switch via the existing URL-synced `useSession` path); kebab opens participation-policy inline editor (reuse `ParticipationPolicyControl` extracted from `Cognition/index.tsx`).
- **`CognitiveOrrery.tsx`** — `export function CognitiveOrrery`. The hero. **Wraps and reuses** `screens/Cognition/FlowChart.tsx` and `screens/Cognition/ParticleField.tsx` verbatim (both are already pure/props-driven — they take `phases/tokenText/detail/terminal/delibPath/finalAttempt` and render). The orrery is the FlowChart fed from `turnStream`:
  - **Live mode**: feed `turnStream.activeTurnId`'s live phase state (today's behavior).
  - **Replay mode**: feed `turnStream.flowSnapshotByTurn.get(replayTurnId)` and `turnStream.ledgerByTurn.get(replayTurnId)` — the LRU-cached (64-turn) snapshots that are *already computed but rendered nowhere*. A thin turn-selector strip (horizontal mini-timeline of cached turn ids with terminal-outcome color dots, sourced from `flowSnapshotByTurn` keys + `turnStream.eventTail`) sets `replayTurnId`. This finally wires the `Xray.tsx` `tracePlaceholder` stub.
  - **Health mode**: overlays each phase node with aggregate status from `turnStream.eventTail` (recent fail/suppression density) and the dream/maintenance ring state from `getDreamState` (process tiles as outer satellites colored by `last_status`).
  - **Provenance mode**: when a ledger entry is selected, highlight the `retrieval`/`ledger` FlowChart nodes and draw the source-kind chips from `EvidenceLedger.sections[].entries[].source_type` — closing the existing no-cross-pane-linkage gap.
  - **Orbital rings**: the 8 memory bands rendered as concentric rings around the FlowChart core, radius/label from `getMemoryBands` (`MemoryBandSummary.count`, `growth[]` → ring thickness/`Spark`). Clicking a ring → `setRoute("memory")` (Atlas) for that band. Governance arcs = translucent constraint arcs whose presence is bound to `getCommitments` (critical = solid amber arc) / `getCreatorDirectives` (active = beacon). Review fault nodes = amber/red pips attached to phase nodes, count from open reviews. These overlays are **decorative-but-bound** SVG layers composited over the reused FlowChart `<svg>`; no new physics engine (the `SemanticTopology` force engine is NOT pulled in here — orrery layout stays the FlowChart's existing baked geometry plus static concentric rings).
- **`OrreryStatusStrip.tsx`** — `export function OrreryStatusStrip`. Below the orrery: current turn id (short), current phase (`turnStream.lastPhase`), terminal outcome chip (`turnStream.terminalOutcome`), final-attempt count (`turnStream.finalAttempt`), delib path (`system_1|system_2`), active dream process (from `dream:process:started/completed` frames already handled in `use-turn-stream`, or `getDreamState`), and a compact mood vector glyph (valence/arousal from `StateSnapshot.current_mood`, mini 2-axis dot). All `tab-num`, eyebrow labels.
- **`AttentionQueue.tsx`** — `export function AttentionQueue`. Right column; a vertical stack of `AttentionCard`s (below). Each card has eyebrow title + count badge + severity chip + a compact body (small-multiple / sparkline / mini-list of ≤3 most-recent rows) + two actions: **open** (pivots to the owning deep screen via `setRoute`) and **inspect** (opens Universal Inspector on the most-salient row, or the first row).
- **`AttentionCard.tsx`** — `export function AttentionCard`. Generic presentational card: `{ title, count, countIsLowerBound?, severity: TagKind, sparkData?, lastChangedAt?, rows: {id,kind,label,chips}[], onOpen, onInspect, loading, error, empty }`. Reuses `Panel`, `Tag`, `Spark`, `Empty`. This is the single card primitive all 7 attention cards render through (no per-card bespoke layout).
- **`use-attention-data.ts`** — `export function useAttentionData(sessionId)`. A composition hook over the standard `useApi` pattern: fans out `getReviews({openOnly:true})`, `getCommitments()`, `getCreatorDirectives()`, `getDreamState()`, `getMemoryBands()`, `getStream({session,kinds:[...],limit})`, and `getPrompts()`, exposes `{data, loading, error, refetch}` per source, and subscribes via `useLiveEventsContext().subscribe` to refetch on `maintenance:tick` and (debounced) `stream:append` and `borg:reset` — mirroring the Review/Dream live-refetch pattern. Derives all counts client-side (see Data presentation). This keeps Mission Control fetch logic out of `index.tsx` and reuses the project's data-fetching convention rather than ad-hoc client calls.

### Existing components/files reused or refactored (real paths)
- `demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/FlowChart.tsx` — reused verbatim as the orrery core (props-driven, no internal fetch). The orrery adds concentric-ring + arc SVG overlays around it; FlowChart's own geometry/renderers untouched.
- `demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/ParticleField.tsx` — reused as the orrery's aesthetic focal layer.
- `demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/Xray.tsx` + `LedgerView.tsx` — reused for the Inspector's turn/ledger panes (and `tracePlaceholder` finally gets a value in replay mode).
- `demo/web/src/hooks/use-turn-stream.ts` — consumed read-only: `flowSnapshotByTurn`, `ledgerByTurn`, `eventTail`, `activeTurnId`, `lastPhase`, `terminalOutcome`, `delibPath`, `finalAttempt`. No changes to its arbitration machinery.
- `demo/web/src/components/SessionsSidebar.tsx` — its `.session-row` markup/CSS (`-top/-label/-time/-preview/-foot`, `.dot.alive`, grouped today/yesterday/earlier) is the basis for `SessionFleet`; extract the row + grouping into a shared piece both consume so the global rail sidebar and the fleet stay one implementation.
- `demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/index.tsx` `ParticipationPolicyControl` — extract to a shared component reused by both Cognition and the fleet.
- `demo/web/src/components/{Panel,Tag,Spark,Empty,Modal}.tsx` — card/chip/sparkline/empty/dialog primitives, used as-is.
- `demo/web/src/components/Rail.tsx` — add `"mission"` to the `RouteId` union and a `RAIL_ITEMS` entry (glyph e.g. `◉` MC, short code `MC`, number `1`, others shift); wire count badges (see below).
- `demo/web/src/components/WhyDrawer.tsx` + `SemanticNodeDetail.tsx`/`SemanticEdgeDetail.tsx` + `SupersededByChip.tsx` — the Universal Inspector drawer (a follow-on shared primitive) reuses these for provenance/why and lineage pivots. Mission Control opens the drawer; the drawer itself is a shell-level component shared across screens.
- `demo/web/src/App.tsx` — owns the new route, threads existing `stateApi/sessionsApi/creatorApi/turnStream` into `MissionControlScreen`, sets default route to `"mission"`.

## Data presentation (per card / region — justified)

**Center orrery (graph, not table):** cognition is inherently a pipeline-with-orbits; the FlowChart graph is the proposal's central navigational metaphor and already encodes phase status as color/animation. Replay turn-selector is a **mini-timeline** (dots by terminal outcome), not a table, because the operator picks "which recent turn" by recency+outcome, not by attributes.

**Attention cards (small multiples + count badge + severity chip + ≤3-row mini-list):** each is a *status glance*, so the dominant element is a count badge with a severity chip and an optional sparkline; the ≤3 rows are the most-recent/most-severe, each a one-line `Tag`-decorated pivot. Full enumeration lives in the deep screen the "open" action targets.

1. **Open reviews by kind** — from `getReviews({openOnly:true})` (one fetch returns all open rows; `ReviewRow.kind` is the 11-kind union). Render as **small multiples**: one mini-bar per `ReviewKind` present, ordered by severity (creator_directive_reconciliation / commitment_reconciliation / contradiction / identity_inconsistency first), count = rows of that kind. Total badge = `state.counts.open_reviews` (cross-check). Severity chip = worst-kind tone. Last-changed = max `created_at`. open → Review & Repair; inspect → first row in Inspector.
2. **Critical / advisory commitments** — from `getCommitments()`, filter `state==="active"`, split by `enforcement_class` (`critical`/`advisory`). Two count badges (critical = amber/`warn`, advisory = neutral). Show ≤3 most-recent active critical by `last_reinforced_at`. open → Governance Studio. (`state.counts.commitments` as cross-check total.)
3. **Creator-directive conflicts** — two structural signals, no word-matching: (a) open reviews of `kind==="creator_directive_reconciliation"` (from the reviews fetch); (b) directives where `status==="active"` whose `superseded_by_id` is set, or active directives canonicalized through a commitment that is revoked/superseded — computed from `getCreatorDirectives()` + `getCommitments()` by id-overlap (the same structural correlation Directives screen already does). Severity = `bad` if any reconciliation open. open → Governance / Review.
4. **Pending dream-extraction episodes** — `getDreamState().pending_extraction_episodes` (also carried on `maintenance:tick` frames for live update without refetch). Count badge + last maintenance-tick stamp + tiny schedule sparkline from `DreamStateResponse.schedule`. open → Dream Ops.
5. **Recent suppressed / observed outcomes** — `getStream({session, kinds:["agent_suppressed","agent_observed"], limit:20})`; classify each via the existing `lib/stream-outcomes.ts` `streamOutcomeSummary` (deliberate-silence / emission-failed / guard-blocked / observed). **Mini-timeline** of the last N outcomes as colored pips (`agent_suppressed`→`bad/warn` by reason, `agent_observed`→`info`). Severity = presence of guard-blocked/emission-failed. inspect → Stream & Provenance detail. NOTE: this is **observation only** — it surfaces what already happened; it does not police or re-gate output.
6. **Quarantined / inactive attachments** — see BACKEND GAP. Card renders with the verified per-id path only if given ids; absent a list endpoint it shows an empty/"needs counts endpoint" state. Status semantics come from `AttachmentMetadataResponse.status` (`quarantined`, `active`, `stream_active`, `parent_active`).
7. **Prompt overrides** — `getPrompts()`, count = blocks with `overridden===true` (or `current_text_kind==="stored_override"`). List overridden block labels; chip `warn` if a `runtime_composed` (host-capabilities) block was frozen into an override. open → Prompt Lab.

**Rail badges:** `Rail.tsx` items show tiny count badges fed from the same derived counts — MC: nothing or aggregate; REV: `open_reviews`; DRM: dream error count (`getDreamState` processes with `last_status==="error"`); GOV: critical-commitment + directive-conflict count; PMT: override count; the fleet/COG: active session count. These reuse `StateSnapshot.counts` where available and the derived counts otherwise.

## Key interactions
- **Session click** → global context switch (`setSessionId`, URL-synced); orrery + status strip + outcome card re-scope to that session.
- **Orrery phase hover** → tooltip with duration / latest detail line / token snippet (from `turnStream` per-phase maps). **Phase click** → open Inspector on the current/replay turn focused on that phase.
- **Orrery ring click** → `setRoute("memory")` (band); **review fault click** → `setRoute("review")`; **dream satellite click** → `setRoute("dream")`; **governance arc click** → `setRoute("commit"/"directives")`.
- **Orrery mode toggle** → Live / Replay (turn picker appears) / Health / Provenance.
- **Attention card open** → owning deep screen; **inspect** → Universal Inspector drawer.

## Cross-link pivots (into/out of Universal Inspector)
Mission Control is a *source* of pivots; the drawer is the shared sink keyed on a discriminated `{kind,id}`:
- session row → `session`; orrery turn/phase → `turn` (+ phase focus) → `ledger_entry`; ledger entry → `episode`/`semantic_node`/`semantic_edge`/`commitment`/`shared_state` via `EvidenceLedgerEntry.source_type` + `citations`.
- review card row → `review_row` (→ its pair nodes/edge for contradiction/duplicate); commitment row → `commitment` (→ `SupersededByChip` lineage); directive-conflict row → `creator_directive` / `review_row`; suppressed/observed row → `stream_entry`; attachment row → `attachment`; prompt row → `prompt_block`; dream card → `dream_audit_row` / process.
- Inverse: opening any of these in the Inspector elsewhere can deep-link back to Mission Control's session/turn context.

## States: loading / empty / error / live
- **Loading**: each region renders its own skeleton (cards show count placeholder + `Empty`-style shimmer); orrery shows the FlowChart's existing queued/"waiting" seed state. Regions load independently via per-source `useApi`, so one slow fetch never blocks the cockpit.
- **Empty**: zero-session → fleet shows operator-session CTA; zero open reviews/commitments/overrides → card shows green "clean" chip (not an error). No-creator-marked → mark-creator CTA in fleet (mirrors App's 409 `operatorChatError` "mark a creator first").
- **Error**: per-source error surfaces inside that card/region only (reuse `ApiError` message), with a retry that calls the source `refetch`; the rest of the cockpit stays live.
- **Live**: `wsState` from `useLiveEventsContext` drives the orrery/status-strip pulse and the top-bar live dot (reconnecting=amber, down=red). `stream:append` flashes session recent-pulse + (debounced) refetches outcome card; `maintenance:tick` updates dream-extraction count + refetches reviews/dream; `turn:phase:*`/`turn:terminal` drive the live orrery; `borg:reset` triggers `window.location.reload()` (existing App behavior) so all cards reseed clean.

## Migration note (what becomes this / what is reused / removed)
- **New default landing.** Today `App.tsx` opens on `CognitionScreen`. Mission Control becomes route `"mission"` and the initial route. Cognition is NOT deleted — it becomes the **Conversation Workbench** (separate spec); its `FlowChart`/`ParticleField`/`Xray`/`LedgerView` are now *shared* between the Workbench (compact, single live turn) and Mission Control (full orrery, any cached turn).
- **`SessionsSidebar` refactor (not duplicate):** extract its `.session-row` + grouping into a shared row component consumed by both the existing global sidebar and the new `SessionFleet`. Per CLAUDE.md hygiene, do not author a parallel session-row impl.
- **`ParticipationPolicyControl` extraction** from `Cognition/index.tsx` into a shared component (used by fleet + workbench).
- **Wiring of already-built-but-unrendered assets:** `flowSnapshotByTurn` (64-turn LRU), `ledgerByTurn`, `eventTail`, and the `Xray` `tracePlaceholder` stub were scaffolded and never surfaced — Mission Control's replay mode + outcome timeline + turn picker consume them with no change to `use-turn-stream`.
- **Nothing in `src/cognition|retrieval|memory|offline` is touched** — this is presentation-only.
- **No router added** — extend the existing custom `RouteId`+conditional-render pattern (per build-tooling guidance); session stays URL-synced via `useSession`.

## Cardinal-constraint compliance
- **No recall gating.** Every card and the orrery surface *what the being knows*, annotated with disclosure/provenance chips (origin_audience, private/operator, trust_rank, taint, citation_type from `EvidenceLedgerEntry`; `SessionRecord.audience_role`/`participation_policy`; `CommitmentItem.audience`). Audience/session/role are **ranking + labeling + scope-to-session-for-the-outcome-card** axes only; no card filters cognition by audience, and the orrery/memory rings show the full bands regardless of who is in the active session.
- **No production policing.** The suppressed/observed card *observes and classifies* existing outcomes via the read-only `stream-outcomes` lib; it never rewrites or re-gates output. Correction/repair affordances route exclusively through the sanctioned endpoints in the deep screens (not from Mission Control directly), and Mission Control only *links* to them.
- **Language-agnostic.** All branching keys on structure — `ReviewKind`, `enforcement_class`, `status`, `participation_policy`, `audience_role`, `source_type`, `current_text_kind`, attachment `status` flags — never on matching words in user content. The directive-conflict detection uses id-overlap/superseded-by structure, not phrase matching.
**New files:** demo/web/src/screens/MissionControl/index.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MissionControl/SessionFleet.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MissionControl/CognitiveOrrery.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MissionControl/OrreryStatusStrip.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MissionControl/AttentionQueue.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MissionControl/AttentionCard.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MissionControl/use-attention-data.ts, demo/web/src/screens/MissionControl/index.test.tsx
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/FlowChart.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/ParticleField.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/Xray.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/LedgerView.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/index.tsx (ParticipationPolicyControl -> extract to shared), demo/web/src/hooks/use-turn-stream.ts (read flowSnapshotByTurn/ledgerByTurn/eventTail), demo/web/src/components/SessionsSidebar.tsx (extract shared session-row), demo/web/src/components/Panel.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Spark.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Empty.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Modal.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Rail.tsx (add 'mission' RouteId + badges), demo/web/src/components/WhyDrawer.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SemanticNodeDetail.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SupersededByChip.tsx, demo/web/src/lib/stream-outcomes.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx, demo/web/src/App.tsx (route wiring + default route)
**API bindings:** GET /api/state, GET /api/sessions, GET /api/memory/bands, GET /api/reviews?open_only=true, GET /api/commitments, GET /api/creator-directives, GET /api/dream/state, GET /api/stream, GET /api/turns/:id/ledger, GET /api/semantic/graph, GET /api/prompts, GET /api/attachments?ids=, GET /api/attachments/:id, POST /api/sessions/operator, POST /api/sessions/:id/participation, POST /api/entities/creator, GET /api/entities/creator, GET /api/correction/:id/why
**Live frames:** stream:append, turn:phase:started, turn:phase:completed, turn:phase:failed, turn:token, turn:token:flush, turn:terminal, turn:delib_path, turn:final_attempt, turn:phase:detail, evidence_ledger:built, dream:process:started, dream:process:completed, maintenance:tick, borg:reset
**Cross-link pivots:** session row -> session, orrery turn/phase -> turn (phase-focused), ledger entry -> episode | semantic_node | semantic_edge | commitment | shared_state (via EvidenceLedgerEntry.source_type + citations), review card row -> review_row (-> paired semantic_node/edge for contradiction/duplicate), commitment row -> commitment (-> SupersededByChip lineage), creator-directive-conflict row -> creator_directive | review_row, suppressed/observed row -> stream_entry, attachment row -> attachment, prompt-override row -> prompt_block, dream card -> dream_audit_row | dream process, memory ring -> Memory Atlas band
**Backend gaps needed:** List/count quarantined+inactive attachments globally: GET /api/attachments is batch-by-?ids= only (AttachmentStatusItem[]) with no list-all or count-by-status; the quarantined/inactive attachments card cannot enumerate without either a known id set or a new endpoint (e.g. GET /api/attachments?status=quarantined or a counts-by-status field). NOTE: the source-side gap is the missing list, not recall — quarantine is a perception/disclosure status, surfaced as a label, never used to hide a memory from cognition., Optional perf: counts-by-kind / counts-by-status aggregate for reviews, commitments, creator-directives, and suppressed/observed stream outcomes. Not required at demo scale (full-list fetches + client-side grouping work and are what this spec binds to), but a counts endpoint would let Mission Control avoid client-side scans as data grows., Recent suppressed/observed outcome COUNT/window summary: today derived by fetching GET /api/stream filtered to kinds agent_suppressed/agent_observed and classifying client-side via stream-outcomes; no endpoint returns a count or recent-window summary of outcomes by class.
**Scope verdict:** Opus 5.0: in-scope — Mission Control is pure presentation/surfacing/cross-linking of already-exposed runtime state (disclosure/provenance rendered as render-time labels, audience used only for ranking and outcome-card session scoping), adds zero recall-gating and zero output-policing; the only true backend need is a non-recall attachment list/count endpoint.
---

## Conversation Workbench (evolved Cognition screen) — four-area cognitive cockpit
# Conversation Workbench — Design Spec

## Purpose

The main demo screen. Evolves the current `CognitionScreen` (`demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/index.tsx`, 435 lines) from a fixed two-pane "chat + Xray of the single live turn" into a four-area cockpit that answers, for *any* turn (live or selected from history): what was said, what Borg did internally, what it saw before responding, why it answered/suppressed/observed, and what it captured afterward.

The decisive change vs. today: a **`selectedTurnId` replay axis**. The current screen only ever renders `turnStream.activeTurnId`; the engine already caches every turn's full flow (`flowSnapshotByTurn`, LRU 64) and ledger (`ledgerByTurn`) but **no UI exposes them**. The Workbench introduces a single selection that defaults to the live/active turn and can be pinned to a past turn, driving the center orrery and the right pane in lockstep.

### Architectural posture (CLAUDE.md compliance)
- This is an **operator console**, presentation only. It renders what the entity *knows* (the evidence ledger, retrieved memory) with **disclosure/provenance labels computed at view time**. It never re-filters or hides recalled rows by audience/session — `EvidenceLedgerEntry.session_scope` and `.taint` and `origin`/actor are rendered as **chips**, not as filter predicates. There is no "show only what this audience may see" toggle on cognition data.
- **No output policing.** The only write paths are `postTurn` (submit a turn) and `setSessionPolicy` (participation). "Why?"/correct/forget are *pivots into the Universal Inspector*, which owns the sanctioned correction endpoints — the Workbench never rewrites or suppresses Borg's emitted text.
- **Language-agnostic.** All grouping/labeling keys on structure (`source_type`, `actor`, `taint`, `session_scope`, `kind`, `outcome`, phase ids) — never on words in user/assistant content. Suppression/observed reasons are rendered via the existing structural `stream-outcomes.ts` classifier (`SuppressionOutcomeClass`), not string matching.

Opus 5.0: in-scope — every gap here is harness *presentation/wiring* (unwired cached snapshots, no turn selector, no cross-pane provenance links, static `--mode` text), not model judgment.

---

## Component breakdown

New folder `demo/web/src/screens/Workbench/` replaces `screens/Cognition/`. Layout shell is a CSS grid (new `.wb` class in `styles.css`, modeled on the existing `.cog` 3-col-with-divider grid): a left column (timeline + composer, `var(--sidebar+) `≈340px), a center column (`1fr`, compact orrery), a right column (`~360px`, "What Borg saw"), and a full-width bottom strip (`var(--turn-history-h)`≈92px). Full-height, children `overflow:hidden`, scroll inside — the project's standard screen pattern.

### NEW files

- **`screens/Workbench/index.tsx`** — `WorkbenchScreen`. Owns the four areas and the new `selectedTurnId` state (`null` ⇒ follow `turnStream.activeTurnId`). Carries forward, *verbatim from current `Cognition/index.tsx`*, the optimistic-send lifecycle (`makeClientMessageId`, `optimisticUserEntry`, `upsertOptimisticEntry`, `markOptimisticStatus`, `withoutReconciledOptimisticEntries`, `sameUserMessageForReconcile`), the `stream:append` live merge, and the reconnect resync (`getStream` + `replaceTailFromEntries`). Props extend `CognitionScreenProps` with one addition: `onInspect(target: { kind: InspectorKind; id: string })` from `App.tsx` (the Universal Inspector entry point). Subscribes through `useLiveEventsContext()` and the shared `turnStream = useTurnStream(live, { sessionId })` (engine unchanged).
- **`screens/Workbench/ConversationLane.tsx`** — `ConversationLane`. The left timeline lane (replaces bare chat bubbles). Renders a chronological lane of typed markers, not just user/assistant bubbles.
- **`screens/Workbench/LaneRow.tsx`** — one marker row per visual kind (user / image / assistant / suppressed / observed / dream-or-internal), with the compact badge set. Reuses `ChatMessage`'s avatar/role/short-turn-id idiom internally but adds marker glyphs and badges.
- **`screens/Workbench/CompactOrrery.tsx`** — `CompactOrrery`. Wraps the existing `FlowChart` for the center, fed a `TurnFlowSnapshot` (live or replayed). Adds the header readout (turn id / current phase / terminal badge / final-attempt / S1·S2 path / live-token activity) and the hover/click affordances FlowChart lacks today.
- **`screens/Workbench/WhatBorgSaw.tsx`** — `WhatBorgSaw`. The right 7-tab pane. Tab `Evidence Ledger` embeds an enhanced `LedgerView`; the rest read their own resources.
- **`screens/Workbench/TurnHistoryStrip.tsx`** — `TurnHistoryStrip`. Bottom horizontal strip of recent turns with replay selection.
- **`screens/Workbench/workbench-utils.ts`** — pure helpers: `streamEntriesToLaneRows` (extends `streamEntriesToChatTurns` to keep `agent_suppressed`/`agent_observed`/`dream_report`/`internal_event` as their own marker rows instead of dropping them), `laneKindForEntry(kind)` → marker class, `terminalOutcomeBadge(outcome)` → `{label, tone}`, `effectiveTurnId(selected, active)`.
- **`components/MiniOrrery.tsx`** (shared) — a tiny 15-dot glyph (queued/running/done/failed colors) for the lane row + history strip "this turn's pipeline at a glance"; reuses the `fc-node-${status}` color tokens. (Proposal §367's "mini-orrery glyph".)

### EXISTING components reused (cite real paths)

- **`screens/Cognition/FlowChart.tsx`** — reused **unchanged** as the center orrery renderer. It is already pure/props-driven (`phases/tokenText/detail/terminal/delibPath/finalAttempt/activeTurnId`); feeding it `flowSnapshotByTurn.get(effectiveTurnId)` replays any cached turn. Keeps the 15-node 3-tier topology, S1/S2 fork (`forkPath`), regen arc (`regenPath`), suppression thorns (`ThornUp`/`ThornSidecar`), `Ganglion`/`Endpoint`/`Edge`, the active-token `ActiveStream` footer, and `ParticleField`. No layout abstraction is built (multi-turn orrery is a Mission-Control concern, out of scope here — compact = one turn).
- **`screens/Cognition/Xray.tsx`** — its role is **split and absorbed**. The `flow|ledger` tab shell is superseded by the center pane + right 7-tab pane. Crucially, its **`tracePlaceholder` prop (defined, never passed a value) and `cachedLedger` prop become load-bearing**: the replay empty-state ("no live trace retained for this turn") and the live-vs-fetched ledger source are exactly the historical-replay path Xray scaffolded. `Xray.tsx` itself is deleted; its two-tab logic is not reused (the new layout is not tabbed flow/ledger).
- **`screens/Cognition/LedgerView.tsx`** — reused, lightly extended. Keeps entry rendering, `trustKind` mapping (`quarantined`/`contested`→bad, `tentative`/`pending`→warn, `locked`/`live`→acc), taint tag, `trust {trust_rank}`, citations, `AttachmentChip`, and the lazy `getLedger(turnId)` with graceful 404 ("ledger not retained pre-restart"). Extension: (1) each entry gets a click → `onInspect`; (2) each entry shows an explicit **scope chip** (`session_scope`) and **actor chip** (`actor`) and **disclosure chip** (`citation_type` / `persistence_class` "assistant self-report"), promoting fields already on `EvidenceLedgerEntry` from prose into labeled chips.
- **`screens/Cognition/ChatInput.tsx`** — reused **verbatim** as the composer. Already does image staging (thumbnails, drag-drop, paste), auto-grow textarea, Enter/Shift-Enter/Cmd-Enter, in-flight de-dupe, restore-on-rejection, and a flags strip. The only change: its hardcoded `--mode auto` static text is replaced by a real **participation-policy chip** wired to the control below it.
- **`screens/Cognition/ChatStream.tsx` / `ChatMessage.tsx` / `chat-utils.ts`** — `ChatMessage` reused inside `LaneRow` (avatar `ψ`/initial, role, short `turn 6chars`, delivery `queued`/`sent`, thought bubble, `refs` chips). `streamEntriesToChatTurns` is extended by `workbench-utils.streamEntriesToLaneRows` rather than forked. `ChatStream`'s auto-scroll + "borg is thinking…" placeholder reused by `ConversationLane`.
- **`ParticipationPolicyControl`** (currently inline in `Cognition/index.tsx`) — **extracted** into `screens/Workbench/ParticipationPolicyControl.tsx` and reused. Keeps `PARTICIPATION_POLICIES` (`active|paused|observing|muted`), free-text reason, `setSessionPolicy` post, and the **operator-role lock** (`audience_role === "operator"` ⇒ locked). Restyled into a prominent labeled control above the composer (proposal §529): each policy renders its meaning line — active "normal", observing "Borg can observe but cannot answer", paused "Borg will not process active participation", muted "Borg will stay silent". These are static UI copy keyed on the enum value, not content inspection.
- Shared kit: `components/Panel.tsx`, `Tag.tsx` (the `acc/warn/bad/info/purple/solid` chip set for every badge), `Empty.tsx` (`.notice`), `AttachmentChip.tsx`, `ImagePlaceholder.tsx`, `SupersededByChip.tsx` (for ledger commitment-source rows that carry supersession). Live/data via `useApi` + `useLiveEventsContext` + the typed client in `api/client.ts`.

---

## Data presentation (per-area, with shape justification)

### Left — Conversation timeline lane (`ConversationLane` + `LaneRow`)
**Choice: a chronological *lane* (vertical timeline), not a chat-bubble list.** Justified by the proposal's data-shape rule that the conversation is an ordered, heterogeneous *event* sequence (not just two-party chat): user_msg, user_image_attachment, agent_msg, **agent_suppressed**, **agent_observed**, and session-relevant **dream_report/internal_event**. A bubble UI structurally cannot show a suppressed/observed/silent turn (today `chat-utils` drops them). The lane keeps every marker on one spine.

Marker kinds (glyph + tone): user (audience initial, `info`), image (`ImagePlaceholder` thumb), assistant (`ψ`, `acc`), suppressed (`bad`, struck pill), observed (`purple`/muted, "observed — not answered"), dream/internal (`☾`/`›`, faint, only when `session_id` matches).

Per-row compact badges (all from real fields): **turn id** (`turn_id`, 6-char, click→Inspector turn), **audience** (`audience`), **source** (`source_message_key` present ⇒ external glyph; else demo), **attachment count**, **delivery status** (optimistic `queued`/`sent`), **suppressed/observed reason** (via `stream-outcomes.streamOutcomeSummary` — structural classes only), **response-to** (`response_to`/`AgentSuppressedStreamContent.user_entry_ids` → clickable stream-id chips). Trailing `MiniOrrery` glyph showing that turn's pipeline (from `flowSnapshotByTurn`) when cached. Selecting a row sets `selectedTurnId`.

### Bottom-left — Composer (`ChatInput` + `ParticipationPolicyControl`)
Composer reused verbatim. Above it, the participation control is the prominent element (proposal §529) — a labeled segmented control with the four policies and their meaning line, locked for operator sessions. Audience indicator renders `activeAudience`. "Send as current session" is implicit (the bound `sessionId`); duplicate/external-id handling stays internal (`makeClientMessageId` + `external_message_id`).

### Center — Compact orrery (`CompactOrrery` → `FlowChart`)
**Choice: the existing node-graph (synaptic flow canvas), not a table or Gantt.** Justified because the 15 phases form a fixed *topology with branches* (S1/S2 fork, regen loop, suppression thorns) that only a graph expresses; a table loses the fork/loop/gate structure that is the whole point. Bound to: phase status/duration (`turn:phase:started/completed/failed`), token activity (`turn:token`/`flush`, the `ActiveStream` footer), S1/S2 (`turn:delib_path`), regen arc (`turn:final_attempt`), terminal badge (`turn:terminal`). Header readout below the canvas: turn id, current phase, `terminalOutcomeBadge`, final-attempt count, delib path, plus a live token snippet. New interactions FlowChart lacks: **hover phase** → tooltip (duration + latest `detailByPhase` line + token snippet from `tokenTextByPhase`); **click phase** → focuses the right pane and (for `ledger`/`retrieval`/`shared`) scrolls the matching ledger group; **click terminal thorn** → opens the suppression diagnostic in the Raw tab.

### Right — "What Borg saw" (`WhatBorgSaw`, 7 tabs)
1. **Evidence Ledger** (default) — `LedgerView` over `effectiveTurnId`'s ledger (live `ledgerByTurn` else `getLedger`). **Choice: grouped sections keyed on the 13 real `EvidenceLedgerSourceType` values**, not a flat list — the ledger already arrives as `sections[]`; we render them in a fixed source-class order (current_user_message → current/prior_session_stream → episode → semantic_node → semantic_edge → action_record → relational_slot → commitment → shared_state → image_attachment → assistant_stream → system_metadata). Every entry shows the required label row as `Tag`s: **actor**, **trust rank**, **scope** (`session_scope`), **taint** (`none`/`assistant_seeded`/`quarantined`/`contested`), **disclosure/citation** (`citation_type` + `persistence_class`), then text/value, then **source links** (`citations[]` → Inspector pivots). Header keeps `estimatedTokens` + transcript-compaction stats. These chips are descriptive labels on globally-recalled rows — never filters.
2. **Shared State** — `ledger.sharedState.entries` if present, else `getSharedState(audience)`; rendered as `SharedStateEntry` rows with kind + lifecycle tag (reuse `shared-state-lifecycle.ts` `lifecycleLabel`/`tagKind`).
3. **Retrieved Memory** — the subset of ledger entries with `via_retrieval === true`, grouped by `source_type` (episode/semantic_node/semantic_edge/action_record/relational_slot). Each pivots to the Inspector. (No separate fetch — derived from the ledger; this is "what recall surfaced for this turn", globally, labeled.)
4. **Commitments** — `getCommitments({ session })`-derived: the commitments referenced in this turn's ledger (`source_type === "commitment"`) plus the live applicable set, each with state/enforcement chips; pivot to Inspector/Governance.
5. **Open Questions** — from `getIdentity().open_questions`, rendered with urgency; the ones touched this turn (matched by ledger `action_record`/citation ids, structurally) flagged. Read-only here; actions live in the Inspector.
6. **Prompt Context** — the static framing via `getAssembledPrompt` (collapsible section tags + text), with the standing note that per-turn dynamic context (this ledger) is shown in tab 1. Link to Prompt Lab.
7. **Raw** — pretty-printed JSON of `{ ledger, flowSnapshot, terminalOutcome, suppression diagnostic }` for the selected turn (the diagnostic from `AgentSuppressedStreamContent`/`FinalizerNoOutput*`).

Tab choice rationale: tabs (not stacked panels) because only one "lens on the same turn" is relevant at a time and the ledger is large; matches the existing `Xray` tab idiom and `Memory` drill view-mode toggle.

### Bottom — Turn-history strip (`TurnHistoryStrip`)
**Choice: a horizontal dense strip of small turn cards, not a table** — it is a *scrubber/selector* across recent turns, kept compact so the four main areas dominate (proposal §453 "avoid giant tables"). `getTurns({ session })` → `TurnHistoryRow[]` (`turn_id`, `started_at`, `audience`, `outcome` via `TurnHistoryOutcomeClass`, `suppression_reason`), reverse-chronological, `next_cursor` "load older". Each card: relative time, `terminalOutcomeBadge`/`outcome` chip, suppression reason (structural), audience, a `MiniOrrery` if `flowSnapshotByTurn` has it, and a **duration only when available from the live/replay cache** (history rows carry no duration — see backend gap). Click ⇒ `setSelectedTurnId` (pin replay); the live/active turn card is marked and clicking it returns to follow-live.

---

## Key interactions

- **Replay axis:** `selectedTurnId` (null = follow live). Center orrery + right pane + lane-row highlight all key off `effectiveTurnId(selectedTurnId, activeTurnId)`. Replaying a cached turn uses `flowSnapshotByTurn`/`ledgerByTurn`; a turn evicted from the 64-LRU (or pre-restart) shows the live-only empty state (Xray's `tracePlaceholder`) plus a lazily-fetched ledger if still retained.
- **Phase → ledger cross-highlight:** clicking the `ledger`/`retrieval`/`shared` phase node scrolls the matching right-pane group; conversely hovering a ledger entry with `via_retrieval` pulses the `retrieval`/`ledger` node. (Closes the "no cross-pane linkage" gap; the provenance data already exists.)
- **Send:** `ChatInput.onSend` → `turnStream.runTurn(TurnRequest)` (existing `postTurn`), optimistic row appears immediately, reconciled on `stream:append`.
- **Suppressed/observed turn:** selecting one shows its diagnostic (Raw tab + lane reason chip) — never editable.

---

## Cross-link pivots (Universal Inspector)

The Workbench is a **source** of pivots and a **target** of "open in source screen". All selections call `onInspect({ kind, id })`:
- ledger entry → kind from `source_type`: `episode`→episode, `semantic_node`/`semantic_edge`→that node/edge, `commitment`→commitment, `shared_state`→shared-state entry, `action_record`→action, `image_attachment`→attachment, stream sources→stream entry.
- ledger `citations[]` and lane `response_to`/`user_entry_ids` → stream-entry pivots.
- lane turn-id badge / history card → turn-kind inspector (Summary tab shows the flow snapshot + ledger).
- "Why?" on a memory-backed ledger row → Inspector's Evidence tab (`getWhy(id)`); correct/forget/invalidate are offered **only inside the Inspector**, routing to the sanctioned correction endpoints. The Workbench never mutates memory inline.
- Inbound: Mission Control's orrery "click phase → open turn inspector" and Stream's "which turn answered this" deep-link land on the Workbench with `selectedTurnId` set.

---

## States: loading / empty / error / live

- **Live:** WS `live` → orrery animates the active turn; lane prepends optimistic + reconciled rows; history strip prepends new terminal turns; right pane updates from `evidence_ledger:built`. `borg:reset` ⇒ `window.location.reload()` (existing App behavior).
- **Reconnecting/down:** `wsState` surfaced as an amber/red dot in the center header (reuse `.live-dot`/`pulse`); on reconnect, `onReconnected` resync (`getStream` tail replace) — existing pattern.
- **Loading:** `useApi` `loading` ⇒ skeleton rows in lane/right/history (reuse `.notice`/dot pulse). Orrery seeds all 15 phases to `queue` (`initialPhases()`).
- **Empty:** no turns ⇒ lane shows `Empty` "no conversation yet — send a message"; orrery shows idle pipeline; right pane "no ledger — submit or select a turn"; history strip "no turns".
- **Error:** per-resource `error` ⇒ inline `Empty` with retry (`refetch`); ledger 404 ⇒ "ledger not retained pre-restart" (existing `LedgerView` handling); replay of evicted turn ⇒ `tracePlaceholder`.

---

## Migration note

- **Becomes this:** `screens/Cognition/` → `screens/Workbench/`. `RouteId` `"cognition"` is renamed/aliased to `"workbench"` in `components/Rail.tsx` (the `RAIL_ITEMS` glyph `ψ`/short-code/⌘-number entry is retargeted; `App.tsx` default `useState<RouteId>("workbench")`). `App.tsx:52,127` (the `useTurnStream` wiring) is unchanged — same hook, same props plus the new `onInspect`.
- **Reused verbatim:** `FlowChart.tsx`, `LedgerView.tsx` (extended, not rewritten), `ChatInput.tsx`, `ChatStream.tsx`, `ChatMessage.tsx`, `chat-utils.ts`, `ParticleField.tsx`, and the entire `use-turn-stream.ts` engine (including `flowSnapshotByTurn`, `ledgerByTurn`, `eventTail`, turn-arbitration). **The engine is not touched** — the Workbench only *consumes* its already-built caches.
- **Extracted/moved:** `ParticipationPolicyControl` and the optimistic-send helpers move out of the 435-line `index.tsx` into `Workbench/index.tsx` + `ParticipationPolicyControl.tsx` (same logic).
- **Deleted:** `screens/Cognition/Xray.tsx` (its two-tab shell is obsoleted by the new layout; its `tracePlaceholder`/`cachedLedger` *contract* is preserved in `CompactOrrery`/`WhatBorgSaw`). `Cognition/index.tsx`'s fixed `.cog` two-pane split is replaced by `.wb`.
- **CSS:** new `.wb`, `.wb-lane`, `.lane-row`, `.mini-orrery`, `.wbs-tab*` classes added to `styles.css`, reusing existing tokens (`--acc/--warn/--bad/--info/--purple`, `--bg-*`, `Tag`/`fc-node-*`/`.dot.alive`/`pulse`). No new color direction. The existing test contract (class hooks like `.stream-row`, accessible roles, `data-testid`) is preserved; new rows get stable `data-testid`s (`lane-row`, `turn-history-row`, `wbs-tab`).

---

## Backend gaps (NOT bound; see requiresBackendGap)
Replay of a turn that has aged out of the 64-LRU or predates a restart cannot show its flow trace (no persisted phase traces). Turn-history rows carry no `duration_ms`, so the strip's duration is "cache-only". An open-object-by-id endpoint would let the Universal Inspector resolve arbitrary ledger `citations` without type-guessing — currently the kind is inferred from `source_type`/id-prefix.
**New files:** demo/web/src/screens/Workbench/index.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Workbench/ConversationLane.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Workbench/LaneRow.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Workbench/CompactOrrery.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Workbench/WhatBorgSaw.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Workbench/TurnHistoryStrip.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Workbench/ParticipationPolicyControl.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Workbench/workbench-utils.ts, demo/web/src/components/MiniOrrery.tsx
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/FlowChart.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/LedgerView.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/ChatInput.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/ChatStream.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/ChatMessage.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/chat-utils.ts, demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/ParticleField.tsx, demo/web/src/hooks/use-turn-stream.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Panel.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Empty.tsx, demo/web/src/components/AttachmentChip.tsx, demo/web/src/components/ImagePlaceholder.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SupersededByChip.tsx, demo/web/src/lib/stream-outcomes.ts, demo/web/src/lib/shared-state-lifecycle.ts, demo/web/src/components/Rail.tsx, demo/web/src/App.tsx, demo/web/src/styles.css
**API bindings:** GET /api/turn, GET /api/stream, GET /api/turns, GET /api/turns/:id/ledger, GET /api/attachments, GET /api/attachments/:id, GET /api/attachments/:id/bytes, POST /api/sessions/:id/participation, GET /api/shared-state, GET /api/commitments, GET /api/identity, GET /api/prompts/assembled, GET /api/correction/:id/why, GET /api/state
**Live frames:** stream:append, turn:phase:started, turn:phase:completed, turn:phase:failed, turn:token, turn:token:flush, turn:terminal, evidence_ledger:built, turn:delib_path, turn:final_attempt, turn:phase:detail, borg:reset
**Cross-link pivots:** ledger entry (source_type=episode|semantic_node|semantic_edge|action_record|relational_slot|commitment|shared_state|image_attachment) -> Universal Inspector, ledger citations[] / lane response_to / user_entry_ids -> stream-entry Inspector, lane turn-id badge + turn-history card -> turn Inspector (flow snapshot + ledger), Why? on memory-backed ledger row -> Inspector Evidence tab (getWhy) -> sanctioned correct/forget/invalidate, phase node (ledger/retrieval/shared) -> right-pane ledger group highlight, inbound: Mission Control orrery phase-click + Stream 'which turn answered this' -> Workbench with selectedTurnId set, Prompt Context tab -> Prompt Lab
**Backend gaps needed:** Persisted per-turn phase traces: GET /api/turns/:id endpoint returning a stored TurnFlowSnapshot so replay works after the 64-turn LRU eviction or a process restart (today flow trace is live/cache-only)., Duration on turn-history rows: TurnHistoryRow has no duration_ms, so the history strip can only show duration for turns still in the flow cache., Unified open-object-by-id endpoint so the Universal Inspector can resolve arbitrary ledger citation ids without inferring kind from source_type/id-prefix.
**Scope verdict:** In-scope: all gaps are harness presentation/wiring (unwired flowSnapshotByTurn/ledgerByTurn caches, missing turn selector, no cross-pane provenance links, static composer mode text, disclosure fields shown as prose not labeled chips); zero recall-gating and zero output-policing introduced — disclosure/taint/scope are render-time labels on globally-recalled rows.

## Stream & Provenance — the durable source-of-truth explorer
# Stream & Provenance

## Purpose

The durable **source-of-truth explorer**. Where the Cognitive Orrery is the *live* turn-replay surface, Stream & Provenance is the *archival* surface: the append-only session stream as it was actually written, grouped into the turns that produced it, with full provenance drill-down and the canonical attachment viewer. It answers the proposal's operator questions: what happened, in what order, which entries were inactive/aborted/quarantined/compressed, what an attachment contained, and which turn answered which input.

It is explicitly a **log/provenance viewer**, not a cognition path. The cardinal memory rule's recall-gating prohibition is about what the *entity* may internally know; this screen renders an operator-facing append-only event log where audience/kind filtering selects *what rows are shown in the table*, exactly as the current `StreamScreen` already does. Audience filtering here is a view convenience over an already-emitted log, never a predicate that decides what the being remembers. All audience, source-key, taint, trust, and citation data is rendered as **render-time disclosure/provenance labels** on the rows, never used to hide a memory from the entity.

## Migration note (what current code becomes this, what is reused/deleted)

This screen evolves `demo/web/src/screens/Stream/index.tsx` (891 lines) in place — same `RouteId: "stream"`, same `<StreamScreen sessionId>` entry from `App.tsx`, same Rail slot. Nothing in `App.tsx` routing or `Rail.tsx` changes.

**Reused verbatim** (moved into the new structure, not rewritten):
- The entire per-kind summary renderer family already in `Stream/index.tsx`: `StreamRowSummary`, `ThoughtSummary`, `PerceptionSummary`, `DreamReportSummary`, `InternalEventSummary` (incl. the `autonomousWakeInfo` "wake" rendering), `ToolCallSummary`, `ToolResultSummary`, `attachmentSummary`, `messageSummary`, `structuralHint`, `textSnippet`, `StreamOutcomeTags`. These are extracted into a new `Stream/StreamRow.tsx` so the timeline and the inspector both consume them.
- `lib/stream-utils.ts` (`mergeEntries`, `sortStreamEntries`, `compareStreamEntries`, `formatTime`, `streamContentText`, `compactStreamText`) — unchanged, the merge/sort backbone.
- `lib/stream-outcomes.ts` (`streamOutcomeSummary`) — unchanged.
- `screen-utils.ts` (`shortId`, `fieldLabel`, `displayValue`, `jsonText`, `isRecord`, `contentField`, `dateLabel`) — unchanged.
- `components/ImagePlaceholder.tsx`, `components/Tag.tsx`, `components/AttachmentChip.tsx`, `components/Empty.tsx`, `components/SupersededByChip.tsx`.
- The live `stream:append` merge, reconnect-refetch-on-`connectionCount`, and per-session reset effects from the current screen — lifted into a new hook (below) unchanged in behavior.
- The CSS classes `.stream-screen`, `.stream-filters`, `.stream-main`, `.stream-detail`, `.stream-row`, `.opt`, `.att-card*` (styles.css lines ~2632-2780) stay; new lane/group classes are added alongside (no parallel vocabulary).

**Refactored:** the single `streamApi` window fetch becomes a small `Stream/use-stream-window.ts` hook (`useStreamWindow(sessionId, { audience, kinds, limit })`) wrapping `useApi(() => getStream(...))` + the existing live-merge/reconnect/reset effects, so the screen body shrinks to layout. The flat `filtered.map(...)` row loop is replaced by turn-grouping.

**Deleted:** nothing of substance — the current flat-list render loop (`Stream/index.tsx` lines 756-806) is replaced by the grouped timeline. The inline `style={{...}}` literals (e.g. `fontSize: 10.5`) are folded into the existing `.eyebrow`/token classes per the design-system hygiene note.

## Component breakdown

```
demo/web/src/screens/Stream/
  index.tsx                 (StreamScreen — refactored: owns layout + filter state + selection)
  use-stream-window.ts      (NEW — windowed fetch + live merge + reconnect/reset, extracted)
  StreamFilterRail.tsx      (NEW — left rail; all filter controls)
  StreamTimeline.tsx        (NEW — center; turn-grouped timeline + lanes)
  TurnGroup.tsx             (NEW — one collapsible turn block: header + entry rows)
  StreamRow.tsx             (NEW — one entry row; hosts the reused *Summary renderers)
  ProvenanceInspector.tsx   (NEW — right pane; normalized summary, metadata, raw, pivots)
  AttachmentViewer.tsx      (NEW — full attachment preview + perception fields + status)
  stream-grouping.ts        (NEW — pure: groupEntriesByTurn, lane assignment, client filters)
  stream-grouping.test.ts   (NEW — colocated vitest for the pure grouping/filter logic)
```

All new files use named exports, `useApi`/`useLiveEventsContext`, props-driven data (no globals), matching the build-tooling conventions.

## Left: Filter rail (`StreamFilterRail.tsx`)

Reuses the existing `.stream-filters > .group > .opt` pill vocabulary. Groups, top to bottom:

1. **stream** (readout): `append-only · {N} events · window counts` (existing).
2. **session**: a readonly chip showing the active `sessionId` (sourced from props; switching session is the app-level `SessionsSidebar`, not duplicated here). A "load older" affordance lives in the limit/cursor group.
3. **kinds**: the 11 `STREAM_KINDS` toggle chips with a colored `.dot` per `kindTag(kind)` and window count — unchanged from current. Maps to `getStream({ kinds })` (server-supported).
4. **audience**: derived `windowAudiences` toggle chips with counts — unchanged. Maps to `getStream({ audience })` when narrowed to one; multi-select stays client-side over the window. Labeled "view filter" so it reads as presentation, not gating.
5. **turn status / active-aborted**: two toggle `.opt` chips — `active` (`turn_status !== "aborted"`) and `aborted-turn` (`turn_status === "aborted"`) with counts. **Client-side** (no server param).
6. **structural flags** (client-side toggles, each with a window count): `has attachment` (`kind === "user_image_attachment"` OR `contentField(content,"attachment_id")` present — keyed on structure, not words), `has turn id` (`turn_id !== undefined`), `has source key` (`source_message_key !== undefined`), `compressed` (`compressed === true`). These are structural predicates on schema fields — language-agnostic.
7. **cursor / limit**: a numeric `limit` input (default 120) and a **"load older"** button that calls `getStream({ before: next_cursor })` and prepends the older page via `mergeEntries`. `next_cursor` from `StreamResponse` drives whether the button is enabled.

The rail uses `aria-label`/`role="button"` on every `.opt` and keeps the `.opt`/`.on` class hooks the existing test suite asserts against.

## Center: Grouped stream timeline (`StreamTimeline.tsx` + `TurnGroup.tsx` + `StreamRow.tsx`)

**Data shape choice: turn-grouped timeline, not a flat table.** Justified by the proposal's explicit "do not render this as one flat row list" and by the data: every emitted entry carries `turn_id` + `entry_index`, so the natural structure is `turn → ordered entries`. A flat list (current behavior) buries the causal arc (user_msg → perception → thought → tool_call → tool_result → agent_msg → internal_event). Grouping makes "which turn answered which input" answerable at a glance.

`stream-grouping.ts` exposes a pure `groupEntriesByTurn(entries, sortDir)`:
- Entries with a `turn_id` are bucketed by it; within a bucket sorted by `entry_index` then `compareStreamEntries`.
- Entries **without** `turn_id` go to a single synthetic **"unclaimed / queued / maintenance" lane** (the proposal's lane). This is keyed purely on `turn_id === undefined` — not on kind names or content words. (`dream_report`, autonomy `internal_event` wakes, and not-yet-claimed `user_msg`s naturally land here because they carry no turn id; we do not branch on their kind to route them.)
- Turn groups are ordered by their earliest entry timestamp (desc = newest turn first, matching current `"desc"` tailing), with the unclaimed lane interleaved by timestamp so a maintenance tick shows where it actually occurred.

**`TurnGroup` header** renders:
- `turn {shortId(turn_id)}` (the canonical pivot label; `font-variant: tabular-nums`).
- An outcome `Tag` derived from the group's terminal-ish entries: if any entry is `agent_suppressed` → `bad` "suppressed"; `agent_observed` → `warn` "observed"; any `agent_msg` → `acc` "replied"; else `warn` "open". (Derived from stream-entry *kinds* present, not from re-judging content.)
- A turn-status `Tag`: `aborted` (`bad`) if any entry has `turn_status === "aborted"`, else `active` (`acc`).
- Entry count + the time span (`formatTime(first)–formatTime(last)`).
- A `▸/▾` collapse toggle; collapsed groups show just the header + a one-line "{n} entries · {kinds present}" summary.
- A "open turn" pivot button (`SupersededByChip` style) → `onOpenObject({ kind: "turn", id: turn_id })`, and an "open ledger" pivot when `evidence_ledger:built` has been seen for that turn or `getLedger(turn_id)` resolves.

**`StreamRow`** (one entry) renders the existing per-kind summary (`StreamRowSummary`) and, per the proposal's "each entry row should show" list:
- `timestamp` (`formatTime`), `entry_index` (small `.idx` chip), `kind` (colored `.k`), compact content summary (reused renderers), `audience` (`.aud`, `"global"` when none).
- **Provenance micro-badges** rendered as `Tag`s (the disclosure-label surface): `sender`/`reply_target` short ids when present, `token_estimate` ("{n} tok") when present, `compressed` (`warn` "compressed") when true, `source_message_key` ("ext key" `info` chip) when present, `response_to` ("→ {n} src" chip) when `response_to.source_entry_ids` non-empty.
- **Attachment badge**: for `user_image_attachment`, the inline `ImagePlaceholder` (size `xs`) + quarantine `Tag` (existing behavior), with status pulled from the batch `getAttachmentStatuses` cache.
- Suppression/observed rows render `StreamOutcomeTags` (existing).
- Selection: clicking sets `selectedId`; the row keeps the `.stream-row`/`.selected` class hooks. The `.kind-attachment` modifier is preserved.

**Live state:** subscribes (via `use-stream-window.ts`) to `stream:append`; new entries merge into their turn group (or the unclaimed lane) with the existing `isNew` flash treatment available via the `.alive`/pulse vocabulary. A turn group that gains an `agent_msg` re-derives its outcome tag live. `maintenance:tick` and `borg:reset` are handled as today (tick → optional refetch; reset → clear + refetch, no `window.location.reload` needed locally since `App.tsx` already handles global reset).

## Right: Provenance inspector (`ProvenanceInspector.tsx`)

Reuses the current `.stream-detail` detail-pane structure and extends it to the proposal's provenance list. Sections for a selected entry:

1. **header**: `[{id}]`, ISO timestamp · kind · audience, and a status `Tag` (`active`/`aborted`/`quarantined`) + session/turn `Tag`s (existing `det-head`).
2. **normalized summary**: the same `StreamRowSummary` render used in the row (so the inspector and the row agree).
3. **metadata / provenance block** (the existing `pre` provenance block, expanded to a labeled `.props` key/value grid): `source_kind`, `session`, `turn`, `audience`, `sender`, `reply_target`, `status`, plus `entry_index`, `token_estimate`, `compressed`, `persistence_class` (e.g. `assistant_self_report`).
4. **source message key**: when present, `source_type` / `source_external_id` / `external_message_id` rows — the external-platform provenance.
5. **response_to sources**: when `response_to.source_entry_ids` is present, render each as a clickable stream-entry chip that selects that entry in-screen (intra-screen pivot) and offers `onOpenObject({ kind: "stream_entry", id })`.
6. **related turn**: "open turn {short}" + "open ledger" pivots.
7. **related ledger** (inline-expandable): when a ledger exists for the entry's `turn_id` (from the live `ledgerByTurn` cache via context, or lazy `getLedger`), render a compact section list reusing the **`LedgerView` from `screens/Cognition/LedgerView.tsx`** (it already renders `EvidenceLedger` with trust/taint/citation tags and `AttachmentChip`s). Each ledger entry's `source_type`/`id` (`episode`, `semantic_node`, `semantic_edge`, `commitment`, `relational_slot`, `action_record`, `shared_state`) becomes a pivot into the Universal Inspector — this is the bridge "which memories did this turn read."
8. **related attachment**: when the entry references an `attachment_id`, the **Attachment viewer** mounts here (below).
9. **related memory records (if known)**: surfaced indirectly via the ledger entries above and via `response_to`; we do **not** fabricate a memory-derivation index the backend doesn't expose (see gaps).
10. **raw content** + **raw JSON**: the existing `<pre>{streamContentText}</pre>` and `<pre>{jsonText}</pre>` blocks.

## Attachment viewer (`AttachmentViewer.tsx`)

For `user_image_attachment` (and any entry carrying `attachment_id`). Lazy `getAttachmentMetadata(id)` (already wired) + batch `getAttachmentStatuses` for visible ids (already wired). Renders, per the proposal:

- **Preview**: `<img>` via `attachmentBytesUrl(id, audience)` (audience-scoped, verified) with an `ImagePlaceholder` fallback while loading / on error / when quarantined; `width × height` from `AttachmentRecord`.
- **Metadata** (`.props` grid): `width`, `height`, `media_type`, `byte_size` (humanized), `sha256` (short + copyable), `storage_ref`/`thumbnail_ref` presence, `created_turn_global`, `parent_turn_id`, `parent_entry_id`, `stream_entry_id`.
- **Perception** (every `ImagePerceptionRecord` field, each rendered as a labeled block or `Tag` cluster — language-agnostic, we render whatever strings the model produced): `caption`, `image_kind`, `visible_text[]`, `objects[]`, `people_or_roles[]`, `scene`, `colors_and_visual_attributes[]`, `spatial_relationships[]`, `possible_user_relevant_details[]`, `uncertainties[]`, `search_terms[]`, plus `embedding_status` (`pending`/`complete`/`failed`) as a `Tag`. Empty arrays render a faint "none" rather than being hidden, so the operator sees coverage gaps.
- **Status** (4 flags as `Tag`s, the proposal's set): `active`, `quarantined` (`bad` when true, shows "quarantine cascade"), `stream_active`, `parent_active`. These are the attachment's disclosure/lifecycle labels.
- Pivots: "open parent turn", "open stream entry" (`stream_entry_id`), "open perception" (`perception_id` as a `system_metadata`-class object in the inspector).

## Cross-link pivots (into/out of the Universal Inspector)

This screen is **the canonical deep-link target** for stream-id and turn-id chips emitted elsewhere (Governance's `source_stream_entry_ids`, Review's episode/edge refs, Dream's audit targets, Cognition chat-message turn ids). It accepts an optional `initialSelection?: { kind: "stream_entry" | "turn"; id: string }` prop so other screens can deep-link a row.

It emits pivots via a generic `onOpenObject?: (ref: { kind: string; id: string }) => void` prop (the Universal Inspector contract other subagents are defining — kept as a thin callback so this screen doesn't depend on the inspector's internals, mirroring the existing `onOpenReview` callback-passing pattern). Pivots exposed:
- **From a stream entry**: open turn, open ledger, open attachment, open `response_to` source entries (intra-screen select), open source-message-key origin.
- **From a turn group**: open turn (full turn object), open ledger, open each ledger source (`episode`/`semantic_node`/`semantic_edge`/`commitment`/`relational_slot`/`action_record`/`shared_state`).
- **From an attachment**: open parent turn, open stream entry, open perception.

Where the Universal Inspector isn't yet present, `onOpenObject` defaults to the **intra-screen** behavior (select the entry / scroll to the turn group), so the screen is fully functional standalone.

## States

- **Loading**: `streamApi.loading && entries.length === 0` → `.notice` "loading stream" (existing). Per-pane: ledger/attachment lazy loads show a small inline "loading" `.notice`.
- **Empty**: no entries → `Empty` "no stream entries in window". A turn group with only suppressed/observed rows still renders (it's meaningful). Empty perception arrays render "none". `windowAudiences.length === 0` → "none in window" (existing).
- **Error**: `streamApi.error` → `.notice.bad` with `error.message` (existing). Ledger 404 → graceful "ledger not retained pre-restart" (existing `LedgerView` behavior). Attachment fetch failure → `ImagePlaceholder` + "perception unavailable" (existing).
- **Live / tailing**: `.stream-main-head` shows the `.live-dot` + "tailing" (existing); new appends flash via the `.alive` pulse vocabulary. Reconnect → refetch window (existing `connectionCount` effect). `borg:reset` → clear all state + refetch.

## Opus 5.0 / cardinal-rule compliance

- **No recall gating**: filters select rows shown in an append-only operator log; they never gate what the entity may internally know. Audience is a presentation/ranking axis here, explicitly labeled "view filter."
- **No output policing**: read-only inspection + pivots only; no rewriting/suppression of entity output. (Corrections live in Review & Repair / Memory Atlas, not here.)
- **Language-agnostic**: every filter and routing decision keys on schema fields (`turn_id`, `kind`, `turn_status`, `compressed`, `source_message_key`, `attachment_id`) — never on matching words in `content`. The per-kind summaries render whatever strings the model produced without judging them.
- **Disclosure as labels**: audience, taint, trust, citation_type, source-key, quarantine all render as render-time `Tag`s on the rows, never as recall predicates.
**New files:** demo/web/src/screens/Stream/use-stream-window.ts, demo/web/src/screens/Stream/StreamFilterRail.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Stream/StreamTimeline.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Stream/TurnGroup.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Stream/StreamRow.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Stream/ProvenanceInspector.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Stream/AttachmentViewer.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Stream/stream-grouping.ts, demo/web/src/screens/Stream/stream-grouping.test.ts
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/screens/Stream/index.tsx (refactored in place: keeps StreamScreen entry, hosts the extracted *Summary renderers), demo/web/src/lib/stream-utils.ts (mergeEntries/sortStreamEntries/compareStreamEntries/formatTime/streamContentText/compactStreamText), demo/web/src/lib/stream-outcomes.ts (streamOutcomeSummary), demo/web/src/screens/screen-utils.ts (shortId/fieldLabel/displayValue/jsonText/isRecord/contentField/dateLabel), demo/web/src/screens/Cognition/LedgerView.tsx (inline related-ledger render in the inspector), demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx, demo/web/src/components/ImagePlaceholder.tsx, demo/web/src/components/AttachmentChip.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Empty.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SupersededByChip.tsx (pivot-chip primitive), demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx (useLiveEventsContext), demo/web/src/styles.css (.stream-screen/.stream-filters/.stream-main/.stream-detail/.stream-row/.opt/.att-card vocabulary)
**API bindings:** GET /api/stream, GET /api/turns, GET /api/turns/:id/ledger, GET /api/attachments, GET /api/attachments/:id, GET /api/attachments/:id/bytes, GET /api/reviews
**Live frames:** stream:append, evidence_ledger:built, maintenance:tick, borg:reset
**Cross-link pivots:** out: open turn (turn_id) -> Universal Inspector / Cognitive Orrery, out: open evidence ledger (turn_id) -> inline LedgerView + Universal Inspector, out: open ledger source (episode/semantic_node/semantic_edge/commitment/relational_slot/action_record/shared_state) -> Universal Inspector / Memory Atlas, out: open attachment (attachment_id) -> Attachment viewer, out: open perception (perception_id) -> Universal Inspector (system_metadata), intra: response_to.source_entry_ids -> select source stream entry in-screen, out: source_message_key external origin chip, in: deep-link target for stream-id / turn-id chips from Governance (source_stream_entry_ids), Review (episode/edge refs), Dream (audit targets), Cognition (chat-message turn id) via initialSelection prop
**Backend gaps needed:** Server-side turn-status filter on GET /api/stream (only session/audience/kinds/limit/before are supported; active/aborted is client-side over the loaded window), Server-side structural filters on GET /api/stream: has-attachment, has-turn-id, has-source-message-key, compressed (none supported; all client-side over the window), Stream date-range filtering (no from/to/since param on GET /api/stream; only a before-cursor exists -> date filtering is client-side over the window), Stream full-text / content search (GET /api/stream has no search/query param; the proposal's 'search stream' action has no backend -> client-side substring over the loaded window only, or a real gap for cross-window search), Forward-pagination / after-cursor (only before-cursor pagination exists; tailing-then-loading-older works, but jumping forward from an old anchor does not), Reviews-by-turn lookup (GET /api/reviews supports only openOnly/kind, no turn_id filter -> 'related reviews for this turn' must client-side scan the open queue or is a gap), Memory-records-derived-from-entry index (no endpoint maps a stream entry id to the episodes/semantic nodes it produced; the proposal's 'related memory records if known' is only inferable via the per-turn ledger sources and response_to, not a direct derivation lookup)
