## Memory Atlas — the 8-band global-recall memory explorer (evolves Memory + SemanticTopology)
## Memory Atlas — full design

### Purpose
Make the entity's *derived substrate* legible: what it remembers, how memory is distributed across the eight bands, which memories are active/contested/contradicted/quarantined, what evidence backs each, and what an operator may safely correct. It evolves the current `screens/Memory/index.tsx` (a uniform 8-band card grid + one generic three-pane `MemoryDrill`) and absorbs `screens/Memory/SemanticTopology.tsx` as the semantic band's graph engine.

**Cardinal framing (non-negotiable).** The Atlas surfaces *global recall* — every band lists everything the entity knows. `audience`, origin, `trust`, `taint`, `state`, `confidence` are **render-time labels + ranking/annotation axes**, never recall filters. There is no audience/session `WHERE` clause anywhere in the Atlas. The "audience" control (see Atlas controls) is a *label highlight + optional sort boost*, explicitly captioned "ranking only — recall is global." This is the single most important design constraint and is asserted in tests (no audience param is ever threaded into `getMemoryBand`).

---

### Component breakdown

#### New files (all under `demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/`)
- **`index.tsx`** — `export function MemoryAtlasScreen({ sessionId, audience, now, onOpenInspector, onOpenReview, onOpenIdentity, onOpenGovernance })`. Owns: `activeBand` state, `selectedId`, the bands-overview fetch (`useApi(getMemoryBands)`), the active-band fetch (`useApi(() => getMemoryBand(activeBand, {...}))`), live tick subscription, and the correction-action runner (lifted from `MemoryDrill`'s `runMemoryAction`). Layout: top `BandOverviewBar` (full width), then a grid `var(--sidebar) minmax(0,1fr) 320px` = AtlasControls / active band view / MemoryInspector — reusing the `.band-detail` grid template already in `styles.css`.
- **`BandOverviewBar.tsx`** — the 8 `BandOverviewCard`s in `BAND_ORDER`. Replaces the landing `bands-grid`. Each card: band glyph + name; `count` with `+` suffix when `count_is_lower_bound`; first two `band.stats` as k/v; a `Spark` of `band.growth`; a health `Tag` (derived structurally — see "health"); latest-update relative time (from band `stats` if present, else omitted); a review-count `Tag` when a related-review count is available (currently a backend gap — rendered only when present). Card click sets `activeBand`. Reuses existing `.band-card` class (test contract) + `Spark`.
- **`AtlasControls.tsx`** — left rail. Band selector (vertical list, reuses `.list`/`.list-row` selectable pattern). Below it, structural filters wired per band via the existing `FilterPillGroup` vocabulary lifted from `MemoryStructuralControls`: query (episodic/semantic/procedural only — `SEARCHABLE_BANDS`), status/state pills, confidence range, source-count range, updated/created range, "has contradiction" (relational `contradicted_count>0` / semantic `status==='contradicted'`), "manual-correction-possible" (id-prefix-correctable). The **audience control is here, captioned "label highlight + sort boost — recall is global, never filtered."**
- **`bands/EpisodicView.tsx`** — vertical-time-axis episode cards (see Episodic).
- **`bands/SemanticView.tsx`** — 5-mode graph explorer wrapper around `SemanticTopology` (see Semantic).
- **`bands/ProceduralView.tsx`** — skill cards with Beta-posterior bar (see Procedural).
- **`bands/AffectiveView.tsx`** — valence/arousal plane + time trail (see Affective).
- **`bands/SelfView.tsx`** — compact self snapshot, deep-links Identity (see Self).
- **`bands/CommitmentsView.tsx`** — compact commitment matrix, deep-links Governance (see Commitments).
- **`bands/SocialView.tsx`** — entity cards + trust×attachment scatterplot (see Social).
- **`bands/RelationalView.tsx`** — evidence-fact table + conflict lens (see Relational).
- **`MemoryInspector.tsx`** — right pane; thin wrapper that, for the selected row, renders the universal-inspector contract. For correctable ids (`ep_`/`semn_`/`seme_`) it shows the sanctioned actions; for others it shows "no in-Atlas correction action for this id type" + a `requiresBackendGap` note. Delegates to `onOpenInspector(objectRef)` for the full drawer.
- **`band-views.ts`** — pure helpers: `bandHealth(detail|summary)` (structural health classifier), `episodeSalienceScale()`, `betaPosteriorBar(alpha,beta)` (mean + 90% credible interval), `valenceArousalToXY()`, `scatterScale()`. Colocated `band-views.test.ts`.

#### Shared component promotions / new shared widgets (under `demo/web/src/components/`)
- **`SemanticEdgeDetail.tsx`** — *promote* the function currently local to `screens/Review/index.tsx:515` into a shared component (Review imports it back). Reused by SemanticView's edge inspector and the Universal Inspector. Mechanical extraction, no behavior change.
- **`MemoryCorrectionPanel.tsx`** — *extract* the duplicated correction modal + `why/forget/correct/invalidate` flow that today exists near-identically in `Memory/index.tsx` and `Identity/index.tsx`. Props: `{ id, correctionKind, defaultPatch, onDone }`. Backs the inspector's Actions tab in both Atlas and Studio. Reuses `Modal`, `.btn`, the JSON-patch textarea, `postCorrectionForget/Correct`, `postSemanticEdgeInvalidate`.

#### Existing files reused as-is
- `screens/Memory/SemanticTopology.tsx` — the force-graph engine, consumed by `SemanticView` unchanged (props `graph/selectedId/onSelectNode`). It already self-refetches on `maintenance:tick`.
- `components/SemanticNodeDetail.tsx` — node detail card in SemanticView + inspector.
- `components/WhyDrawer.tsx` + `getWhy` — the universal "why?" affordance across all bands.
- `components/{Panel,Tag,Modal,Empty,Spark}.tsx`, `.btn`/`.tbl`/`.dot`/`.pill`/`.list` CSS, oklch tokens, `useApi`, `useLiveEventsContext`.
- `hooks/use-api.ts` for all fetches; `api/client.ts` `getMemoryBands/getMemoryBand/getSemanticGraph/getSemanticNode/getSemanticEdge/getWhy/postCorrection*/getReviews`.

#### Deleted / migrated
- `screens/Memory/index.tsx` is **replaced**. Its valuable internals are not thrown away: `BAND_ORDER`, `BAND_DESCRIPTIONS`, `correctionActionKind`, `defaultMemoryPatch`, `SEARCHABLE_BANDS`, `FilterPillGroup`/`MemoryStructuralControls`, `sortedRows`/`structuralFilterRows`, `runMemoryAction`, `refetchAfterMemoryCorrection`, and the per-band `switch` logic in `detailRows`/`BandSpecificDetail` are **lifted and split** into the per-band view modules and `band-views.ts` (the `detailRows` switch is the natural seam — each `case` becomes a typed view consuming `MemoryBandDetail` directly instead of flattening to the lossy `MemoryRow`). The generic `MemoryDrill` three-pane and `BandCard` are removed in favor of `BandOverviewBar` + specialized views. `Rail.tsx` `RouteId` `"memory"` now mounts `MemoryAtlasScreen`; the `App.tsx` conditional-render line swaps the import.
- The landing-page `CorrectionReviewSummaryPanel` and static "identity governance" panel move out of Memory: the correction-review count becomes a band-card review `Tag`; identity governance belongs to Identity Studio / Governance Studio.

---

### Per-band data presentation (justified by the proposal's data-shape rules)

**Band overview header (all 8).** Cards, not a table — narrative status records, per "object cards for narrative records / count badges + sparklines for status." `health` is derived **structurally**, never by inspecting content text: episodic `health=ok`; semantic `warn` if any `status∈{contested,contradicted}` else `bad` if `quarantined`; procedural `warn` if any `requires_manual_review`; affective neutral; relational `bad` if any `state∈{contested,quarantined,revoked}`; commitments by superseded/revoked ratio. Language-agnostic by construction.

**Episodic → timeline + detail cards** (`EpisodeMemoryItem[]`, paginated via `next_cursor`). Vertical time axis; each card = `title`, `narrative`, `participants`, `audience` *(label tag)*, `start_time–end_time`, `significance`, `confidence`, `source_count`, `tags`, `lineage.{derived_from,supersedes}` as jump-chips, `vector_dims`, `source_stream_ids` as provenance chips. Salience drives card size/brightness (`significance`→`episodeSalienceScale`). `load more` paginates (real for episodic). Time-based + narrative ⇒ timeline of cards, per proposal.

**Semantic → graph explorer, 5 modes** (`SemanticMemoryNode[]`+`SemanticMemoryEdge[]`; graph via `getSemanticGraph(limit=300)`). Mode tabs reuse the `semanticViewMode` toggle pattern already in `MemoryDrill`:
- *topology* — `SemanticTopology` force-graph (glyph by `kind`, color by `status`, radius∝`edge_count`, edge CSS by `SemanticRelation`, typed-edge legend). Selection two-way-bound to inspector.
- *table* — `.tbl` of nodes (label/kind/status/confidence/source_count/domain/aliases/edge_count) — relationships-as-rows for scanning.
- *neighborhood* — `getSemanticNode(id)` + filter graph to its edges (1-hop); renders `SemanticNodeDetail` + neighbor list.
- *contradiction* — filter to `status∈{contested,contradicted}` nodes and `contradicts`/invalidated edges; surfaces `invalidated_{at,by_edge_id,by_review_id,by_process,reason}`.
- *source-evidence* — selected node/edge → its `source_episode_ids`/`evidence_episode_ids` as episode jump-chips (pivots into Episodic).
- Actions on edges: **invalidate-edge** (`postSemanticEdgeInvalidate` with reason+`at`) and **why?** (`getWhy`). Graph because relationships are the data, per proposal.

**Procedural → skill cards** (`ProceduralMemoryItem[]`). Card = `applies_when`, `approach`, `status`, `successes`/`failures`/`attempts`, `sample_count`, `last_used`, `last_successful`, `requires_manual_review` badge. **Beta posterior bar**: `betaPosteriorBar(alpha,beta)` renders mean `α/(α+β)` as a filled bar with a 90% credible-interval whisker — visualizes reliability + uncertainty, not just a ratio. "requires review" → `warn` Tag.

**Affective → valence/arousal plane + time trail** (`MoodSnapshot current` + `MoodHistoryEntry[] history`). 2-axis plane (x=valence −1..1, y=arousal 0..1) with current point glowing (phosphor green), history as a fading time-trail polyline (`valenceArousalToXY`), trigger annotations from `trigger_reason`/`recent_triggers`. Side panel: `half_life_hours`, recent triggers. `provenance` rendered as labeled k/v, not raw JSON. Replaces today's flat point-list. Timeline + plane because mood is time-series over a 2D space.

**Self → compact snapshot, deep-links Identity** (`IdentityResponse` shape). A read-only summary card (values/goals/traits/open_questions/growth_markers counts + current autobiographical period + most recent identity event) with a prominent "Open Identity Studio" pivot (`onOpenIdentity`). The Atlas deliberately does **not** re-flatten identity (that's Studio's job) — avoids the current Memory/Identity duplication.

**Commitments → compact matrix, deep-links Governance** (`CommitmentItem[]`). A compact matrix: rows = `directive_family` (or "global"), cells = state counts (active/superseded/revoked/expired) as `Tag`s, with critical-vs-advisory split. Matrix because it's scope/lifecycle data. "Open Governance Studio" pivot (`onOpenGovernance`) for full lifecycle/supersession chains.

**Social → entity cards + trust×attachment scatterplot** (`SocialMemoryItem[]`). Cards (name/`trust`/`attachment`/`interaction_count`/`commitment_count`/`history_count`/`last_interaction_at`/`updated_at`) AND a scatterplot: x=`trust`, y=`attachment`, size∝`interaction_count`, color = recent vs stale (by `last_interaction_at` vs `now` — a *time* classification, not a content match). Cards for profiles + graph/plane for the relational structure, per proposal.

**Relational → evidence-fact table + conflict lens** (`RelationalMemoryItem[]` + `counts`). `.tbl`: subject / slot_key / value / state / sources_count / contradicted_count / alternate_count / name_provenance. **Conflict lens** = a toggle filtering rows to `state∈{contested,quarantined,revoked}` OR `contradicted_count>0` OR `alternate_count>0`, surfacing alternates side-by-side. Table because relational slots are tabular, per the proposal's explicit "relational slots → table" rule.

---

### Right Memory Inspector
Always present; universal-inspector contract: **Summary** (typed per band), **Evidence** (`source_stream_ids`/`source_episode_ids`/`evidence_episode_ids` as jump-chips), **Relationships** (lineage/edges/alternates), **Actions** (sanctioned correction only), **Raw JSON**. Actions via `MemoryCorrectionPanel`: `why?` (all ids), then for `ep_`/`semn_`/`seme_` → forget/correct (JSON-patch + reason, queues a correction review) / invalidate-edge. For other id types: disabled with an explicit gap note. Disclosure labels (`audience`, `taint` where present on the row, `state`, `confidence`, `trust_rank` on ledger-sourced views) render as `Tag`s **at view time** — shown, never used to hide the row.

---

### Cross-link pivots (into/out of Universal Inspector)
- **Out of Atlas:** episode card → source stream entries + derived semantic nodes/edges + autobiographical period; semantic node → evidence episodes + supporting/contradicting edges + related reviews; relational row → subject entity + alternates + reviews; any row → `onOpenInspector({type, id})`; Self → Identity Studio; Commitments → Governance Studio; review `Tag` → `onOpenReview()`.
- **Into Atlas:** the Cognitive Orrery's "click memory ring → open band"; the Universal Inspector's "open in source screen" for any band-id; the Conversation Workbench ledger entry (`source_type` episode/semantic_node/edge/relational_slot) → deep-link into the matching band + select id.
- Pivot mechanism mirrors the existing cross-screen callback pattern (`onOpenReview`/`onOpenIdentity`/`onOpenGovernance`/`onOpenInspector`) threaded from `App.tsx` — no router needed.

---

### States
- **Loading:** band overview shows skeleton cards (count placeholders); active view shows `Empty`-style "loading band…". Reuses `useApi.loading`.
- **Empty:** zero-count band → `Empty` notice ("no <band> memories yet"); conflict lens with no conflicts → "no contested/quarantined facts"; graph with 0 nodes → topology empty state (already handled by `SemanticTopology`).
- **Error:** `useApi.error` → `Empty` with error text + `refetch` button (mirrors existing screens).
- **Live:** subscribe to `maintenance:tick` and `dream:process:completed` (consolidator/curator/semantic-extractor/belief-reviser etc.) → refetch band overview + active band detail; `SemanticTopology` already self-refreshes on tick. `borg:reset` handled globally by `App.tsx` (reload). New-memory insertion gets a brief flash on the affected card/row (reuse the `pulse` keyframe), not constant animation.

---

### Migration note
`screens/Memory/` → `screens/MemoryAtlas/`. `MemoryScreen` deleted; `MemoryAtlasScreen` mounted on the same `RouteId "memory"`. `SemanticTopology.tsx` moves under MemoryAtlas (or stays and is imported) unchanged. The lossy `MemoryRow` normalization is removed — views consume `MemoryBandDetail` variants directly. The duplicated correction modal becomes shared `MemoryCorrectionPanel`; Review's local `SemanticEdgeDetail` becomes shared `components/SemanticEdgeDetail.tsx`. Tests: keep the `.band-card` class hook and add `data-testid` per band view; preserve accessible names on action buttons (suite asserts `getByRole("button",{name})`). No new runtime deps; keep custom routing + `useApi`/`LiveEventsProvider`.
**New files:** demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/index.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/BandOverviewBar.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/AtlasControls.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/MemoryInspector.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/band-views.ts, demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/band-views.test.ts, demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/bands/EpisodicView.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/bands/SemanticView.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/bands/ProceduralView.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/bands/AffectiveView.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/bands/SelfView.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/bands/CommitmentsView.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/bands/SocialView.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/bands/RelationalView.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SemanticEdgeDetail.tsx, demo/web/src/components/MemoryCorrectionPanel.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/MemoryAtlas/MemoryAtlas.test.tsx
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/screens/Memory/SemanticTopology.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SemanticNodeDetail.tsx, demo/web/src/components/WhyDrawer.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Panel.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Modal.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Empty.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Spark.tsx, demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx, demo/web/src/api/client.ts, demo/web/src/api/types.ts (MemoryBandDetail union + per-band item types), demo/web/src/screens/Memory/index.tsx (BAND_ORDER, BAND_DESCRIPTIONS, correctionActionKind, defaultMemoryPatch, SEARCHABLE_BANDS, FilterPillGroup/MemoryStructuralControls, runMemoryAction — lifted into the new modules), demo/web/src/screens/Review/index.tsx (SemanticEdgeDetail — promoted to shared), demo/web/src/styles.css (.band-card, .band-detail grid, .tbl, .list, .pill, oklch tokens, pulse keyframe)
**API bindings:** GET /api/memory/bands, GET /api/memory/bands/:id, GET /api/semantic/graph, GET /api/semantic/nodes/:id, GET /api/semantic/edges/:id, GET /api/correction/:id/why, POST /api/correction/:id/forget, POST /api/correction/:id/correct, POST /api/correction/semantic-edges/:id/invalidate, GET /api/correction/reviews, GET /api/reviews
**Live frames:** maintenance:tick, dream:process:completed, dream:process:started, borg:reset
**Cross-link pivots:** episode → source stream entries (Stream & Provenance), episode → derived semantic nodes/edges (Semantic band), episode → autobiographical period (Identity Studio), semantic node → evidence episodes (Episodic band), semantic node/edge → related reviews (Review & Repair via onOpenReview), relational fact → subject entity + alternates (Universal Inspector), Self snapshot → Identity Studio (onOpenIdentity), Commitments matrix → Governance Studio (onOpenGovernance), any band row → Universal Inspector (onOpenInspector), Cognitive Orrery memory ring → open band (inbound), Conversation Workbench ledger entry → band + select id (inbound)
**Backend gaps needed:** Per-item disclosure label on band items (origin_audience vs current, private/operator flag) — band item types carry audience+source ids but no explicit disclosure label; today only the evidence ledger carries trust_rank/taint/citation_type. Atlas renders what is present and must not invent an audience filter., Correction (why/forget/correct) for procedural/affective/social/relational/commitments ids — correctionActionKind only routes ep_/semn_/seme_; other band ids have no correction endpoint, so the inspector disables actions for them., Pagination + search for social/relational/affective/commitments bands — only episodic/semantic paginate via next_cursor and only episodic/semantic/procedural are searchable; large bands load one page., Band-level health + related-review-count rollup on /api/memory/bands — health is derived client-side from item status today; a review-count per band and a 'latest update' timestamp are not in MemoryBandSummary (review Tag only renders when a count is available)., Semantic neighborhood/contradiction/source-evidence modes need a per-node neighborhood or by-status edge query — currently rebuilt client-side from the 300-node graph snapshot + getSemanticNode; does not scale to large graphs.
**Scope verdict:** Opus 5.0: in-scope — data-shape-appropriate presentation of globally-recalled substrate with disclosure labels rendered at view time; no recall gating (audience is label+ranking only) and no output policing (correction is the sanctioned why/forget/correct/invalidate path, never rewriting model output).
---

## Identity Studio -- the self-model studio (evolves the Identity screen)
## Identity Studio -- self-model studio

### Purpose

Make Borg's self-model **understandable and governable** by an operator: what values it holds (established vs candidate), which goals are active/blocked/done/abandoned, which traits are established vs candidate, which questions remain open (with rumination pressure), how growth and the autobiography are evolving, and what changed recently and why. It replaces the current flat `IdentityScreen` 12-column card dump with a five-region studio that gives each self-model facet a fit-for-shape presentation, while preserving the screen's two-track write model (sanctioned direct self-band writes vs correction-queue proposals).

**Architectural posture (cardinal rules).** Identity rows are the being's *global self-knowledge*; this screen NEVER filters or hides a value/goal/trait/question/marker/period by current audience/session. `source`, `source_process`, and event `provenance` render as **origin/disclosure LABELS** (Tag chips + the `why?` drawer), never as recall gates. There is no deterministic scoring or rewriting of model output here; the only mutations are the sanctioned identity-lifecycle writes and correction routes. All grouping/sorting keys on enums and ids (`state`, `status`, `record_type`, `category`, `goal_id`) -- never on words in free-text fields -- so it is fully language-agnostic.

---

### Migration note (what this becomes)

- **`demo/web/src/screens/Identity/index.tsx` is the screen this evolves.** The flat `<div className="identity">` 12-col grid (hero + goals span-5 + open-questions span-7 + traits span-5 + oq-events span-7 + values span-5 + growth span-7 + periods span-12) is **restructured** into the five regions below. The route id `"identity"` in `components/Rail.tsx` and the `IdentityScreen` named export are **kept** (no router change; conditional render in `App.tsx` stays).
- **Reused verbatim from the current screen** (moved into sub-files, not rewritten): `DirectWriteNotice` + `directWriteAcknowledged` gate, `DirectPatchLabel`/`DirectPatchNotice`, `IdentityCorrectionButtons`, `OpenQuestionEventsSection` (generalized -- see below), `IdentityEmptyBand`, the `eventActionTag`/`provenanceLabel`/`eventRecordSummary`/`shortId`/`clamp01` helpers, the lifecycle modals (resolve/abandon/bump/complete/block/progress), and the create modals (`+ value`/`+ goal`/`+ growth`). The `identity-direct-write-zone` + ack-checkbox flow is preserved exactly (it is the sanctioned host-boundary write path).
- **Removed/superseded:** the single flat grid layout and the redundant `self`-band rendering that Memory's `MemoryDrill` also flattens (per Memory Atlas: self-band should defer to Studio). The current `OpenQuestionEventsSection` rendered once at screen bottom over all events is **split** into per-section history feeds (still one fetch, filtered by `record_type`).
- **Shared-component extraction (also used by Memory Atlas):** `IdentityCorrectionButtons` + the correct/forget modal + `WhyDrawer` are factored into a shared `components/CorrectionButtons.tsx` / reuse of existing `components/WhyDrawer.tsx`, so Atlas and Studio stop diverging. This is the highest-leverage de-dup noted in ground truth.

---

### Component breakdown

**New files (all under `demo/web/src/screens/Identity/`):**

- `index.tsx` (refactor of existing) -- owns `useApi(getIdentity, [])`, the `directWriteAcknowledged` state, modal state, busy state, and `refetchAfterIdentityAction`. Subscribes via `useLiveEventsContext().subscribe` to `maintenance:tick` and `dream:process:completed` to `refetch()` (self-narrator / ruminator / belief-reviser write the self-band offline -- mirrors how `SemanticTopology` refetches on tick). Renders the five regions; threads `onOpenInspector(ref)` callback down.
- `SelfSnapshot.tsx` -- the header card (counts + current period + most recent event) and the optional **SelfRings** SVG.
- `SelfRings.tsx` -- the optional concentric-ring viz (gated behind a toggle pill; off by default for first ship).
- `ValuesBoard.tsx` -- two card groups (established / candidate).
- `GoalsKanban.tsx` -- four lanes.
- `TraitsBoard.tsx` -- trait cards.
- `OpenQuestionQueue.tsx` -- the priority queue + selected-question event timeline.
- `GrowthTimeline.tsx` -- growth markers timeline + autobiographical-period timeline.
- `identity-events.ts` -- pure helper: `eventsForRecord(events, recordType, recordId?)` (filter `IdentityEvent[]` by `record_type` and optional `record_id`), plus the moved `eventActionTag`/`provenanceLabel`/`eventRecordSummary` helpers. This is the structural seam that lets one `open_question_events` feed power every section's history.

**Reused existing components (real paths):**
- `components/Tag.tsx` (`TagKind` acc/warn/bad/info/purple/solid) -- all state/urgency/confidence chips.
- `components/Modal.tsx` -- create + lifecycle + correct + forget modals.
- `components/Empty.tsx` -- per-region empty states.
- `components/Spark.tsx` -- growth-cadence sparkline in the snapshot.
- `components/Panel.tsx` (`.panel`/`.panel-header`/`.panel-body`) -- region containers.
- `components/WhyDrawer.tsx` + `getWhy` -- universal provenance drawer (the disclosure-label surface).
- `components/SupersededByChip.tsx` -- the established jump-to-object chip pattern, reused for the goal-link pivot on open questions and for `goal_id`->goal navigation.
- CSS vocabulary: `.id-card`, `.timeline .ev`, `.meter`/meter-fill (confidence/urgency/strength), `.btn sm`/`.btn sm ghost`/`.btn live-write`, `.eyebrow`, `.tab-num`, `.divider`, the oklch signal tokens. Goal lanes reuse the `.list`/`.list-row` selectable-with-glowing-left-bar pattern rather than inventing kanban CSS.

---

### Data presentation (per region, with justification)

**1. Self snapshot header (`SelfSnapshot`).** A wide stat card (full width). Left: count stats -- `values.length` (split established/candidate), active-goal count (`goals.filter(status==="active")`), established-trait count, `open_questions` open count, `growth_markers.length`. Center/right: current autobiographical period = the period with `end_ts === null` (or max `start_ts`) -- label + date range + truncated narrative. Footer: **most recent identity event** = `open_question_events` sorted by `ts` desc, first row, rendered as `action` Tag + `shortId(record_id)` + relative time. A `Spark` of growth-marker cadence (markers bucketed by week) sits beside the counts. *Why a stat card, not a table:* this is a fixed, small, heterogeneous summary -- the proposal's "large self-state card" -- and matches the existing hero pattern.

**Optional Self Rings (`SelfRings`, toggle-gated).** A single SVG (~360x360) of concentric rings on a graphite field, drawn with the same oklch tokens and pulse vocabulary as the flow canvas (`@keyframes pulse`, `drop-shadow` glow). Inner ring = values (arc segments sized by `priority`, color by `state`: established=`--acc`, candidate=`--text-mute`, opacity by `confidence`). Next ring = goals (segment color by `status`: active=`--acc`, blocked=`--warn`, done=`--info`, abandoned=`--text-faint`). Next ring = traits (segment opacity by `confidence`, color by `state`). Outer ring = open questions + growth markers (open questions as ticks sized by `urgency`; growth markers as dots at angular position by `ts`). **Pulse on recent change:** any object whose id appears in an `IdentityEvent` within the last N minutes (derived from the events feed `ts`, not wall-clock guessing) gets a transient glow. Clicking any segment opens that object in the Universal Inspector. This is the "wow" element; it is purely a *presentation* of `/api/identity` data, decorative-but-bound, and ships behind a toggle so the functional board is the default.

**2. Values (`ValuesBoard`).** Two labeled card groups -- **established** (`state==="established"`) and **candidate** (`state==="candidate"`) -- because `state` is the proposal's grouping axis and is a structural enum. Each card: `label` (sans prose), `description`, `priority` chip, a confidence meter (existing meter CSS, `clamp01(confidence)` width + `confidence.toFixed(2)`), `support_count` (acc Tag) and `contradiction_count` (bad Tag when >0), `last_affirmed` relative time, and an evidence chip row of `evidence_episode_ids` (each `shortId` chip pivots to Memory Atlas episodic). Actions row: `why` / `correct` / `forget` (via shared `IdentityCorrectionButtons`). `+ value` lives in the region header behind the `DirectWriteNotice` ack gate (`postValue`). *Why grouped cards, not a table:* values are few, prose-heavy, and the established/candidate split is the primary read.

**3. Goals (`GoalsKanban`).** Four lanes by `status`: **active / blocked / done / abandoned**. Each lane is a scrollable column of goal cards (reusing `.list-row` styling). Card: `description`, `priority` chip, `progress_notes` (sans, truncated with expand), `target_at` ("target" relative date, warn-tinted if past and active), `created_at`. Lane-appropriate lifecycle actions (the proposal's set, all already wired): active -> `mark progress` (modal: note + 0-100, `patchGoal{action:"progress"}`), `complete` (`patchGoal{action:"complete"}`), `block` (`patchGoal{action:"block"}`); plus `why`/`correct`/`forget` on every card. `goal_id` back-references from open questions render a "linked questions" chip. `+ goal` in the active-lane header behind ack gate (`postGoal`). *Why kanban, not cards/table:* `status` is a lifecycle pipeline and the proposal explicitly calls for lanes; the lane is the at-a-glance state read. Each card carries a `DirectPatchLabel` on lifecycle actions (they write the live self-band) -- preserved from current behavior.

**4. Traits (`TraitsBoard`).** A responsive grid of trait cards grouped established/candidate (same `state` axis as values). Card: `label`, a **strength** meter and a **confidence** meter (both `clamp01`), `support_count` / `contradiction_count` chips (this fixes the current gap where Identity hides `contradiction_count` that `MemoryDrill` shows), and an evidence-episode chip row pivoting to Memory Atlas. Actions: `why`/`correct`/`forget`. *Note:* there is **no trait add/lifecycle endpoint** in the verified catalog -- traits are read-only-plus-correction here (no `+ trait`); see backend gaps. *Why cards:* traits are sparse attribute objects best shown as labeled meters.

**5. Open-question queue (`OpenQuestionQueue`).** A priority-ordered queue (master list) + selected-question detail with an event timeline (detail pane). The list sorts open questions by `urgency` desc then `unresolved_rumination_ticks` desc (rumination pressure), with status filter pills (all/open/resolved/abandoned -- keying on `status` enum). Each row: `question` (sans), an **urgency meter**, a `goal_id` link chip (pivots to the linked goal via `SupersededByChip`-style nav), `source` origin Tag, `unresolved_rumination_ticks` ("ruminated N×" Tag, warn-tinted when high), `last_ruminated_at` / `last_touched` relative times, `status` Tag. Actions on open rows: `resolve` (modal: resolution text, `patchOpenQuestion{action:"resolve"}`), `abandon` (modal: reason), `bump` (`{action:"bump"}`, urgency +). Below the selected question: its **event timeline** -- `OpenQuestionEventsSection` generalized via `eventsForRecord(events, "open_question", selectedId)` showing create/resolve/update/duplicate-merge/`without review gate` rows with provenance labels and old->new summaries (the richest existing provenance surface, kept). *Why a queue + timeline, not flat cards:* open questions are a worklist with urgency/recency ordering and per-item history; the proposal explicitly asks for "queue-like design" + "events timeline below the selected question."

**6. Growth + autobiography (`GrowthTimeline`).** Two stacked timelines using the existing `.timeline .ev` vocabulary. **Growth markers** (sorted `ts` desc): each `ev` shows `category` Tag, `what_changed` (sans), `before_description -> after_description` when present, a confidence meter (warn-tinted below 0.6, preserving current behavior), `source_process` origin Tag (pivots to Dream/Stream), and evidence-episode chips. `+ growth` behind ack gate (`postGrowthMarker`). **Autobiographical periods**: horizontal columns (current period highlighted with `--acc` left bar), each with `label`, start/end date range, `narrative` (sans), `themes` chips, and `key_episode_ids` chips pivoting to Memory Atlas. *Why timeline + columns:* growth is inherently chronological; periods are spans best read as a horizontal track. Both also expose a per-record history via `eventsForRecord(events, "growth_marker"|"autobiographical_period", id)` in the inspector. *Note:* autobiography narratives are **read-only** (no edit endpoint) -- see gaps.

---

### Key interactions

- **Selection.** Clicking any card/lane-row/queue-row/timeline-`ev` selects the object and opens it in the **Universal Inspector** drawer (Summary / Evidence / Relationships / Timeline / Actions / Raw JSON). The Studio supplies the object payload and a typed object-ref (`{kind:"value"|"goal"|"trait"|"open_question"|"growth_marker"|"autobiographical_period", id}`) so the inspector's Timeline tab can render `eventsForRecord` and the Actions tab can render the right lifecycle/correction buttons.
- **Two write tracks, preserved.** Direct self-band writes (`+ value`/`+ goal`/`+ growth`, lifecycle patches) are gated behind `DirectWriteNotice` ack + carry `DirectPatchLabel` ("writes live self-band"). Correction (`correct`) queues a review (`postCorrectionCorrect` -> "queue" button), `forget` is immediate (`postCorrectionForget`), `why` opens `WhyDrawer`. After any mutation, `refetchAfterIdentityAction()` re-pulls `getIdentity`.
- **Live.** `maintenance:tick` and `dream:process:completed` frames trigger `refetch()`; the SelfRings pulse keys off freshly-arrived events. No per-frame identity data exists, so this is a refetch-on-tick pattern (matching Review/Dream).

---

### Cross-link pivots (into/out of Universal Inspector)

- **Value/Trait card -> Inspector:** evidence-episode chips (`evidence_episode_ids`) pivot to Memory Atlas episodic; `why` -> provenance; Timeline tab -> `eventsForRecord(...,"value"|"trait")`.
- **Goal <-> Open question:** `goal_id` chip on a question pivots to its goal; goal card shows back-linked questions. Both directions key on `goal_id` (structural).
- **Open question -> Inspector:** `source` label pivots to originating Stream entry / process; event rows with `review_item_id` pivot to Review & Repair; `eventsForRecord(...,"open_question",id)` drives the inline + inspector timeline.
- **Growth marker -> Inspector:** `source_process` pivots to Dream Ops (the offline process) / Stream dream_report; `evidence_episode_ids` -> Memory Atlas.
- **Autobiographical period -> Inspector:** `key_episode_ids` -> Memory Atlas episodic; period is the pivot target for an episode's "open autobiographical period" link (Memory Atlas -> Studio).
- **Inbound:** Memory Atlas self-band and the Universal Inspector "Open in source screen" deep-link here selecting the relevant object.

---

### States: loading / empty / error / live

- **Loading:** `useApi` `loading` -> region skeletons (existing `.id-card` shells with shimmer); SelfRings shows a faint static ring.
- **Empty:** each region uses `IdentityEmptyBand`/`Empty` ("no established values yet", "no active goals", "no open questions", "no growth markers recorded", "no autobiographical periods"). Candidate/established groups collapse to a single empty card when both empty.
- **Error:** `useApi` `error` -> a `Panel` with the `ApiError` message + a `refetch()` retry button (mirrors existing screens). Mutation errors surface in the modal footer (existing pattern).
- **Live:** SelfRings/snapshot "most recent event" update on tick; a small `.dot.alive` pulse in the header signals a recent self-band write. Optimistic lifecycle patches are not needed (these are explicit operator actions with confirm modals); on success the refetch reconciles.

---

### Backend gaps (deferred -- not blockers for the core spec)

The verified catalog fully covers values-add, goals-add+lifecycle, growth-add, open-question lifecycle, and identity read. The genuine gaps below are scoped to extra affordances, not the core studio:

- **Trait add / lifecycle:** no `POST /api/identity/traits` or trait-state-change route exists -- traits are read+correct only.
- **Autobiography narrative edit / period lifecycle:** no endpoint to edit a period's `narrative`/`themes`/`key_episode_ids` -- read-only.
- **Correction reach for identity objects:** `why`/`correct`/`forget` POST to `/api/correction/:id/*`, but the existing correction id-prefix router only routes `ep_`/`semn_`/`seme_`; value/goal/trait/open-question/growth/period ids need correction-router prefix support for these buttons to resolve. (Backend `correction/service.ts` already emits `record_type: value|goal|trait` events, so the service side exists; the router/dispatch is the gap.)
- **Goal "forget"/"correct" parity:** same correction-router gap as above applies to `goals` ids.
**New files:** demo/web/src/screens/Identity/SelfSnapshot.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Identity/SelfRings.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Identity/ValuesBoard.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Identity/GoalsKanban.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Identity/TraitsBoard.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Identity/OpenQuestionQueue.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Identity/GrowthTimeline.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Identity/identity-events.ts, demo/web/src/components/CorrectionButtons.tsx
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/screens/Identity/index.tsx (refactored from flat 12-col grid into 5-region studio; route id and IdentityScreen export kept), demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Modal.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Empty.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Spark.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Panel.tsx, demo/web/src/components/WhyDrawer.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SupersededByChip.tsx, demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts (useApi), demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx (useLiveEventsContext), existing Identity internals reused: DirectWriteNotice, DirectPatchLabel/DirectPatchNotice, IdentityCorrectionButtons, OpenQuestionEventsSection (generalized via record_type), IdentityEmptyBand, eventActionTag/provenanceLabel/eventRecordSummary/shortId/clamp01, demo/web/src/styles.css vocabulary: .id-card, .timeline .ev, meter, .btn sm/.btn live-write, .list-row glowing-left-bar, .eyebrow, .tab-num, oklch signal tokens
**API bindings:** GET /api/identity, POST /api/identity/values, POST /api/identity/goals, PATCH /api/identity/goals/:id, POST /api/identity/growth-markers, PATCH /api/identity/open-questions/:id, GET /api/correction/:id/why, POST /api/correction/:id/forget, POST /api/correction/:id/correct
**Live frames:** maintenance:tick, dream:process:completed, borg:reset
**Cross-link pivots:** value -> Universal Inspector (evidence_episode_ids -> Memory Atlas episodic; why provenance; record event timeline), trait -> Universal Inspector (evidence_episode_ids -> Memory Atlas episodic; support/contradiction; record timeline), goal <-> open question via goal_id (bidirectional pivot), open question -> source (Stream entry/process) + review_item_id -> Review & Repair, growth marker -> source_process (Dream Ops / Stream dream_report) + evidence_episode_ids (Memory Atlas), autobiographical period -> key_episode_ids (Memory Atlas episodic); inbound target of episode 'open period' link, inbound: Memory Atlas self-band + Universal Inspector 'Open in source screen' deep-link into Studio object
**Backend gaps needed:** POST /api/identity/traits (trait add) and trait state-change endpoint -- not in catalog; traits read+correct only, Autobiography narrative/themes/key_episode_ids edit endpoint and period lifecycle -- not in catalog; periods read-only, Correction id-prefix router support for value/goal/trait/open_question/growth_marker/autobiographical_period ids (current router only routes ep_/semn_/seme_) so why/correct/forget resolve for identity objects -- correction/service.ts already emits these record_types, the dispatch/prefix routing is the gap, Goal correct/forget parity (same correction-router prefix gap as above)
---

## Governance Studio — consolidated commitments / directives / shared-state / sessions console (route "governance")
## Governance Studio

### Purpose

One console answering: *what rules, boundaries, directives and shared states govern the being right now; for which audience/session/entity; who authorized them; what lifecycle state are they in; could disclosure widen; what should be revoked or superseded.* It consolidates four current surfaces (Commit, Directives, the shared-state correlation panel currently buried inside Directives, and the session/entity controls currently in `SessionsSidebar` + Identity's governance stub) into one route, `governance`, with a top scope matrix + four tabs.

**Architecture honesty.** Everything here is DISCLOSURE / AUTHORIZATION / LIFECYCLE metadata and host-boundary control — never recall gating. The disclosure-scope map renders `activation_scope` / `content_scope` / `mention_policy` as a *policy label view*; the shared-state board shows every audience's entries in full, labeled by lifecycle kind, never filtered out of the being's view. No screen here decides what the being may internally know; the operator actions are the sanctioned host-boundary controls (revoke / supersede / correct-proposal / participation policy / borg-role). Correct/forget route through the correction *review queue* (a proposal), not a direct rewrite of the being's output. Nothing keys on user-content wording — all grouping/filtering keys on structural fields (`directive_family`, `audience`, `state`, `kind`, `activation_scope`, entity ids).

---

### New files (under `demo/web/src/screens/Governance/`)

- **`index.tsx`** — `GovernanceScreen({ sessionId, audience, onOpenReview, onOpenInspector, onSessionPolicyChanged })`. Owns the active tab (`useState<GovTab>`), the scope-matrix selection (which row scopes the tabs below), and the shared `governanceData` fetch. Mirrors the screen-as-prop-driven-shell convention; data via `useApi`.
- **`gov-data.ts`** — the single fan-out loader `loadGovernanceData(sessionId)` and the matrix aggregator. Wraps `Promise.all([getCommitments({state:"all"}), getCreatorDirectives({status:"all"}), getSessions(), getState({session:sessionId}), getReviews({openOnly:true}), getDreamAudit({limit:50}), getCreatorEntity()])`, then fans out `getSharedState(audience)` over the discovered audience-label set — **lifting `loadDirectiveSupportData`'s audience-discovery verbatim** (`uniqueStrings(["self", ...state.audiences, ...sessions.map(s=>s.audience_label)])`, plus the `SESSION_AUDIENCE_DISCOVERY_CAP` truncation warning). Returns a normalized `GovernanceData` bundle + a derived `ScopeMatrixModel`.
- **`ScopeMatrix.tsx`** — the top matrix component (table). Pure/props-driven over `ScopeMatrixModel`.
- **`CommitmentsTab.tsx`** — extracted from the current Commit screen body (family/audience/state grouping, chain view, detail panel, create-wizard, revoke/correct modals).
- **`DirectivesTab.tsx`** — extracted from the current Directives screen body (detail sections, revoke/supersede), **plus** the new `DisclosureScopeMap` sub-component.
- **`SharedStateBoard.tsx`** — the per-audience lifecycle kanban board.
- **`SessionsEntitiesTab.tsx`** — session participation/role controls + entity create / set-creator / borg-role controls.
- **`gov-lineage.ts`** — shared supersession-chain builder (chain root + branch walk) extracted from Commit so both Commitments and Directives tabs render chains through one helper feeding `SupersededByChip`.
- **`gov-scope.ts`** — pure label maps: `activationScopeLabel`, `contentScopeLabel`, `mentionPolicyLabel`, and `scopeTagKind` (maps the scope/policy unions to existing `TagKind` signals; e.g. `public`→`info`, `operator_only`/`subject_only`/`allow_list`→`warn`, `all_except`→`warn`, `never_mention`→`bad`, `proactive`→`acc`). Keying is on the typed union values only — no word matching.

### Reused / refactored existing components and files

- **`src/components/SupersededByChip.tsx`** — reused verbatim for every supersession/canonical-target jump (commitments, directives, shared-state `superseded_by_id`). `onOpen` is wired to `onOpenInspector(id)` plus an in-tab retarget (the existing `openCommitmentReference` pattern from Commit).
- **`src/lib/shared-state-lifecycle.ts`** — reused as-is for `lifecycleLabel` / `tagKind` in `SharedStateBoard` (today only Directives uses it).
- **`src/components/WhyDrawer.tsx`** + `getWhy` — reused for the "why?" provenance affordance on commitment/directive/shared-state rows (id-prefix permitting); identical to its Commit usage.
- **`Panel`, `Tag`, `Modal`, `Empty`, `Spark`** and the CSS vocabulary (`.tbl`, `.btn`/`.btn.primary`/`.btn.live-write`/`.btn.ghost`/`.btn.sm`, `.pill`/`.filter-pills`, `.props`, `.list`/`.list-row`, `.page-head`/`.page-body`, `.eyebrow`, `.dot`, `.divider`, `.modal-form`/`.modal-field`) — reused. New classes namespaced `.gov-*` (`.gov-matrix`, `.gov-matrix-cell`, `.gov-scopemap`, `.gov-board`, `.gov-board-col`, `.gov-chain`). No new color tokens; reuse `--acc/--warn/--bad/--info/--purple` and their `-dim`/tint conventions.
- **`useApi`** (`hooks/use-api.ts`), **`useLiveEventsContext`** (`hooks/live-context.tsx`) — standard fetch + live-refetch pattern.
- **`components/Rail.tsx`** — `RouteId` union edited: remove `"commit"` and `"directives"`, add `"governance"`. `RAIL_ITEMS`: replace the CMT(↵,5) and DIR(§,6) rows with one `{ id:"governance", label:"governance", short:"GOV", glyph:"§", num:5 }` (the proposal's GOV glyph; reuse `§`). Renumber the remaining rail entries (review 6, dream 7, prompts 8). `App.tsx` route-switch chain replaces the two `CommitScreen`/`DirectivesScreen` branches with one `GovernanceScreen` branch and threads `onOpenReview={() => setRoute("review")}` and `onSessionPolicyChanged={refetchSessionState}`.

### Deleted / absorbed

- `src/screens/Commit/index.tsx` and `src/screens/Directives/index.tsx` are removed; their body logic moves into `CommitmentsTab.tsx` / `DirectivesTab.tsx` (the screen wrappers + their duplicated filter rails and two parallel supersession renderers collapse into the shared `gov-lineage.ts` + one filter rail). The shared-state correlation block in Directives is generalized into `SharedStateBoard` and a per-directive "what live state this rule touches" sub-panel. The session participation control currently inline in `Cognition/index.tsx` (`ParticipationPolicyControl`) and `SessionsSidebar` stays where it is for the chat flow, but its logic is **reused** (not duplicated) by `SessionsEntitiesTab` via the shared `setSessionPolicy` client call; the tab is the governance-grade editor, the sidebar stays the quick switcher.

---

### Top: Scope Matrix (`ScopeMatrix.tsx`)

**Data shape choice — a table/matrix, not cards or a graph.** The proposal's data-shape rule: a fixed small set of columns evaluated across a list of comparable entities is a matrix. Rows are heterogeneous scopes (sessions, audiences, entities) but every column is a uniform aggregate, so a dense `.tbl` with grouped row-bands (a `<tbody>` per row-class with an eyebrow caption row: SESSIONS / AUDIENCES / ENTITIES) reads best and matches the existing table idiom.

Columns (all client-derived from `GovernanceData`, no new endpoint):
- **participation policy** — sessions: `session.participation_policy` as a `Tag` (active→acc, paused/observing→warn, muted→bad). Audiences/entities: blank or aggregate "n active / n muted" across their sessions.
- **role** — `session.audience_role` (`operator`→`Tag.solid`, `participant`→plain); entity row shows `borg_role` (`creator`→acc tag).
- **active commitments** / **critical commitments** — counts from `commitments.filter(state==="active")` grouped by `audience` (null→"global"); critical = `enforcement_class==="critical"`. A `Spark` of active-by-family optional.
- **creator directives** — count of `status==="active"` directives whose `activation_scope`/allow-list structurally includes that audience/entity (join on `activation_allowed_entity_ids` / `subject_entity_id`; `public`/`same_as_disclosure` count for all). Structural join only.
- **shared-state entries** — per-audience entry count from the fanned-out `getSharedState`, with a mini lifecycle breakdown (locked/live/tentative/invalidated dots).
- **open reviews** — count of open `reviews` whose kind/refs touch this scope (`creator_directive_reconciliation`, `commitment_reconciliation`, correction rows referencing the scope's ids).
- **latest turn** — `session.last_turn_id` (short id) + `last_activity_at` via `formatTime`; clickable → Universal Inspector (turn).
- **dream impact** — count of recent `dream/audit` rows whose `targets` reference an id owned by this scope (commitment/directive/shared-state id); chip → Dream Ops audit.

Interactions: clicking a matrix cell *scopes* the tabs below (selecting an audience row filters all four tabs to that audience and lifts that audience into `SharedStateBoard`); clicking an id-bearing value opens the Universal Inspector. A "clear scope" pill resets to all-audiences. The matrix is the single "where is governance pressure" readout the proposal asks for.

---

### Tab 1: Commitments (`CommitmentsTab.tsx`)

**Presentation — grouped master/detail table.** Group order `directive_family → audience → state` (the proposal's order). Family group headers show the existing per-family summary (`N commitments | M active | K superseded`). Within a family, audience sub-bands ("global" for `audience===null`), then state. Default filter "active only"; pills toggle `revoked / expired / superseded`. Filter rail (state / enforcement `critical|advisory` / audience / family / flat-vs-family group mode) reused from Commit. Sort by `priority` then `created_at`.

Row fields (all from `CommitmentItem`): `text`, `type`, `kind`, `enforcement_class`, `critical_domain`, `state`, `priority`, `audience`, `made_to`, `about`, `committed_by`, `source`, `created_at`/`expires_at`/`revoked_at`(+`revoked_reason`), `superseded_by_id`, `source_stream_entry_ids`, `last_reinforced_at`, `canonicalized_by_artifact_entry_id`.

Detail panel: full props + enforcement explanation (critical vs advisory copy) + **supersession chain** via `gov-lineage.ts` → `SupersededByChip` (click-to-jump retargets selection). `critical_domain` rendered as a `Tag.warn` when set.

Flows: **Create wizard** (`postCommitment`, `CreateCommitmentRequest`) — modal stepping type (`COMMITMENT_CREATE_TYPES`) → kind (`COMMITMENT_KINDS`) → directive text / priority(1-10) / audience / made_to / about / directive_family / expires_at, labeled "creator-authored advisory" (`.btn.live-write`). **Revoke** (`postCommitmentRevoke`, optional reason; active only). **Correct** (`postCorrectionCorrect`, JSON-patch + reason; button "queue" — routes to correction review, not a direct write). **Why** (`WhyDrawer`).

---

### Tab 2: Creator directives (`DirectivesTab.tsx`)

**Presentation — master/detail table + per-directive disclosure-scope map.** Filter: status (`active|superseded|revoked|all`), sort by status-rank then priority then created_at (reused from Directives).

Detail sections (from `CreatorDirectiveItem`): identity (`kind`, `subject_kind`, `subject_entity_name`/`subject_entity_id`), activation (`activation_scope`, `activation_allowed_entity_ids`, `activation_excluded_entity_ids`), content (`canonical_fact`, `operational_directive`, `text`, `content_scope`, `mention_policy`), operations (`status`, `priority`, `source_session_id`, `authorization_stream_entry_ids`, `content_source_stream_entry_ids`, `superseded_by_id`, `revoked_reason`).

**Key viz — Disclosure scope map (`DisclosureScopeMap` in `DirectivesTab.tsx`).** A small two-lane diagram (NOT a graph lib — a CSS flex/`.props`-style band), rendered per selected directive, answering the proposal's four questions structurally:
- **who activates it** — `activation_scope` label (`gov-scope.ts`) + the `activation_allowed_entity_ids` / `activation_excluded_entity_ids` rendered as entity chips (each chip → Universal Inspector entity).
- **who may receive content** — `content_scope` label (public / operator_only / allow_list / subject_only / all_except).
- **mention posture** — `mention_policy` label (proactive / answer_if_asked / only_if_topic_raised / never_mention) as a `Tag` toned by `scopeTagKind`.
- Each lane is a labeled row of `Tag`s; entity-bearing scopes expand their id lists inline. This is explicitly a **policy/label view** — it states the disclosure boundary the model honors at emission; it does not hide anything from cognition and does not gate recall. A `.panel-note` reiterates "disclosure label, not a recall filter."

Actions: revoke (`revokeCreatorDirective`, reason required, active only); supersede (`supersedeCreatorDirective`, pick eligible active replacement; disabled when none); open authorization/content source stream ids → Universal Inspector; open reconciliation review → `onOpenReview()` (Review & Repair, `kind=creator_directive_reconciliation`). Reconciliation resolution itself lives in Review & Repair (`resolveCreatorDirectiveReconciliation`), surfaced here as a deep-link + an "N open reconciliations" badge.

---

### Tab 3: Shared state (`SharedStateBoard.tsx`)

**Presentation — per-audience lifecycle kanban board.** Choice rationale: shared-state entries have a discrete lifecycle enum with no priority/time ordering that a table would surface better, and the operator wants "what's locked vs tentative vs invalidated for this audience" at a glance — a column-per-state board is the natural fit (the proposal's board spec). An audience selector (pills over the discovered audience set; default `self`, or the audience lifted from the scope matrix) sits above the board. Seven columns via `shared-state-lifecycle.ts`: **locked · live · low-salience live · dormant live · tentative · pending · invalidated** (column header uses `lifecycleLabel`, column accent uses `tagKind`).

Each card (`SharedStateEntry`): `state_key`, `text`, owner (`owner_entity_id` → entity inspector), `rank`, `created_at`/`last_updated_at`, `superseded_by_id` (`SupersededByChip`), and the `canonicalizes` bundle rendered as four chip clusters (goal_ids / commitment_ids / action_ids / open_question_ids), each chip → Universal Inspector for that object. `provenance_stream_entry_ids` / `last_updated_stream_entry_ids` → Stream & Provenance. Live: re-fetch the active audience's shared-state on `maintenance:tick`.

This board is read+pivot, not mutate (shared-state has no direct write endpoint; corrections to canonicalized objects happen on those objects). A `.panel-note` clarifies it shows the audience's *common-ground* state the being tracks — labels, never a recall gate.

---

### Tab 4: Sessions & entities (`SessionsEntitiesTab.tsx`)

**Presentation — two tables.** Sessions table (`SessionRecord`): `label`, `source_type`, `source_url`, `audience_label`, `audience_role`, `privacy_level`, `status`, `participation_policy`, `last_turn_id`, `message_count`. Inline participation-policy editor (`setSessionPolicy`, `PARTICIPATION_POLICIES` = active/paused/observing/muted + free-text reason), **locked when `audience_role==="operator"`** (reusing the existing lock rule); on success calls `onSessionPolicyChanged` so App refetches state+sessions. `source_url`/`status`/`privacy_level` are read-only labels (no endpoint mutates them).

Entities controls: current creator from `getCreatorEntity` (an `EntityRecord` card or empty-state); **create entity** (`POST /api/entities` — name + optional kind; note this is the POST the proposal omitted but exists); **set creator by name** (`setCreatorByName` → `POST /api/entities/creator`); **set/clear borg-role** (`setEntityBorgRole` → `POST /api/entities/:id/borg-role`, `creator | null`); **ensure operator session** (`openOperatorSession` → `POST /api/sessions/operator`, 409 → "mark a creator first"). All danger-tinted (`.btn.live-write`) with confirm where they write the being's structural state.

---

### Cross-link pivots (Universal Inspector)

Every id-bearing value in every tab and the matrix calls `onOpenInspector(id)` (the shared right-side drawer). Pivots *out*: commitment → source stream entries / directive family / supersession chain / related reviews / revoke / correct; creator directive → authorization + content-source stream entries / subject entity / supersession chain / reconciliation review / revoke / supersede; shared-state → owner entity / canonicalized goal/commitment/action/open-question / provenance stream entries; session → latest turn / audience entity; entity → its sessions + directives where subject. Pivots *in*: the inspector's "open in source screen" for a commitment/directive/shared-state/session/entity routes to `governance` with the right tab + selection preset (the screen accepts an optional `initialFocus?: { tab, id }` prop so the inspector can land precisely). `SupersededByChip` is the in-tab jump primitive; the inspector is the cross-screen one.

### States

- **Loading**: matrix + active tab show skeleton rows (reuse `.notice` "loading governance state"); the fan-out is the slow path, so the matrix renders progressively (commitments/directives/sessions first, shared-state columns fill as per-audience fetches resolve).
- **Empty**: per-tab `Empty`/`.notice` ("no commitments", "no creator directives — mark a creator and author one", "no shared state for {audience}", "no sessions"). Disclosure-scope map empty when no directive selected.
- **Error**: `useApi` error surfaces a `.notice.bad` with refetch; partial errors (one audience's shared-state fails) degrade that column to an inline error chip, not the whole screen.
- **Live**: subscribe via `useLiveEventsContext`; `maintenance:tick` and `borg:reset` refetch `loadGovernanceData` (reset also clears selection); `stream:append` triggers a debounced refetch of commitments/shared-state (new commitments/state can land mid-turn). Mirrors the Review/Dream live-refetch pattern; reconnect → refetch.

### Migration notes

`commit` + `directives` routes and screen files are removed; their logic is reused in the new tabs, with the two parallel supersession-chain renderers de-duplicated into `gov-lineage.ts` and the two near-identical filter rails into one. The shared-state correlation panel leaves Directives and becomes the generalized `SharedStateBoard` + a per-directive "live state touched" view. The Rail loses two entries and gains one (`governance`, GOV, §), so 9 routes become 8. Tests: add `Governance.test.tsx` colocated, asserting matrix aggregation, tab switching, supersession-chip jumps, create/revoke/supersede flows (fetch-stubbed, method-aware), disclosure-scope labels, and the operator-role policy lock — preserving the existing accessible-query + `data-testid` + class-hook conventions and the `makeLiveSource`/`jsonResponse`/`requestPath` test idioms.

### Backend gap

The only proposal feature without a backend route: **commitment_reconciliation has no dedicated resolution endpoint** (unlike `POST /api/reviews/:id/creator-directive-reconciliation`). Gov Studio therefore only *surfaces* commitment-reconciliation pressure (counts + deep-link); actual resolution is left to Review & Repair via the generic `PATCH /api/reviews/:id`. A `POST /api/reviews/:id/commitment-reconciliation` would let Gov Studio close that loop in-place (see requiresBackendGap).
**New files:** demo/web/src/screens/Governance/index.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Governance/gov-data.ts, demo/web/src/screens/Governance/ScopeMatrix.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Governance/CommitmentsTab.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Governance/DirectivesTab.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Governance/SharedStateBoard.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Governance/SessionsEntitiesTab.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Governance/gov-lineage.ts, demo/web/src/screens/Governance/gov-scope.ts, demo/web/src/screens/Governance/Governance.test.tsx
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/components/SupersededByChip.tsx, demo/web/src/lib/shared-state-lifecycle.ts, demo/web/src/components/WhyDrawer.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Panel.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Modal.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Empty.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Spark.tsx, demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Directives/index.tsx (loadDirectiveSupportData audience-discovery fan-out, scope diff logic), demo/web/src/screens/Commit/index.tsx (family-group + supersession chain-root + openCommitmentReference logic), demo/web/src/components/Rail.tsx (RouteId union + RAIL_ITEMS), demo/web/src/App.tsx (route switch + onOpenReview/onSessionPolicyChanged wiring), demo/web/src/styles.css (.tbl/.btn/.pill/.props/.list/.eyebrow/.dot vocabulary + oklch signal tokens), demo/web/src/api/client.ts (getCommitments/getCreatorDirectives/getSharedState/getSessions/setSessionPolicy/etc.)
**API bindings:** GET /api/commitments, POST /api/commitments, POST /api/commitments/:id/revoke, GET /api/creator-directives, POST /api/creator-directives/:id/revoke, POST /api/creator-directives/:id/supersede, GET /api/shared-state, GET /api/sessions, POST /api/sessions/:id/participation, POST /api/sessions/operator, GET /api/entities/creator, POST /api/entities, POST /api/entities/creator, POST /api/entities/:id/borg-role, GET /api/state, GET /api/reviews, GET /api/dream/audit, GET /api/correction/:id/why, POST /api/correction/:id/correct
**Live frames:** maintenance:tick, stream:append, borg:reset
**Cross-link pivots:** commitment id <-> Universal Inspector (commitment): source stream entries, directive family, supersession chain, related reviews, creator directive id <-> Universal Inspector (directive): authorization + content-source stream entries, subject entity, supersession chain, reconciliation review, shared-state entry <-> Universal Inspector: owner entity, canonicalized goal/commitment/action/open-question ids, provenance stream entries, session row -> Universal Inspector (turn via last_turn_id) and (entity via audience_entity_id), entity row -> Universal Inspector (entity): its sessions + directives where subject, stream-id chips (source_stream_entry_ids / authorization_stream_entry_ids / provenance_stream_entry_ids) -> Stream & Provenance, dream-impact cell -> Dream Ops audit row, open-reconciliation badge -> Review & Repair (kind=creator_directive_reconciliation / commitment_reconciliation), Universal Inspector 'open in source screen' -> Governance Studio via initialFocus {tab,id}
**Backend gaps needed:** POST /api/reviews/:id/commitment-reconciliation — a dedicated resolution endpoint for commitment_reconciliation reviews, mirroring the existing creator-directive-reconciliation route; absent today, so Gov Studio can only surface + deep-link these (resolution stays via generic PATCH /api/reviews/:id in Review & Repair), GET /api/entities (list) — the matrix's entity rows are currently reconstructable only from getCreatorEntity + session audience_entity_id joins; a full entity-list endpoint would let the entities row-band and entity controls enumerate beyond the creator without inferring from sessions
**Scope verdict:** In-scope: pure operator-UI consolidation + disclosure/lifecycle label presentation and sanctioned host-boundary controls (revoke/supersede/participation/borg-role/correct-proposal); no recall gating, no output policing — shared-state and disclosure scopes are rendered as labels, never used to hide memory from cognition.
