## Review & Repair Cockpit (consolidates Review + scattered correction flows + Correction Lab)
## Purpose

Review & Repair is the operator's safety-critical truth-repair cockpit. It answers the five questions the proposal (response.md 6.7) names: *what is wrong/uncertain, what evidence backs each side, what action is safe, what changes if I resolve it, what did the correction do.* It evolves the existing `screens/Review/index.tsx` (1230 lines) into a three-pane cockpit and **absorbs the scattered correction-initiation buttons** currently duplicated across Memory, Identity, and Commit into a coherent **Correction Lab** sub-mode -- healing the "propose in Commit / resolve in Review" seam called out in the ground-truth governance report.

This is an OPERATOR console. Per the borg CLAUDE.md it inspects/corrects/repairs only via the **sanctioned correction & review endpoints**. It is NOT production policing: it never rewrites or suppresses the entity's user-facing output; every mutation is a queued review proposal or an explicitly-warned reversible repair acting on memory objects, not on emitted text. All disclosure/provenance/taint/origin_audience fields are rendered as **labels on recalled rows**, never as recall filters -- the queue shows every open review regardless of audience.

---

## Component breakdown

### New files (under `demo/web/src/screens/Repair/`)

- **`index.tsx`** -- `RepairScreen` (named export, prop-driven; receives `sessionId`, `audience`, and a new `onOpenInspector(objectId)` callback from `App.tsx`, mirroring the existing `onOpenReview` callback pattern). Owns: sub-mode state (`"queue" | "lab"`), selected review id, selected resolution action, filter state, and the lazy pair-drill cache. Loads via `useApi`: `getReviews({ openOnly })`, `getCorrectionReviews()`, `getCreatorDirectives()`, `getCommitments()`. Subscribes to `useLiveEventsContext().subscribe` and refetches on `maintenance:tick` and on live reconnect (`onReconnected`) -- the exact pattern Review and Dream already share. Layout is a bespoke 3-col grid `.repair-screen { grid-template-columns: 300px minmax(0,1fr) 340px }` matching the `.stream-screen`/`.band-detail` idiom (full-height grid, children `overflow:hidden`, scroll inside).
- **`ReviewQueue.tsx`** -- left pane. Kind-grouped list (`ReviewQueueGroup` per kind) + filter rail.
- **`EvidenceComparison.tsx`** -- center pane router that dispatches on `review.kind` to one of: `SemanticPairCompare`, `DirectiveReconcileCompare`, `CommitmentReconcileCompare`, `CorrectionCompare`, `BeliefRevisionCompare`, `GenericReviewDetail`.
- **`ResolutionPanel.tsx`** -- right pane. Contextual action set + note field + affected-objects list + before/after preview + confirm gating.
- **`CorrectionLab.tsx`** -- the sub-mode: id input -> why -> correct/forget/invalidate -> result.
- **`repair-actions.ts`** -- pure helpers: `actionsForKind(kind): ReviewResolution[]`, `resolutionEndpointFor(kind, action)` (returns a discriminated descriptor naming which of the 4 split routes to call), `severityRank(row)` (structural, see below), `affectedObjectIds(row): {id,kind}[]` (reads `row.refs` structurally), `hasMissingRefs(row)`. No keyword/word matching -- keys only on `kind`, `refs` keys, and id prefixes.
- **`repair-refs.ts`** -- id-prefix router reused from Memory's `correctionActionKind`: `ep_`->episode, `semn_`->semantic_node, `seme_`->semantic_edge, plus directive/commitment/review prefixes for inspector pivots. Centralizes the prefix logic Memory currently inlines.
- Colocated tests: `Repair.test.tsx`, `repair-actions.test.ts` (match `src/**/*.test.tsx?`, RTL + `fetch`-stub + `makeLiveSource` conventions; assert on `getByRole`, `aria-label`s, and stable `data-testid`s like `data-testid="review-row"`, `data-testid="resolution-action"`).

### Existing components reused (real paths)

- **`components/WhyDrawer.tsx`** -- reused verbatim as the universal provenance affordance in both Correction Lab and per-review "why?" buttons (it already renders any `WhyResponse` for any id via `getWhy`).
- **`components/SemanticNodeDetail.tsx`** + **`SemanticEdgeDetail`** -- reused in `SemanticPairCompare` for contradiction/duplicate/misattribution drill (Review already does exactly this).
- **`components/SupersededByChip.tsx`** -- reused for lineage/jump-to-survivor chips in directive and commitment reconciliation, and as the cross-link primitive into the Universal Inspector.
- **`components/Tag.tsx`** (TagKind acc/warn/bad/info/purple/solid), **`Panel.tsx`**, **`Modal.tsx`**, **`Empty.tsx`** (`.notice`), plus CSS-only primitives `.btn`(+`.primary`/`.live-write`/`.ghost`/`.sm`), `.tbl`, `.pill`/`.filter-pills`, `.list`/`.list-row` (glowing-left-bar selected), `.props`/`.why-*`, `.divider`. No new visual vocabulary -- extend existing `.repair-*` classes onto these patterns; reuse `--acc/--warn/--bad/--info/--purple` oklch tokens and the `.eyebrow` micro-label idiom.
- **`hooks/use-api.ts`**, **`hooks/live-context.tsx`** / `useLiveEventsContext`, **`lib/stream-utils.ts`** `formatTime` (already imported by Review/Dream).
- **`api/client.ts`** functions: `getReviews`, `patchReview`, `getCorrectionReviews`, `patchCorrectionReview`, `resolveCreatorDirectiveReconciliation`, `getWhy`, `postCorrectionForget`, `postCorrectionCorrect`, `postSemanticEdgeInvalidate`, `getSemanticNode`, `getSemanticEdge`, `getCreatorDirectives`, `getCommitments`. All already exist.

---

## Data presentation (per data-shape, justified)

### Left -- Review queue (`ReviewQueue.tsx`)
**List grouped by kind**, not a flat table: the queue is heterogeneous and action sets differ per kind, so grouping by the 11-member `ReviewKind` (fixed `REVIEW_KIND_ORDER` carried over from current Review) makes the operator's job legible. Each group header shows the kind label + open count `Tag`. Each row uses the `.list-row` selectable pattern (glowing-left-bar on `.selected`) and renders structurally from `ReviewRow`:
- short id (`#${row.id}`), kind `Tag`, `formatTime(created_at)`, resolved-state dot.
- **reason** (one line, truncated) -- this is `row.reason`, server-authored, displayed as-is (no parsing).
- **affected object id chips** from `affectedObjectIds(row.refs)` -- structural read of `refs` keys (e.g. `node_a`/`node_b`/`edge_id`/`survivor_id`/`member_ids`), each a `SupersededByChip`-style chip that pivots to the inspector.
- **severity heuristic** -- a *structural* badge (`critical`/`advisory`/`info`), derived only from kind + refs shape (e.g. `creator_directive_reconciliation` & `commitment_reconciliation` with critical-enforcement members rank high; `belief_revision`/`new_insight` rank low). NEVER from scanning content text -- this stays language-agnostic.
- **source-process** badge if `row.refs` or `row.resolver_diagnostic` carries a `process`/`source_process` field (the `__borg_review_resolver_*` refs Review already extracts); shown as a `Tag.dim` when present, omitted otherwise.
- **missing-ref warning** dot (`--bad`) when `hasMissingRefs(row)` -- a referenced node/edge id is absent from the loaded set.

**Filter rail** (`.filter-pills`, reusing the auto-dropdown-over-8 pattern): open-only toggle, kind multi-select, age buckets (derived from `created_at`), affected-object-type, has-missing-references, and **origin filter** (manually-created / dream-created / correction-created) -- all keyed on structural fields (`refs.origin`, source-process presence, the `correction` kind), never on words. These filters rank/scope the *operator's review list*; they are not entity recall gates -- the entity's memory is untouched by them.

### Center -- Evidence comparison (`EvidenceComparison.tsx`)
Dispatches on `review.kind`:

- **`SemanticPairCompare`** (contradiction / duplicate / misattribution / temporal_drift / identity_inconsistency / new_insight): **side-by-side two-column cards** -- the natural shape for "candidate A vs candidate B." Each column is a `SemanticNodeDetail` (lazy `getSemanticNode` for ids in `refs`), showing label/kind/status/confidence/source_count/domain/aliases. Between them, the connecting `SemanticEdgeDetail` (lazy `getSemanticEdge`) renders relation/weight/valid-from/to/invalidation state. Below: a **source-episode strip** -- grouped episode-id chips from each candidate's `source_episode_ids`, each pivoting to the inspector. A **missing-reference warning** banner (`--warn`) lists any ref id that failed to load. Disclosure/provenance labels (origin_audience, private/operator, trust_rank, taint) render as `Tag`s on each candidate where the node carries them -- shown, never used to hide a candidate.
- **`DirectiveReconcileCompare`** (creator_directive_reconciliation): **member cards in a column stack with per-scope-field diff highlighting** (carrying over Review's `scopeDifferences`), showing scope, mention_policy, content policy (canonical_fact / operational_directive), allowed/excluded entity ids. A **survivor picker** (radio over member ids). A **disclosure-widening warning** (`--warn` banner): computed *structurally* by comparing the survivor's disclosure scope/mention_policy against each superseded member -- if the survivor would expose a fact to a broader audience scope than a member currently allows, warn. This is a structural scope comparison (scope/policy enum widening), not content inspection, and honors the cardinal rule: it warns the operator about a disclosure-policy change, it does not gate recall.
- **`CommitmentReconcileCompare`** (commitment_reconciliation): **commitments grouped by scope and directive_family**, presented as a **scope x family matrix/grouped list** so scope mismatches are visually obvious. A **scope-mismatch warning** (`--warn`) fires structurally when grouped members carry differing `audience`/scope values. Survivor/revoke/keep options surfaced. (See gap note -- resolution routes through generic `PATCH`.)
- **`CorrectionCompare`** (correction): renders the queued JSON patch (`refs.patch`) as a **before/after field diff** against the current object state (lazy-fetched by target id), plus the correction prompt summary and target-object header. Accept/reject only.
- **`BeliefRevisionCompare`** (belief_revision): old-belief -> new-belief pair with the revision rationale from `refs`; dismiss-only.
- **`GenericReviewDetail`** (skill_split + fallback): structured `.props` rows over `refs` (skill-split proposal: applies_when / approach / split rationale).

Every center view exposes the standard controls (proposal 4): **Copy ID, Open in source screen, Show provenance (`WhyDrawer`), Show related stream entries (pivot), Run why?** -- as a consistent header toolbar, removing the "every screen invents its own detail panel" problem.

### Right -- Resolution panel (`ResolutionPanel.tsx`)
Contextual action buttons from `actionsForKind(kind)`, each mapped to the correct backend route via `resolutionEndpointFor`:

| Kind | Actions | Route |
|---|---|---|
| contradiction / duplicate | keep_both, supersede, invalidate, dismiss | `PATCH /api/reviews/:id` (supersede/invalidate carry `winner_node_id`) |
| new_insight | accept, invalidate, dismiss | `PATCH /api/reviews/:id` |
| misattribution / temporal_drift / identity_inconsistency / skill_split | keep_both / supersede / dismiss (per kind) | `PATCH /api/reviews/:id` |
| correction | accept, reject | `PATCH /api/correction/reviews/:id` (`{action:"accept"\|"reject", note?}`) |
| belief_revision | dismiss | `PATCH /api/dream/review/:id` (dismiss-only) |
| creator_directive_reconciliation | supersede-to-survivor, keep-independent | `POST /api/reviews/:id/creator-directive-reconciliation` |
| commitment_reconciliation | accept / reject / dismiss / keep (+ survivor) | `PATCH /api/reviews/:id` (**no dedicated route -- see gap**) |

Every action carries: a **note/reason field** (textarea; *required* where the current screens require it -- directive revoke/supersede), an **affected-objects list** (the `affectedObjectIds` chips, so the operator sees blast radius), and a **before/after preview** where computable (the patch diff for correction; survivor-vs-superseded for reconciliation; node status change for invalidate). **Confirmation modal (`Modal`) ONLY for destructive/reversing actions** -- supersede, invalidate, invalidate_edge, archive_node, forget, revoke -- per the proposal's "confirmation only for destructive" rule. Non-destructive (dismiss, keep, keep_both, accept-correction-into-queue) resolve directly. On success: refetch reviews + emit `onReviewResolved` so the queue updates; the just-resolved row animates out.

### Correction Lab sub-mode (`CorrectionLab.tsx`)
A second tab on the same screen (sub-mode toggle in the page head, same pattern as Memory's browser/topology toggle). Flow: **(1)** object-id input (or arrives pre-filled via inspector pivot / "correct this" deep-link from Memory/Identity/Commit) -> **(2)** `repair-refs.ts` resolves the correctable kind from prefix (`ep_`/`semn_`/`seme_`); if unsupported, show "no correction actions for this id" `.notice` -> **(3)** auto-run **why?** (`WhyDrawer` inline, or `getWhy` rendered in a `.why-kv` block) -> **(4)** choose: **correct** (field-aware patch form prefilled by `defaultMemoryPatch` per target type + free-text reason; `postCorrectionCorrect` -> labeled "queue" because it routes to the correction-review queue), **forget/archive/revoke** (`postCorrectionForget`, confirm modal), **invalidate edge** (`seme_` only; `postSemanticEdgeInvalidate` with `at` ms + reason) -> **(5)** show the resulting `ReviewRow` (for correct) or `CorrectionForgetResponse` (for forget) inline, with a link to the new queue row and to the audit/provenance. This makes "select id -> why -> correct/forget/invalidate -> resulting review" a single coherent workflow, replacing the JSON-patch-textarea buttons scattered in three screens.

---

## Key interactions
- Select review row (left) -> center renders kind-specific comparison -> right shows contextual actions. Keyboard: up/down to move selection, Enter focuses the primary safe action.
- Pair drill: clicking a candidate id chip lazily fetches the node/edge and expands its detail; clicking a source-episode chip pivots to the inspector.
- Survivor picker selection live-updates the disclosure-widening / scope-mismatch warning banner.
- Correction Lab id field accepts paste or deep-link prefill; Tab advances why -> action.

## Cross-link pivots (into/out of Universal Inspector)
- **Out:** every object-id chip (affected nodes/edges/episodes/directives/commitments/stream-ids) calls `onOpenInspector(id)` -> Universal Inspector opens that object with its own Summary/Evidence/Relationships/Timeline/Actions/Raw tabs. "Open in source screen" pivots to Memory Atlas band / Governance Studio / Stream as appropriate (via existing `setRoute` callbacks).
- **In:** the inspector's "Show related reviews" and any object's "has review" badge deep-link back here with that review pre-selected; Memory/Identity/Commit "correct" buttons deep-link into Correction Lab with the id pre-filled. Dream Ops' belief-revision handoff (`onOpenReview`) lands on the belief_revision group.

## States
- **Loading:** `.notice "loading review queue"`; center shows skeleton `.list` rows; lazy pair fetches show per-card "loading provenance".
- **Empty:** zero open reviews -> `Empty`/`.notice` "no open reviews -- substrate is consistent" with a subtle `--acc` tint (a healthy state, not an error). Empty Correction Lab -> prompt to paste an id.
- **Error:** `useApi` error -> `.notice bad` with `error.message` + retry (`refetch`); `getLedger`-style graceful 404 for refs not retained ("referenced object no longer present").
- **Live:** `maintenance:tick` and dream-process frames refetch the queue (review-resolver/belief-reviser/reconciler runs surface new rows live); a small `--acc` pulse on the affected kind group header when new rows arrive; `borg:reset` clears selection and refetches; `wsState` "reconnecting"/"down" shows a `--warn` strip (reuse StatusBar tone classes).

## Migration note
- **Becomes this:** `screens/Review/index.tsx` (1230 lines) is refactored into `screens/Repair/` -- its `REVIEW_KIND_ORDER`, per-kind action sets (`GENERIC_REVIEW_ACTIONS`), per-kind detail field builders, semantic pair drill, `scopeDifferences` reconciliation diff, and resolver-diagnostic block are extracted into `ReviewQueue`/`EvidenceComparison`/`repair-actions.ts` rather than rewritten.
- **Absorbed:** the correction-initiation modals duplicated in `screens/Memory/index.tsx` (`runMemoryAction`, `defaultMemoryPatch`, the `ep_/semn_/seme_` prefix router), `screens/Identity/index.tsx` (`IdentityCorrectionButtons`), and `screens/Commit/index.tsx`'s "correct" modal move into `CorrectionLab.tsx`. Those screens keep a thin "correct this" button that deep-links into the Lab (one shared flow instead of three near-identical JSON-patch textareas), closing the propose/resolve seam.
- **`RouteId`** "review" entry in `components/Rail.tsx` is renamed/retargeted to `RepairScreen`; the rail glyph/short-code stays. `App.tsx`'s `onOpenReview` callbacks (from Memory and Dream) point at the new screen unchanged.
- **Deleted:** the standalone duplicate correction modal JSX in Memory/Identity/Commit (logic consolidated, not lost); the static "identity governance" panel stub in Memory if it only linked to review counts.

## Cardinal-constraint compliance
- **Memory global / disclosure contextual:** the queue lists *every* open review; disclosure/origin_audience/trust/taint are rendered as `Tag` labels on candidate rows, never as filters that remove a review or hide a memory from the operator. The disclosure-widening and scope-mismatch warnings are *operator-facing structural alerts about a policy change*, computed from scope/policy enums -- not recall gates.
- **No output policing:** every mutation targets memory/semantic/governance objects through sanctioned correction/review endpoints; nothing inspects or rewrites the entity's emitted user-facing text.
- **Language-agnostic:** all branching keys on `review.kind`, `refs` keys/shape, id prefixes, and enum scopes -- zero keyword/pronoun/phrase matching on user content. `reason`/patch text is displayed verbatim, never parsed for meaning.
**New files:** demo/web/src/screens/Repair/index.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Repair/ReviewQueue.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Repair/EvidenceComparison.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Repair/ResolutionPanel.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Repair/CorrectionLab.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Repair/repair-actions.ts, demo/web/src/screens/Repair/repair-refs.ts, demo/web/src/screens/Repair/Repair.test.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Repair/repair-actions.test.ts
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/components/WhyDrawer.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SemanticNodeDetail.tsx (SemanticNodeDetail + SemanticEdgeDetail), demo/web/src/components/SupersededByChip.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Panel.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Modal.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Empty.tsx, demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx, demo/web/src/lib/stream-utils.ts (formatTime), demo/web/src/api/client.ts, demo/web/src/screens/Review/index.tsx (refactored: REVIEW_KIND_ORDER, GENERIC_REVIEW_ACTIONS, scopeDifferences, pair drill, resolver-diagnostic block), demo/web/src/screens/Memory/index.tsx (correctionActionKind/defaultMemoryPatch/runMemoryAction extracted into CorrectionLab), demo/web/src/screens/Identity/index.tsx (IdentityCorrectionButtons consolidated into CorrectionLab), demo/web/src/screens/Commit/index.tsx (correct modal consolidated into CorrectionLab), demo/web/src/components/Rail.tsx (RouteId 'review' retargets to RepairScreen)
**API bindings:** GET /api/reviews, PATCH /api/reviews/:id, POST /api/reviews/:id/creator-directive-reconciliation, GET /api/correction/reviews, PATCH /api/correction/reviews/:id, GET /api/correction/:id/why, POST /api/correction/:id/forget, POST /api/correction/:id/correct, POST /api/correction/semantic-edges/:id/invalidate, GET /api/semantic/nodes/:id, GET /api/semantic/edges/:id, GET /api/creator-directives, GET /api/commitments, PATCH /api/dream/review/:id
**Live frames:** maintenance:tick, dream:process:started, dream:process:completed, borg:reset
**Cross-link pivots:** out: affected-object id chips (semn_/seme_/ep_/directive/commitment/stream ids) -> onOpenInspector(id) -> Universal Inspector, out: 'open in source screen' -> Memory Atlas band / Governance Studio / Stream via setRoute, out: source-episode chips -> Stream & Provenance inspector, in: inspector 'show related reviews' / 'has review' badge -> Repair with review pre-selected, in: Memory/Identity/Commit 'correct this' button -> Correction Lab with object id pre-filled, in: Dream Ops belief-revision handoff (onOpenReview) -> belief_revision group
**Backend gaps needed:** commitment_reconciliation has NO dedicated resolution endpoint (unlike creator-directive-reconciliation): the survivor/revoke/keep flow must resolve through the generic PATCH /api/reviews/:id, passing survivor/intent in the note field. A POST /api/reviews/:id/commitment-reconciliation would be the parity fix but does not exist in the catalog., No endpoint returns a per-review 'severity' or 'source_process' field directly; severity badge and origin filters are derived client-side from kind + refs/resolver_diagnostic shape. A server-provided severity/source_process on ReviewRow would make these non-heuristic., before/after preview for correction reviews needs the current target-object state; reconstructed client-side from getSemanticNode/getSemanticEdge or band detail. A correction-preview/diff endpoint would make the preview authoritative rather than reconstructed.
**Scope verdict:** In-scope: this is operator-console presentation/repair consolidation -- it surfaces disclosure/provenance labels at render time and routes mutations through sanctioned correction/review endpoints; it does NOT recall-gate by audience nor police the entity's output.
---

## Dream Ops -- Offline-Maintenance Control Room (evolves the Dream screen)
## Dream Ops -- offline-maintenance control room

### Purpose

Make the dream/offline-maintenance cycle feel like **operating a machine**, not reading admin tables. It answers the proposal's six operator questions: which offline processes are enabled, what ran recently, what is scheduled, what *would* change if I ran maintenance now, what changed last time, and can I revert it. It is the evolution of the current `DreamScreen` ("dream cycle" cockpit), keeping its proven plan->apply->audit->revert spine and live two-sweep progress machinery, but re-laying it into four explicit zones with a real process-subset+budget workbench and a true schedule lane.

This is an operator/ops surface. It plans/applies/reverts maintenance only through the sanctioned `/api/dream/*` endpoints and the dismiss-only `/api/dream/review/:id`. It never inspects-and-suppresses the entity's user-facing output (no production policing), and nothing here filters what the entity may internally recall -- audience/provenance appear only as render-time labels on audit targets.

### Route + migration

- **Route id stays `dream`** (in `RouteId` union, `components/Rail.tsx`, glyph `☾`). `App.tsx` already passes `onOpenReview={() => setRoute("review")}` to `DreamScreen` -- preserved.
- **What this becomes:** `screens/Dream/index.tsx` (1204 lines) is refactored in place, not rewritten from zero. The file is split so the monolith becomes a thin orchestrator plus zone components colocated in `screens/Dream/`.
- **What is reused verbatim from today's screen:** the live-frame reducer (`dream:process:started/completed`, `maintenance:tick`, `stream:append`/dream_report, reconnect refetch on `connectionCount` bump), the `DreamRunStatus` two-sweep state machine (`planning->planned->running->done|fail`), `auditRunGroups`, `oldNewPairs`/`pairedKeys`, `auditHasReversal`/`auditCanRevert`/`auditRevert*`/`auditStatusLabel`/`auditStatusTone`, `maintenanceTickTone`/`maintenanceTickSummary`, `statusTag`, and the `PROCESS_NAMES` ordering. These move into `screens/Dream/dream-utils.ts` so the zone components share them.
- **What is deleted/replaced:** the current single `page-head` action row (`plan`/`apply` buttons firing `postDreamPlan({})`/`postDreamApply({})` with empty args) is replaced by the **Plan/Apply Workbench** zone that sends a real `{ processes, budget }`. The inline `style={{fontSize:10.5,...}}` scheduler indicator in the page-head (a stray literal) is replaced by the **Maintenance-Health Header** card grid. The current "schedule sparkline" (recent-runs strip) is replaced by a proper **Schedule Lane** timeline reading `state.schedule` (`DreamScheduleItem[]`). The audit-log section is reframed as the **Audit Ledger** zone but reuses the existing run-id grouping and payload renderer.

### Data fetching

- One `useApi(getDreamState, [])` owns the whole snapshot (`DreamStateResponse`: `processes`, `pending_extraction_episodes?`, `schedule`, `dream_reports`, `audit_rows`, `belief_revision_rows`, `scheduler{enabled,light_interval_ms,heavy_interval_ms,light_processes,heavy_processes,process_budgets}`). This is the established pattern; do not add a parallel fetcher.
- Audit ledger uses `state.audit_rows` for first paint and calls `getDreamAudit({limit:50})` after any apply/revert to refresh (matches current behavior).
- Live refresh: subscribe via `useLiveEventsContext().subscribe`; refetch on `maintenance:tick`, on `stream:append` containing a `dream_report` entry, and on reconnect (`connectionCount` increment). `borg:reset` -> `App.tsx` already reloads.

### Component breakdown

**New files (all under `demo/web/src/screens/Dream/`):**

- `index.tsx` -- slimmed `DreamScreen` orchestrator: owns `useApi(getDreamState)`, the live subscription/reducer, `runStatus` map, `plan`/`applyResult` state, busy state, and renders the four zones in a `.dream-ops` grid. Keeps the `onOpenReview?` prop.
- `dream-utils.ts` -- the extracted pure helpers listed above (run grouping, old/new diff pairs, status/tone mappers, tick summary, `PROCESS_NAMES`, plus a new `PROCESS_DESCRIPTIONS` map keyed by `DreamProcessName` for card descriptions when the backend `description` is just the slug).
- `MaintenanceHealthHeader.tsx` -- the 8-card header (see Data presentation).
- `ProcessGrid.tsx` + `ProcessCard.tsx` -- the 12-process matrix; `ProcessCard` is the per-process tile (reuses today's `DreamCard` look via `.dream-card` classes).
- `PlanApplyWorkbench.tsx` -- process-subset selector + budget input + plan/apply controls + plan-result and apply-result layouts.
- `ScheduleLane.tsx` -- the schedule timeline.
- `AuditLedger.tsx` + `AuditRunHeader.tsx` + `AuditRowDetail.tsx` -- run-id-grouped ledger and the per-row inspector body (reuses the existing payload renderer / `oldNewPairs` view).
- `RevertConfirm.tsx` -- the diff-like revert confirmation surface.

**Existing components reused (cite paths):**

- `components/Panel.tsx` -- every zone is a `Panel` (`.panel` + `.panel-header` eyebrow + `.panel-body`).
- `components/Tag.tsx` -- status chips (`acc`=ok/live, `bad`=error, `warn`=reverted/exhausted, `purple`=dream-origin). `statusTag()` already returns these kinds.
- `components/Modal.tsx` -- plan-preview and apply-confirm dialogs (existing usage retained).
- `components/Spark.tsx` -- per-process recent-run mini bars in `ProcessCard` (reuse the existing sparkline rather than re-rolling).
- `components/Empty.tsx` (`.notice`) -- loading/empty/error states.
- `lib/stream-utils.ts` `formatTime` -- all timestamps (already imported by Dream today).
- `screens/screen-utils.ts` -- `shortId`, `displayValue`, `displayTargetSummary`, `fieldLabel`, `isRecord`, `jsonText`, `dateLabel`.
- `components/SupersededByChip.tsx` -- the canonical jump-to-entity chip; reused in `AuditRowDetail` to render audit target ids as inspector pivots (keeps cross-screen nav consistent with Commit/Directives/Review).
- `components/WhyDrawer.tsx` -- read-only `getWhy(id)` provenance drawer on correctable audit targets.
- `hooks/use-api.ts`, `hooks/live-context.tsx` -- standard data + live wiring.

### Data presentation (shape justified per the proposal's rules)

**Zone 1 -- Maintenance-Health Header (card row).** Cards, not a table, because these are 8 independent scalar vitals scanned at a glance (proposal: cards for scalar/narrative records, tables reserved for homogeneous rows). Each card: eyebrow label + big tabular-num value + sub-line, using `.dream-card`/`.stat`-style classes:
1. **Scheduler** -- `scheduler.enabled` -> `.dot.alive` (green pulse) "enabled" / `.dot.mute` "disabled".
2. **Light interval** -- `scheduler.light_interval_ms` formatted (`Xs`/`Xm`), sub-line lists `light_processes.length` processes.
3. **Heavy interval** -- `scheduler.heavy_interval_ms`, sub-line `heavy_processes.length`.
4. **Pending extraction** -- `pending_extraction_episodes ?? 0` (amber tint if >0).
5. **Open belief revision** -- `belief_revision_rows.length` (amber if >0); whole card is a pivot to Review (`onOpenReview`).
6. **Last tick** -- from the latest `maintenance:tick` frame (state held in `lastMaintenanceTick`): cadence + `formatTime(ts)`; tone via `maintenanceTickTone`.
7. **Errors** -- count of audit/tick errors in the most recent run / last tick `errors`; `.bad` when >0.
8. **Recent changes** -- last tick `changes` (or sum across newest run-id group); green `.dot.alive` when a run is live (any `runStatus` value in `planning|planned|running`).

**Zone 2 -- 12-Process Grid (matrix of cards).** A responsive `.dream-grid` of 12 `ProcessCard`s in fixed `PROCESS_NAMES` order. Matrix-of-cards (not a table) because each process is a small dashboard with live state + a mini sparkline -- the proposal explicitly calls process tiles a control-room surface. Each `ProcessCard` (driven by `DreamProcessSummary` + `runStatus.get(name)`):
- **Header:** process name + `enabled` indicator (`.dot.alive` green if `enabled`, else mute) + live-running pill: `runStatus` -> `planning`/`planned`/`running` shows amber `.dream-card.running` pulse (`dreamCardPulse`), `done` green, `fail` red.
- **Description:** `description` from backend, falling back to `PROCESS_DESCRIPTIONS[name]`.
- **Budget:** `budget` (number|null). If null, "default"; shows the effective `scheduler.process_budgets[name]` when present. An inline edit affordance is a backend gap (see requiresBackendGap) -- read-only with a tooltip for now.
- **Last run:** `formatTime(last_run_at)` or "never".
- **Last status:** `statusTag(last_status)` -> `Tag` (`ok`/`error`/idle).
- **Last audit id:** `#{last_audit_id}` as a chip; click pivots the Audit Ledger to that run/row and opens the inspector.
- **Latest errors:** if the newest `dream_report` for a run touching this process has `errors`, show count `.bad`; expand shows messages.
- **Recent runs sparkline:** `Spark` over the last N audit applied-at buckets for this process (audit-sourced vs synthesized coloring as today).
- Selecting a card sets `selectedProcess` and seeds the workbench's process selector to include it.

**Zone 3 -- Plan/Apply Workbench (form + two result layouts).**
- **Controls:** a process-subset multi-select (checkbox chips over `PROCESS_NAMES`, default = all; "all"/"none"/"light set"/"heavy set" quick-pick chips derived from `scheduler.light_processes`/`heavy_processes`), and a **budget** numeric input (tokens). These populate `DreamPlanRequest { processes?, budget? }`. This is the concrete new capability: today the screen sends `{}`.
- **Run plan** (`postDreamPlan({processes, budget})`): dry-run; opens a `Modal` listing `DreamPlanResponse.processes` (`DreamPlanProcess[]`).
- **Plan result layout** (per the proposal): for each process row -> `name`, `would_change` (badge), `summary`, `budget_used`, `changes.length`, `errors.length`, `budget_exhausted` (amber `Tag`). Each `changes[]` entry is expandable showing `action` + `displayTargetSummary(targets)` + `preview` (raw via `jsonText`). Footer: `total_budget_used`, `changes`, `plan_id`.
- **Apply plan** (`postDreamApply({plan_id})` when a plan exists, else `{processes,budget}`): confirm via `Modal`, close immediately so the **process grid lights up live** through `dream:process:*` frames (keep this behavior verbatim -- it is good UX for the 30-120s cycle).
- **Apply result layout** (per the proposal): `run_id`, `applied[]` (name + `audit_id`/`audit_ids` chips that pivot into the Audit Ledger + inspector + `changes`), `failed[]` (name + message, `.bad`), `duration_ms` (formatted), `total_budget_used`.
- **Budget metering note:** live in-run budget metering is a known backend gap (currently stubbed). The workbench shows planned `budget_used` and applied `total_budget_used` from responses, not a live meter.

**Zone 4 -- Schedule Lane (timeline).** A horizontal timeline (proposal: timelines for time-based data; dream schedule is explicitly listed) over `state.schedule` (`DreamScheduleItem[]`), sorted by `scheduled_at`, with a "now" marker. Each item: `process` label, `formatTime(scheduled_at)`, a **source** tag colored by `source` (`scheduler`=mute, `audit`=acc, `stream`=info), and a pivot chip -- `audit_id` -> Audit Ledger row, `stream_entry_id` -> Universal Inspector (stream entry). This replaces the current recent-runs sparkline strip with the structurally correct timeline the data supports.

**Zone 5 -- Audit Ledger (timeline grouped by run id) + Revert.** Rows grouped by `run_id` via the existing `auditRunGroups`; render with `AuditRunHeader` (run id `shortId`, `latestAppliedAt` via `formatTime`, `processes[]` chips, `revertedCount`, inline `DreamReport` summary from `reportsByRunId`: changes/tokens_used/errors/dry_run/budget_exhausted_processes/notes). Per row (`MaintenanceAuditRow`): `process`, `action`, `displayTargetSummary(targets)`, `applied_at`, `reverted_at`, `reverted_by`, status via `auditStatusLabel`/`auditStatusTone` (`ok`/`auto-resolved`/`reverted`). Click a row -> `AuditRowDetail`: applied `targets`, `reversal` payload, raw JSON (`jsonText`), and the revert action gated by `auditCanRevert` (has reversal AND not yet reverted).

**Revert as diff-confirm, not blind modal** (`RevertConfirm.tsx`): opens with a structured before/after view built from `oldNewPairs(reversal)` (the existing `old_*`/`new_*` pairing) plus a `previous_fields` restore preview when present; remaining reversal keys render as a key/value `.props` block; raw JSON behind a `<details>`. The confirm button performs `revertDreamAudit(id)` then `Promise.all([refetch(), getDreamAudit()])`. This upgrades today's per-row revert modal into a dedicated diff surface satisfying "diff-like confirmation, not a blind modal".

### Key interactions

- Select a process card -> highlights it, seeds the workbench selection, and filters the schedule lane + audit ledger to that process (a local view filter, not a recall gate).
- Plan -> preview modal -> apply -> live tiles light up -> apply-result panel renders with audit-id pivots.
- Audit row -> inspector body -> revert -> diff-confirm -> revert applied, ledger refetched, affected row flips to `reverted`.
- Belief-revision: header card + any `belief_revision_rows` surface a "resolve in Review" pivot via `onOpenReview()`. Dismiss-only `patchReviewItem(id,{action:'dismiss'})` is offered inline for belief-revision rows (the only action that endpoint accepts -- do not offer accept/reject here).

### Cross-link pivots (into/out of the Universal Inspector)

- **Out:** audit row target ids -> inspector (episode/semantic node/edge/commitment/directive, by id prefix) via `SupersededByChip`; `audit_id` chips (process card, apply result, schedule lane) -> Audit Ledger row + inspector; `stream_entry_id` (schedule lane) and dream-report run -> inspector stream entry / Stream screen; process -> its latest `dream_report` stream entry.
- **In:** the Universal Inspector's "open in source screen" for a `dream audit row` and the Cognitive Orrery's outer dream-satellite click both deep-link here (set route `dream`, preselect run/process). Cross-screen nav uses the existing callback pattern (`setRoute` in `App.tsx`), not a router.
- `getWhy(id)` is offered read-only on audit targets where the id is correctable (`ep_`/`semn_`/`seme_`), reusing `WhyDrawer`; this is provenance inspection, not gating.

### States: loading / empty / error / live

- **Loading:** `api.loading && data===null` -> `<div className="notice">loading dream cycle</div>` (existing).
- **Error:** `api.error` -> `.notice.bad` with message (existing).
- **Empty:** no audit rows -> `Empty` "no maintenance has run yet"; no schedule items -> lane shows "nothing scheduled"; a process never run -> card shows "never" + idle dot.
- **Live:** running processes pulse (`dreamCardPulse`/amber), apply button shows `planning N/M` / `applying N/M` counters from `runStatus`, last-tick card animates on `maintenance:tick`, header "recent changes" dot goes green `.alive` while any run is in-flight. Reconnect refetches; `borg:reset` reloads.

### Cardinal-constraint compliance

- **No recall gating:** this screen never hides a memory/audit by audience or session. Audit `targets` and provenance render as labels; the only filters are operator conveniences (process subset, run id) over already-fetched global data.
- **No production policing:** all mutations are sanctioned host-boundary maintenance endpoints (plan/apply/revert/dismiss). Nothing here reads the entity's emitted text and decides whether it reaches a user.
- **Language-agnostic:** all branching keys on structure -- `DreamProcessName` slugs, `source` enum, `action` strings, id prefixes, `last_status` enum, `would_change`/`budget_exhausted` booleans. No matching on user-content words.
**New files:** demo/web/src/screens/Dream/index.tsx (refactored orchestrator), demo/web/src/screens/Dream/dream-utils.ts, demo/web/src/screens/Dream/MaintenanceHealthHeader.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Dream/ProcessGrid.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Dream/ProcessCard.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Dream/PlanApplyWorkbench.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Dream/ScheduleLane.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Dream/AuditLedger.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Dream/AuditRunHeader.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Dream/AuditRowDetail.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Dream/RevertConfirm.tsx
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/screens/Dream/index.tsx (existing 1204-line DreamScreen: live reducer, DreamRunStatus two-sweep machine, auditRunGroups, oldNewPairs/pairedKeys, audit*/maintenanceTick* helpers, statusTag, PROCESS_NAMES), demo/web/src/components/Panel.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Modal.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Spark.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Empty.tsx, demo/web/src/components/SupersededByChip.tsx, demo/web/src/components/WhyDrawer.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/screen-utils.ts, demo/web/src/lib/stream-utils.ts (formatTime), demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx, demo/web/src/styles.css (.dream-*, .dream-card, .dream-grid, dreamCardPulse, .btn, .dot.alive, .panel, .tag, .spark, .props)
**API bindings:** GET /api/dream/state, POST /api/dream/plan, POST /api/dream/apply, GET /api/dream/audit, POST /api/dream/audit/:id/revert, PATCH /api/dream/review/:id, GET /api/correction/:id/why
**Live frames:** dream:process:started, dream:process:completed, maintenance:tick, stream:append, borg:reset
**Cross-link pivots:** audit row target id -> Universal Inspector (episode/semantic node/edge/commitment/directive by id prefix), audit_id chip (process card / apply result / schedule lane) -> Audit Ledger row + inspector, schedule-lane stream_entry_id -> Universal Inspector (stream entry) / Stream screen, process -> its latest dream_report stream entry, belief-revision rows + header card -> Review & Repair (existing onOpenReview), audit target why? -> WhyDrawer (read-only provenance) where id is ep_/semn_/seme_, Universal Inspector 'open in source screen' for dream audit row -> this screen (preselect run/process), Cognitive Orrery dream satellite -> this screen
**Backend gaps needed:** Editable per-process budget: scheduler.process_budgets is readable in GET /api/dream/state but there is no write endpoint to set a process budget (no PUT/PATCH for scheduler config). Card budget edit is read-only until such an endpoint exists., Toggle a process enabled/disabled: DreamProcessSummary.enabled is read-only; no endpoint to flip it., Edit scheduler intervals (light_interval_ms/heavy_interval_ms): read-only in state, no write endpoint., Live in-run budget metering: only planned (DreamPlanProcess.budget_used) and post-run (total_budget_used) figures exist; no streaming budget-consumed frame, so a live budget meter is not bindable (consistent with the existing 'budget metering ships in v2' stub)., reverted_by attribution: MaintenanceAuditRow.reverted_by is surfaced but there is no operator-identity input on revert; it is server-set.
**Scope verdict:** Opus 5.0: in-scope -- operator-console presentation of maintenance ops (process health, plan/apply/audit/revert) over sanctioned endpoints; it surfaces labels and never gates recall or polices the entity's output.
---

## Prompt Lab: three-pane prompt-block editor (evolves the Prompts screen)
## Prompt Lab -- prompt-block editor

### Purpose
Operator/developer surface for inspecting and overriding the **5 static framing blocks** that compose borg's cacheable system-prompt prefix. It answers the proposal's four questions: which blocks are default vs overridden, what the current assembled prompt is, what saving a given block *freezes*, and what changed. This is harness configuration of the entity's *own* framing voice -- it does not touch memory, recall, disclosure, or the entity's output, so none of the CARDINAL memory/output-policing rules constrain it. The one architectural subtlety it must present faithfully is the runtime-composed `host_capabilities` block, where **saving freezes live connector-injected text into a static override** (a real backend state transition, surfaced as a warning + diff, not new machinery).

The data is **5 fixed records keyed by `PromptKey`** plus one assembled-text document. That shape (a small fixed enumerated list + one detail + one document preview) maps to the canonical **three-pane master/detail** used by `Memory/MemoryDrill` (`.band-detail` = `280px 1fr 320px`), not a table (too few rows, rich detail) and not a graph (no relationships between blocks). The proposal explicitly asks for left-list / center-diff-editor / right-assembled-preview, which this layout delivers directly.

### Migration note (what the current screen becomes)
Current `screens/Prompts/index.tsx` (341 lines) is a vertical scroll: a collapsible `AssembledPromptPreview` on top, then a flat `.prompts-list` of `PromptCard`s each owning its own textarea + save/reset + freeze modal. The redesign **re-layouts, does not rewrite the logic**:
- `PromptsScreen` becomes a three-pane `.band-detail` grid with selection state (`selectedKey: PromptKey`), defaulting to the first block. The `prompts-intro` copy moves into a `.page-head` eyebrow.
- `PromptCard` is **split**: its per-block state machine (`draft`, `busy`, `error`, `confirmDraft`, `save`/`requestSave`/`confirmSave`/`reset`, the `useEffect` re-sync on `current_text`/`updated_at`/`overridden`) moves into the new center `PromptEditor` component, which renders whichever `selectedKey` is active. The list row is now just a selectable summary (`isRuntimeComposedHostBlock`, kind/override/updated tags).
- `AssembledPromptPreview` is **promoted** from a collapsible top banner to the always-visible right pane `AssembledPromptPane`, gaining section-outline navigation, in-pane search, copy-section, and a rough token estimate.
- `diffPromptLines` (LCS) + `PromptDiff` + `promptLines` + `PromptDiffLine` are **kept verbatim** and reused both in the freeze-confirm modal (as today) and as the basis of the new center three-way diff view.
- **Nothing is deleted from the API surface.** Same four bindings (`getPrompts`, `getAssembledPrompt`, `putPrompt`, `deletePrompt`).

### Component breakdown

NEW files (under `demo/web/src/screens/Prompts/`):
- `demo/web/src/screens/Prompts/PromptBlockList.tsx` -- left pane. Renders 5 `.list-row` (reusing the Memory list-row vocabulary: `.ttl` = `block.label`, `.meta` = key + kind + updated). Each row shows the structural `Tag`: `overridden` -> `<Tag kind="warn">overridden</Tag>`, else `runtime_composed` -> `<Tag kind="info">runtime composed</Tag>`, else `<Tag>default</Tag>` (same mapping as current `PromptCard` head). Selection = `.list-row.selected` (left phosphor bar). Props: `{ blocks, selectedKey, onSelect, onInspect }`.
- `demo/web/src/screens/Prompts/PromptEditor.tsx` -- center pane. Owns the per-block edit state machine lifted from `PromptCard`. Renders a **three-column diff/edit view**: `default_text` (read-only `<pre>`), `current_text` (read-only `<pre>`, labeled by `current_text_kind`), and an editable `<textarea>` (the draft). Below: a toggle `show diff` that renders `PromptDiff` for the active comparison (default-vs-edited by default; a small segmented control switches the diff base between `default` and `current`). Actions row: `save` (primary), `reset to default` (ghost, only when `overridden`), `copy default` / `copy current` / `copy edited` (ghost `.btn.sm`, via `navigator.clipboard` with the non-secure-context fallback already used by `makeClientMessageId` -- a `<textarea>`+`execCommand` shim if `clipboard` is unavailable on the LAN/plain-HTTP deploy). Hosts the existing freeze-confirm `Modal` for runtime-composed blocks unchanged. Props: `{ block, refetch }`.
- `demo/web/src/screens/Prompts/AssembledPromptPane.tsx` -- right pane. `getAssembledPrompt` -> renders (a) a **section outline**: `data.sections` as clickable `Tag` chips; clicking scrolls the `<pre>` to that section heading and flashes it (the assembled text is section-delimited; match the section label as a literal substring -- structural, not linguistic); (b) the **full text** in the existing `.prompt-preview-text` `<pre>`; (c) an in-pane **search** input (substring match over the assembled text, `prev`/`next`, "N matches" count, highlight via wrapping matched ranges -- pure substring, language-agnostic); (d) **copy section** (copies the substring for the selected outline section) and copy-all; (e) a **rough token estimate** chip: `Math.ceil(text.length / 4)` tokens, rendered with a `Tag` and the literal label "rough est." plus a tooltip noting it is a client-side char heuristic, not the model tokenizer. Props: `{ data, loading, error, focusSection? }`.

REUSED existing components/files (real paths):
- `demo/web/src/screens/Prompts/index.tsx` -- refactored into the three-pane shell + selection state; keeps `diffPromptLines`, `PromptDiff`, `promptLines`, `PromptDiffLine`, `isRuntimeComposedHostBlock`, `refetchAll`.
- `demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx` -- kind/override badges, section chips, token-estimate chip.
- `demo/web/src/components/Modal.tsx` -- freeze-confirm dialog (unchanged).
- `demo/web/src/components/Panel.tsx` -- wrap each pane (`.panel` + `.panel-header` eyebrow: "blocks" / "editor" / "assembled prompt").
- `demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts` -- `useApi(getPrompts, [])` and `useApi(getAssembledPrompt, [])` (same as today).
- `demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx` -- `useLiveEventsContext().subscribe` for the `borg:reset` refetch.
- `demo/web/src/screens/screen-utils.ts` -- `dateLabel` (updated-at), and reuse `jsonText` for the inspector Raw JSON tab.
- CSS: all existing `.prompt-*` classes in `styles.css` (270-443) are reused; `.prompt-preview-text` (the `<pre>`), `.prompt-freeze-warning`, `.prompt-freeze-diff*`, `.prompt-card-text` (textarea), and the `.band-detail`/`.list`/`.list-row`/`.detail`/`.props`/`.page-head`/`.page-body` layout vocabulary. New classes needed: `.prompt-lab` (the 3-col grid alias of `.band-detail`), `.prompt-editor-cols` (3-col default/current/edited), `.prompt-search`, `.prompt-search-hit` (highlight; use `--acc-tint`), `.prompt-token-est`. Fold the stray `rgba(224,160,80,0.4)` in `.prompt-card.overridden` into `var(--warn-dim)` while touching the file (noted design-system gap).

### Data presentation (justified)
- **Left = list (not table):** 5 rows, each needs a multi-line summary (label + kind + override + timestamp + a status dot). The `.list-row` pattern (used for all 8 memory bands) is the established idiom; a table is overkill for 5 rows.
- **Center = three-column diff editor:** the proposal's explicit "default / current / edited" ask. Side-by-side read-only `<pre>`s for `default_text` and `current_text` plus the editable draft, with the on-demand LCS line-diff (`PromptDiff`) when the operator wants the change made explicit. For `runtime_composed` blocks the "current" column is labeled "live connector-composed" so the operator sees *what would be frozen*.
- **Right = document + outline (not a tree/graph):** the assembled prompt is one flat sectioned string; an outline of `sections` chips + a searchable `<pre>` is the correct shape. Token estimate is a single derived scalar -> a chip.

### Key interactions
- Select a block in the left list -> center editor rebinds to that `PromptKey`, draft resets to its `current_text` (existing `useEffect` dependency set).
- Edit the textarea -> `dirty` computed (`draft !== current_text`); `save` enabled when `dirty && draft.trim().length > 0`.
- Save a `static_default`/`stored_override` block -> `putPrompt` directly (existing path). Save a `runtime_composed` block -> open freeze-confirm `Modal` showing `prompt-freeze-warning` + `PromptDiff(default_text, draft)` (existing path, verbatim).
- Reset-to-default -> `deletePrompt` (only when `overridden`), confirm via the same disabled-button + busy pattern.
- Copy default/current/edited and copy-section -> clipboard with execCommand fallback.
- Right-pane outline chip click -> scroll/flash the matching section in the `<pre>`. Search -> substring find with match count + prev/next.
- Cross-screen inbound: a `focusBlockKey?: PromptKey` prop on `PromptsScreen` (threaded from `App.tsx`, same way Memory/Dream receive `onOpenReview`) lets Review/Dream "the fix is a better prompt" handoffs deep-link to a specific block. Since routing is the custom `RouteId` switch (no params), add an optional `App`-level `promptFocusKey` state set alongside `setRoute("prompts")`; keep it minimal (mirrors the existing callback-prop nav pattern, no router).

### Universal Inspector cross-links
- **Out:** each list row has an "inspect" affordance opening the Universal Inspector on object id `prompt_block:<key>`. Inspector tabs for a prompt block: **Summary** (label/description/kind/overridden/updated), **Raw JSON** (`jsonText(block)`), **Actions** (save current draft / reset-to-default -- the only supported mutations; no why/forget/correct -- prompt blocks are not memory objects). Relationships/Evidence/Timeline tabs render empty-state ("prompt blocks have no provenance graph; revision history is a backend gap").
- **In:** the inspector's generic "open in source screen" for a `prompt_block:<key>` deep-links here with `focusBlockKey` set. Review/Dream "better prompt" handoffs pivot in the same way.

### States
- **Loading:** `api.loading && api.data === null` -> `<div className="notice">loading prompts</div>` (current behavior). Per-pane: assembled pane shows `loading assembled prompt` notice independently (it has its own `useApi`).
- **Empty:** `api.data === null` -> `no prompts available` (current). The 5 blocks always exist server-side, so the realistic empty is only transient.
- **Error:** `api.error` -> `<div className="notice bad">{message}</div>` (current). Per-action errors render inline in the editor actions row via `role="alert"` (current `.warn` span). Assembled-pane error is isolated to that pane.
- **Live:** subscribe to `borg:reset` -> `refetchAll()` (block overrides + assembled text re-baseline after a wipe). There is **no** prompt-specific WS frame; do not synthesize one. A `borg:reset` already triggers `window.location.reload()` at the App level today, so this pane-local refetch is belt-and-suspenders for the case where reset handling becomes non-reloading.
- **Dirty/unsaved:** switching the selected block while the editor is dirty shows a small inline "unsaved edits" `Tag kind="warn"` next to the block in the list and keeps the draft per-key (so switching away and back preserves it) -- low-cost UX, no backend.

### Testing contract (preserve for the existing suite)
Keep accessible queries stable: `getByRole("button", { name: /save/ })`, `/reset to default/`, the freeze `getByRole("dialog")` with title "freeze host capabilities override?", and the `aria-label="static default versus saved override diff"` on `PromptDiff`. Add `data-testid="prompt-block-row"` per list row and `data-testid="assembled-token-estimate"` on the estimate chip. New tests colocated as `Prompts/PromptEditor.test.tsx`, `Prompts/AssembledPromptPane.test.tsx`, fetch-stubbed via the existing `requestPath` routing convention.
**New files:** demo/web/src/screens/Prompts/PromptBlockList.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Prompts/PromptEditor.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Prompts/AssembledPromptPane.tsx
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/screens/Prompts/index.tsx (refactored to 3-pane shell; keeps diffPromptLines/PromptDiff/promptLines/PromptDiffLine/isRuntimeComposedHostBlock/refetchAll), demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Modal.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Panel.tsx, demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/screen-utils.ts (dateLabel, jsonText), styles.css .prompt-* / .band-detail / .list / .list-row / .detail / .props / .page-head / .page-body vocabulary
**API bindings:** GET /api/prompts, GET /api/prompts/assembled, PUT /api/prompts/:key, DELETE /api/prompts/:key
**Live frames:** borg:reset
**Cross-link pivots:** out: list-row inspect -> Universal Inspector on prompt_block:<key> (Summary/Raw JSON/Actions tabs), in: Universal Inspector 'open in source screen' for prompt_block:<key> deep-links to Prompt Lab with focusBlockKey, in: Review 'fix is a better prompt' handoff deep-links to a specific block by PromptKey, in: Dream 'better prompt copy' handoff deep-links to a specific block by PromptKey
**Backend gaps needed:** Prompt revision history: no endpoint exists for per-block override audit trail / list-prior-versions / restore-a-prior-override. The only history surfaced today is the single default-vs-current diff. A 'Prompt Lab becomes auditable' feature (proposal line 1996) needs e.g. GET /api/prompts/:key/revisions + POST restore. Flagged, not faked., Exact token count: the right-pane estimate is a rough client-side chars/4 heuristic. A precise per-block / assembled token count would require a backend tokenizer endpoint (none exists). Estimate is clearly labeled 'rough est.', Inspector Relationships/Evidence/Timeline for prompt blocks: prompt blocks have no provenance graph or stream-entry lineage in the API; those inspector tabs render empty-state until/unless revision-history backend lands.
**Scope verdict:** Opus 5.0: in-scope -- this is operator UI presenting/editing the harness's OWN static framing prompt (config), with the freeze warning being faithful presentation of a real backend state transition; it touches no memory recall and does no output policing, so it triggers neither recall-gating nor output-policing guardrails.

## Admin / Runtime — the quarantined operator/runtime console
## Purpose

Admin / Runtime is the 10th route and the **quarantine zone for destructive and runtime-level actions**. Its operator job (per proposal 6.10): keep `reset`, cache-clear, connection diagnostics, and runtime introspection *out of* the normal cognition/memory/governance workflows so they are never one mis-click away during ordinary operation. It answers three operator questions: (1) *what am I connected to?* (runtime version, API/WS base, live socket state), (2) *is the plumbing healthy?* (WS reconnect state, last maintenance heartbeat, dream-process error pip, client-captured backend errors, route table), and (3) *how do I blow it away safely?* (reset-demo behind the `RESET` confirm token, plus a non-destructive client-cache clear).

**Architectural note (CLAUDE.md compliance).** This screen is the easiest in the app to keep clean: it surfaces *no* episodic/semantic/self/social memory and runs *no* recall. There is therefore nothing here to audience-gate — the "memory is global to the being; disclosure is contextual" invariant is satisfied vacuously. It also performs **no production policing**: cache-clear and refetch act on the *client's* fetched copies, never on the entity's output or substrate; the only substrate mutation is the sanctioned host-boundary `/api/admin/reset`. The error log captures *transport/client* failures (HTTP status + endpoint), never the entity's generated text, and never inspects or judges model output.

## Routing / migration

- Add `"admin"` to the `RouteId` union in `demo/web/src/components/Rail.tsx` and a 10th `RAIL_ITEMS` entry: `{ id: "admin", label: "admin", short: "ADM", glyph: "⚙", num: 0 }` (num `0` mapped to `⌘0`, keeping the 1-9 ladder intact; alternatively `num: 10` if a two-char chord is acceptable — recommend `0`). The active-rail glowing-left-bar treatment is reused as-is.
- Add the conditional render branch in `demo/web/src/App.tsx` screen-shell chain: `{route === "admin" ? <AdminScreen ... /> : null}`. No router library — extend the existing custom `useState<RouteId>` switch (consistent with the build-tooling recommendation to *not* add react-router).
- **Migration / deletions.** The `ResetButton` currently mounted in the **Topbar** (`demo/web/src/components/Topbar.tsx`, the `.topbar-reset` danger affordance) moves into this screen's danger-zone card. Recommended: remove it from the Topbar so reset has exactly one home (the quarantine), OR keep a small topbar shortcut that simply routes to `admin` rather than firing the modal. The `StatusBar`'s currently-hardcoded `model opus-4.7` / `emb qwen3-8b · 4096d` segments (`demo/web/src/components/StatusBar.tsx:81-89`) are *runtime facts* that belong on this screen's Runtime card; leave them in the StatusBar for the global strip but treat Admin as their canonical, fuller home. Nothing else is deleted — Admin is purely additive.

## Component breakdown

**New files (all under `demo/web/src/`):**

- `screens/Admin/index.tsx` — exports `AdminScreen`. Top-level layout is the standard `.full-page` / `.page-head` + `.page-body` screen frame (same as Identity/Dream). Body is a responsive card grid (reuse the `.dream-grid`-style `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(...))` pattern, NOT a table — these are heterogeneous status records, which the proposal's data-shape rules class as **object cards**, not rows). Props: `{ route: RouteId; sessionId: string; onNavigate: (route: RouteId) => void; onClearClientCache: () => void; }` — all data-fetching via the shared `useApi` hook and `useLiveEventsContext()`, matching the prop-driven/context-injected convention the test suite relies on.
- `screens/Admin/RuntimeCard.tsx` — the "Runtime" `Panel`. Renders a `.props` key/value list (the existing detail-row idiom): `version` (from `useApi(() => getState({ session: sessionId }), [sessionId])` → `StateSnapshot.version`), `api base` (`apiBase()` — shows `same-origin` when empty), `ws base` (`wsBase()`), `live url` (`liveUrl()`), `model`/`embeddings` (the runtime facts relocated from StatusBar), and `active session` (`StateSnapshot.active_session`, click pivots to inspector). Each value uses `.tab-num` where numeric. Loading → skeleton `.props` rows; error → inline `.warn` with retry calling `refetch`.
- `screens/Admin/ConnectionCard.tsx` — the live-socket diagnostics `Panel`. Reads `const { wsState, connectionCount } = useLiveEventsContext();`. Renders a `Tag` whose `kind` maps the existing convention: `live → acc` (phosphor-green, pulsing `.dot.alive`), `reconnecting → warn` (amber), `down → bad` (red). Below it: `connections N` (`connectionCount`, `.tab-num`), `last heartbeat` (subscribes to `maintenance:tick` via `useLiveEventsContext().subscribe`, storing the last frame's `ts` rendered as relative time + `cadence`/`changes`/`errors` chips reusing StatusBar's `maintenanceTickTone` mapping), and `last reset` (subscribes to `borg:reset`, shows when the last reset frame arrived before App's reload). This is the only place the operator sees socket health without reading the topbar dot.
- `screens/Admin/RouteDiagnosticsCard.tsx` — a small `.tbl` (this IS tabular: uniform `{glyph, short, label, chord, mounted?}` rows, so a table is correct per data-shape rules). Iterates `RAIL_ITEMS`, marks the currently-mounted `route` with an `.active` row + `Tag kind="acc"`, shows the `?session=` URL state (from `useSession`-derived `sessionId`, flagged `default` when equal to `DEFAULT_DEMO_SESSION_ID`), and a "secure context" / "crypto.randomUUID available" readout (relevant to the LAN/plain-HTTP `makeClientMessageId` fallback). Each row's short-code is click-to-navigate via `onNavigate(item.id)`.
- `screens/Admin/ClientErrorLogCard.tsx` — renders the client-captured error ring buffer (newest-first list, reusing the `.list` / `.list-row` selectable-row pattern). Each row: HTTP `status` (`Tag kind="bad"`), endpoint path, `ApiError.message`, and relative timestamp. Empty state → `Empty`/`.notice` ("no client-captured errors this session"). A "clear log" ghost button empties the buffer. This is the proposal's "latest backend errors if captured client-side."
- `screens/Admin/DangerZoneCard.tsx` — a red-bordered (`oklch(--bad-dim)` border, reuse the `.prompt-card.overridden` warn-border idiom but in `--bad`) `Panel`. Contains: (a) **Reset demo** — mounts `<ResetButton />` **reused verbatim** (`demo/web/src/components/ResetButton.tsx`); it already owns the `RESET`-token `Modal`, `postAdminReset`, and `window.location.reload()`. (b) **Clear client cache** — a `.btn ghost` opening a `Modal` (non-destructive, no token required) that calls `onClearClientCache()`. Copy clarifies: "clears the *client's* fetched copies and re-fetches; does not touch the being's memory." The two actions are visually separated, reset clearly the more dangerous (token-gated, `--bad`; cache-clear `--warn`/ghost).
- `lib/client-error-log.ts` — NEW pure module: a module-scoped capped ring buffer (`MAX_CAPTURED_ERRORS = 60`, mirroring `eventTail` cap convention) with `recordClientError({status, path, message, ts})`, `getClientErrors()`, `clearClientErrors()`, and a `subscribe(listener)` fan-out (same Set-of-handlers pattern as `use-live-events`). Fed from a small tap added to `api/client.ts` `fetchJson` catch path (record `ApiError` before re-throwing) — a one-line, behavior-preserving addition that does NOT change throw semantics.
- `components/AppErrorBoundary.tsx` — NEW React class `ErrorBoundary` wrapping the `.screen-shell` in App. On `componentDidCatch` it records to `client-error-log` and renders a `.notice` fallback with a "go to admin" button. There is currently **no** error boundary in the app; this is the net-new resilience piece the proposal's "client-side error capture" implies.

**Reused existing components / files (real paths):**

- `demo/web/src/components/ResetButton.tsx` — reused verbatim (the load-bearing reuse target).
- `demo/web/src/components/Panel.tsx`, `Modal.tsx`, `Tag.tsx`, `Empty.tsx`, `StatusBar.tsx` (its `maintenanceTickTone`/`moodLabel` helpers are the model for the heartbeat tone) — card chrome, chips, dialogs.
- `demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts` (`useApi`), `demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx` (`useLiveEventsContext`), `demo/web/src/hooks/use-session.ts` (`DEFAULT_DEMO_SESSION_ID`, session-URL state).
- `demo/web/src/api/client.ts` — `getState`, `apiBase`, `wsBase`, `liveUrl`, `postAdminReset` (via ResetButton), `RESET_CONFIRM_TOKEN`, `ApiError`.
- `demo/web/src/components/Rail.tsx` — `RAIL_ITEMS`, `RouteId` (extended).
- CSS: extend `demo/web/src/styles.css` with `.admin-*` classes following the `.dream-*`/`.prompt-*` namespacing convention; **no new color tokens** — reuse `--bad`/`--bad-dim` (danger), `--warn` (cache-clear), `--acc` (live), and the existing `.props`/`.list`/`.tbl`/`.dot.alive`/`pulse` vocabulary. The `--bad` danger-zone border closes the recommendation to fold `.topbar-reset`'s amber into a token rather than a literal.

## Data presentation (per proposal §7 data-shape rules)

- **Runtime / Connection / Danger** = **object cards** (heterogeneous status records, not rows) — correct per "object cards for narrative/status records," not "raw tables."
- **Route diagnostics** = **table** (`.tbl`) — uniform repeated rows of identical shape; the proposal explicitly allows tables for this kind of uniform list.
- **Client error log** = **timeline-ish list** (`.list-row`, newest-first) — time-based event data, matching "use timelines for time-based data."
- **Connection state** = a single live `Tag` + pulsing `.dot.alive`, the existing live-signal grammar; no graph/matrix is warranted (no relationships, no scope axis here).

## Key interactions

1. **Reset demo** — `ResetButton` modal, type `RESET`, `postAdminReset()`, App's `borg:reset` handler (and ResetButton's own `window.location.reload()`) clears everything. Busy state shows the existing `.dream-running` spinner.
2. **Clear client cache** — confirm modal → `onClearClientCache()` in App: `Promise.all([refetchState(), refetchSessions(), refetchCreator()])` plus a new `turnStream.resetCaches()`-style flush of the live-turn `flowSnapshotByTurn`/`ledgerByTurn`/`eventTail` (exposed from `use-turn-stream`), then `clearClientErrors()`. No reload, no substrate mutation.
3. **Route navigation** — clicking any route row calls `onNavigate(id)` (wired from App's `setRoute`).
4. **Live updates** — Connection card re-renders on `wsState`/`connectionCount` change; heartbeat + last-reset readouts update from subscribed `maintenance:tick` / `borg:reset` frames (no polling).
5. **Error capture** — `fetchJson` catch tap + `AppErrorBoundary` push into `client-error-log`; the card subscribes and re-renders live; "clear log" empties it.

## Cross-link pivots (into / out of the Universal Inspector)

- **Out → Dream Ops:** a compact "process health" pip on the Connection card (count of dream processes currently in error, read from a lightweight `useApi(getDreamState)` *only if* cheap, else just a "open Dream Ops" link) navigates to `dream`. Admin does not duplicate Dream's plan/apply/audit surface.
- **Out → Universal Inspector:** `active_session` value opens the session in the inspector (the inspector's session pivot); route rows pivot to their screens.
- **Out → Stream & Provenance:** "last reset"/"last heartbeat" can deep-link to the stream tail to see the corresponding `dream_report`/`internal_event` entries.
- **Inbound:** Admin exposes *no* per-object surface, so the Universal Inspector does not pivot *into* Admin — it is a terminal operator utility, by design (quarantine).

## States

- **Loading:** Runtime card shows skeleton `.props` rows while `getState` is in flight; Connection card renders immediately from context (`wsState` starts `reconnecting`).
- **Empty:** error log empty → `.notice` "no client-captured errors this session"; dream-error pip hidden when zero.
- **Error:** `getState` failure → inline `.warn` + retry (`refetch`); a failed reset surfaces inside ResetButton's own `.warn role="alert"` (unchanged).
- **Live:** `wsState` drives the green/amber/red Tag + pulse; `down` (after 5 failed attempts) shows red and a "reconnecting…" note. During reset, the whole screen is effectively torn down by the reload.

## Test contract notes

Keep accessible queries stable: `role="button"` names (`reset borg`, `clear client cache`, `clear log`), `getByRole("dialog")` for both modals, and `data-testid` on the connection-state Tag (`data-testid="ws-state"`) and error rows (`data-testid="client-error-row"`). Live frames injected via `LiveEventsProvider` per the existing `makeLiveSource` test pattern; `getState`/`postAdminReset` via the `fetch`-stub-by-pathname convention. Reset flow already has coverage via `OperatorActions`/`ResetButton`; extend rather than fork.
**New files:** demo/web/src/screens/Admin/index.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Admin/RuntimeCard.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Admin/ConnectionCard.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Admin/RouteDiagnosticsCard.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Admin/ClientErrorLogCard.tsx, demo/web/src/screens/Admin/DangerZoneCard.tsx, demo/web/src/lib/client-error-log.ts, demo/web/src/components/AppErrorBoundary.tsx
**Reuses/refactors:** demo/web/src/components/ResetButton.tsx (reused verbatim, relocated from Topbar), demo/web/src/components/Panel.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Modal.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Tag.tsx, demo/web/src/components/Empty.tsx, demo/web/src/components/StatusBar.tsx (maintenanceTickTone/moodLabel as the heartbeat-tone model; runtime model/emb facts relocated here as canonical home), demo/web/src/components/Rail.tsx (RAIL_ITEMS + RouteId extended with 'admin'), demo/web/src/hooks/use-api.ts, demo/web/src/hooks/live-context.tsx (useLiveEventsContext -> wsState/connectionCount/subscribe), demo/web/src/hooks/use-session.ts (DEFAULT_DEMO_SESSION_ID, ?session= URL state), demo/web/src/api/client.ts (getState, apiBase, wsBase, liveUrl, postAdminReset, RESET_CONFIRM_TOKEN, ApiError), demo/web/src/App.tsx (screen-shell route branch + onClearClientCache wiring around existing refetchState/refetchSessions/refetchCreator), demo/web/src/styles.css (.dream-*/.prompt-*/.props/.list/.tbl/.dot.alive/pulse vocabulary + existing oklch tokens)
**API bindings:** GET /api/state, POST /api/admin/reset
**Live frames:** borg:reset, maintenance:tick
**Cross-link pivots:** out -> Dream Ops (dream-process error pip / open maintenance cockpit), out -> Universal Inspector (open active_session), out -> Stream & Provenance (last reset / last heartbeat -> stream tail entries), out -> any route via the route-diagnostics table rows, inbound: none by design (Admin is a terminal operator utility, exposes no per-object surface to pivot into)
**Backend gaps needed:** None. 'Client-captured backend errors' is implemented purely client-side (ApiError tap in fetchJson + ErrorBoundary + an in-memory ring buffer); it needs no new endpoint. If a future requirement wanted server-side runtime health beyond GET /api/state.version + GET /api/dream/state (e.g. process uptime, queue depth, memory/db size, build SHA), that would be a NEW read-only GET /api/admin/health endpoint -- explicitly out of scope for this design and flagged only as a potential follow-up.
**Scope verdict:** In-scope: this is pure operator-console chrome (runtime introspection + quarantined destructive actions); it surfaces no entity recall and polices no model output, so it implies neither recall-gating nor output-policing -- presentation only.
